### [1] [Déjà-Events: Multi-view 3D Geometry with Event Cameras via Sparsity-Aware Adaptive Gating](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21302)
*Arda Cinar Demirtas,Pablo Varela Bernal,Marc Pollefeys,Sunghwan Hong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出仅事件相机输入的前馈模型 Déjà-Events，可从多视角事件中一次性预测深度、相机位姿和点图；通过从冻结的 RGB 教师模型 Déjà View 蒸馏最终 token，并引入稀疏感知自适应门控，在少 3D 标签、仅仿真数据训练下取得优于事件基线的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 事件相机在高速或弱光场景中优于传统相机，但带稠密 3D 真值的事件数据集规模和多样性远小于 RGB 数据，限制了前馈式 3D 重建模型的发展。

Method: Déjà-Events 是事件-only 单次前向模型，输入多视角事件并输出深度、相机位姿和点图。方法在成对 RGB-事件记录上，将冻结的 RGB 教师 Déjà View 的最终 token 作为监督，让学生复现教师表示，无需深度或位姿真值，仅训练约 1% 参数。针对事件在边缘和运动纹理处稠密、无纹理区域稀疏的特点，提出稀疏感知自适应门控，使共享 Transformer 块的门控适配事件并按 patch 的事件密度调整，从而在增加 refinement 步数时保持精度。

Result: 仅在仿真数据上训练，Déjà-Events 在 EventScape 上取得所评估事件方法中最低的点图误差，并在真实 MVSEC 和 DSEC 上零样本取得最低点图误差。DSEC 上将最强基线 AbsRel 从 0.30 降至 0.15，误差减半，参数量约为其十分之一；在 EventScape 和 DSEC 上深度误差最低，在 EventScape 上轨迹误差最低。

Conclusion: 结果表明，通过 RGB 教师蒸馏和面向事件稀疏性的自适应门控，可在不依赖稠密 3D 标签的情况下构建高效的事件-only 前馈 3D 感知模型，并具备较强零样本泛化能力。

Abstract: Event cameras keep capturing structure where conventional cameras blur or saturate, which makes them attractive for 3D perception in fast or poorly lit scenes. Event datasets with dense 3D ground truth, however, are far smaller and less diverse than the image collections behind feed-forward 3D reconstruction models. We present Déjà-Events, an event-only model that predicts depth, camera pose and pointmaps from multiple views in a single forward pass. Rather than relying on scarce 3D labels, we distil it from Déjà View, a feed-forward RGB model that refines its tokens by repeatedly applying one shared transformer block. On paired RGB and event recordings, the event student learns to reproduce the frozen teacher's final tokens, which requires no depth or pose ground truth and trains only about 1% of its parameters. Because events are dense at edges and moving texture but nearly absent in textureless regions, we further introduce sparsity-aware adaptive gating, which adapts the shared block's gates to events and adjusts them per patch by event density, so that accuracy holds as refinement steps are added. Trained only on simulated data, Déjà-Events achieves the lowest pointmap error among the event-based methods we evaluate, on EventScape and zero-shot on the real MVSEC and DSEC datasets. On DSEC it halves the error of the strongest baseline (0.15 vs. 0.30 AbsRel) with about a tenth of its parameters. It also has the lowest depth error on EventScape and DSEC and the lowest trajectory error on EventScape.

</details>


### [2] [MVSTF: Multi-View Spatio-Temporal Fusion for LiDAR-Based Moving Object Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20872)
*Jiaoju Zhou,Chao Ye,Xuebo Yang,Weiyang Lin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出端到端多视角时空融合网络MVSTF，利用序列点云生成多视角图像与特征差异，并行提取时空及维度特征，实现高精度运动目标分割。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态环境中移动机器人识别物体运动属性困难；现有方法受边界模糊、遮挡、视角约束和训练复杂度限制。

Method: 将序列点云生成多视角图像及特征差异；设计多分支端到端网络，包含双下采样块DDB、空间注意力块SAB、多核卷积网络块MKCNN、双特征融合块DFFB和点融合模块PF，分别从特征差异提取运动特征、从多视角图像提取几何与语义特征；引入辅助语义头提升鲁棒性与可解释性。

Result: 在SemanticKITTI和KITTI-Road基准上验证，在SemanticKITTI MOS验证集和KITTI-Road验证集取得state-of-the-art结果，在隐藏的SemanticKITTI-MOS测试集取得有竞争力结果。

Conclusion: MVSTF通过多视角时空融合与多分支特征提取，有效提升动态环境下运动目标分割性能，并具备良好鲁棒性和可解释性。

Abstract: Autonomous mobile robots confront significant chal lenges in recognizing the motion properties of objects in dy namic environments. However, existing methods are hindered by limitations such as boundary ambiguity, occlusion, viewpoint constraints, and training complexity. In this paper, we propose an innovative end-to-end Multi-View Spatio-Temporal Fusion network, named MVSTF, which utilizes sequential point clouds to generate multi-view images and feature differences, and designs a multi-branch network to extract spatio-temporal and dimen sional features in parallel, and realizes high-precision moving object segmentation. First, sequential point clouds are utilized to generate multi-view images and feature differences. Then, an end-to-end multi-branch network including Dual Down-sampling Blocks (DDB), Spatial Attention Blocks (SAB), Multi-Kernel Convolutional Neural Network blocks (MKCNN), Dual Feature Fusion Blocks (DFFB), and the Point Fusion (PF) module, is designed to extract motion features from feature differences and geometric and semantic features from multi-view images. Finally, an auxiliary semantic head is introduced to further improve the algorithm's robustness and the model's interpretability. This work is validated on the SemanticKITTI and KITTI-Road bench marks, achieving state-of-the-art results on the SemanticKITTI MOS validation set and the KITTI-Road validation set, and competitive results on the hidden SemanticKITTI-MOS test set. The code is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [3] [Correspondence-Free Camera Pose Estimation by Matching Moments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20797)
*Subhransu S. Bhattacharjee,Dylan Campbell,Rahul Shome*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出无学习、无对应关系的盲 PnP 方法 MomentPnP：利用十四阶置换不变图像矩初始化相机位姿，并用 EM 联合优化位姿与对应权重。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 盲 PnP 需在 2D-3D 点集对应关系未知时估计标定相机位姿，现有学习基线在旋转精度和抗外点方面仍有不足。

Method: 证明在完整无噪观测下，至多四阶的十四个置换不变图像矩可对至少 20 个点的几乎任意 3D 点集唯一确定固定相机位姿；MomentPnP 通过匹配观测 2D 点与投影 3D 点的这些矩，在无对应关系下初始化位姿，再用期望最大化迭代精化位姿和对应权重。

Result: 在三个基准测试集上，无论有无外点，中位旋转误差较最佳学习基线降低 5–170 倍；即使外点数量与内点相当也能恢复准确位姿；在稠密 ETH3D 扫描上，点集增大时位姿精度提升，尽管单个对应更难分辨。

Conclusion: 基于高阶置换不变矩的无对应初始化结合 EM 精化，为盲 PnP 提供了高精度且抗外点的有效方案。

Abstract: The blind perspective-$n$-point (PnP) problem is to estimate the pose of a calibrated camera from 2D and 3D point sets with unknown correspondences. We show that, for complete, noiseless observations, fourteen permutation-invariant image moments up to order four identify a fixed camera pose for almost every 3D point set containing at least twenty points. Our learning-free method, MomentPnP, matches these moments between the observed 2D points and the projected 3D points to initialize a camera pose without correspondences and refines the pose and correspondence weights using expectation--maximization. Across the test sets of three benchmarks, our method reduces median rotation error by $5$--$170\times$ relative to the best learned baseline, both with and without outliers. We recover accurate poses even with as many outliers as inliers. On dense ETH3D scans, pose accuracy improves as the point sets grow, even as individual correspondences become harder to resolve.

</details>


### [4] [TRR: A Thermal Interaction Engine for Robot Simulation and RGBT Data Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20792)
*Biwen Yang,Zhe Cao,Jin Zhang,Haoyang Sun,Zonglu Hu,Jintao Zhao,Zhehao Zhang,Kaizheng Chen,Chengyi Liu,Chengyu Wei,Siyuan Wu,Ruiheng Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出热机器人渲染器TRR，将空间与深度分辨的热状态绑定到接触双方，模拟接触、输运与分离中的局部热交换，并同步生成RGBT观测与任务检查，用于构建包含热历史因果关系的机器人学习基准与工作流。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人动作会改变后续交互的热条件；释放后保留的热、随物体输运的热或表面下储存的热，即使当前温度相近，也会影响后续接触结果。训练轨迹需捕捉早期动作与后期观测及任务结果之间的热效应联系。

Method: TRR为接触双方附加空间和深度分辨的热状态，更新接触、输运和分离过程中的局部热交换，并在同一执行时钟上生成同步RGB-热（RGBT）观测与事件局部任务检查。基于同一状态构建六任务、2,224个静态-动态对（共4,448条轨迹）的配对基准，包含同步双视角观测、状态与动作；并提供RGBT观测适配器、机器人配置与相机视角资源及场景导出工作流。

Result: 可见热历史将专用学习语料上的安全接触点选择从75.0%提升至91.7%；在新加热流程与共享反馈执行下，动态热历史训练使模拟迁移完成率提升50个百分点。作者在MuJoCo和Isaac Sim上演示RGBT观测适配器，报告10种机器人配置和6个相机视角的工程清单。

Conclusion: TRR通过显式建模保留热、空间输运与内部储存对后续接触的影响，为热感知机器人学习提供同步RGBT数据、基准与开发工作流；相关基准、工作流和平台资源将作为计划公开发布的首批内容。

Abstract: A robot's actions change the thermal conditions of its next interaction: heat retained after release, carried with objects or stored beneath the surface can change a later contact's outcome despite similar current temperatures. Training trajectories must capture how these effects connect earlier actions to later observations and task outcomes. We present TRR, a \emph{Thermal Robot Renderer} that attaches spatial and depth-resolved thermal state to both contact partners, updates local heat exchange through contact, transport and separation, and generates synchronized RGB--thermal (RGBT) observations and event-local task checks on one execution clock. Controlled interventions show why retained heat, spatial transport and internal storage matter for subsequent contact outcomes. From the same state, TRR produces a paired six-task benchmark of 2,224 static--dynamic pairs, that is, 4,448 trajectories with synchronized dual-view observations, states and actions. Visible thermal history raises safe contact-site selection from 75.0\% to 91.7\% on a dedicated learning corpus, and training on dynamic histories raises simulated transfer completion by 50 percentage points under a new heating procedure with shared feedback execution. We demonstrate RGBT observation adapters on MuJoCo and Isaac Sim, report an engineering inventory of ten prepared robot configurations and six demonstrated camera viewpoints, and describe a developer workflow that carries an existing robot scene to exported RGBT records. The benchmark, the workflow and the platform resources described in Section 6 form the first items of a planned public release. Demonstrations and resource updates are available on the project page.

</details>


### [5] [Instructions Before Actions: Prospective and Retrospective Language Supervision for Driving Vision-Language-Action Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20631)
*Angel Martinez-Sanchez,Ross Greer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文研究驾驶VLA模型的指令遵循评估，区分行动前的前瞻性指令与行动后的回顾性标注，并构建ADVLAT-Engine同步语音指令、道路视频与车辆轨迹；结果显示微调可降低轨迹误差，但指令遵循效果不稳定，反事实指令下部分模型表现出更强响应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 驾驶VLA模型虽能接收导航指令，但仅凭轨迹预测准确不能证明模型真正遵循指令；现有驾驶语言监督的采集与评估方式混杂了前瞻性指令和回顾性标签，导致难以区分轨迹预测与指令遵循。

Method: 开发ADVLAT-Engine同步语音指令、道路场景视频和车辆轨迹；在48条重放路线上比较三种导航应用，并对比Google Maps与乘客在87个机动动作上的语言；在导航应用前瞻性驾驶数据上微调四个开源驾驶VLA；在真实路口使用反事实指令评估指令响应，并收集四人的回顾性标注用于后续受控比较。

Result: 乘客在双向宣布的机动动作中使用更多场景依赖语言；微调使乘客驾驶轨迹误差降低23-59%，但指令影响仍不一致；真实路口反事实指令下，微调后的Alpamayo 2 Super在15 m和5 m分别提升31和18个准确率点，Qwen-Drive对离散命令的替代机动遵循比句子更可靠；回顾性标注的下游学习分析尚待完成。

Conclusion: 研究将机动预测与指令遵循区分开来，并建立比较前瞻性与回顾性驾驶语言监督的框架，强调需用反事实指令和场景依赖语言更严格评估驾驶VLA的指令遵循能力。

Abstract: Driving vision-language-action (VLA) models can accept navigation instructions, yet predicting a driver's trajectory does not establish that a model follows the instruction. We study how driving-language supervision is collected and evaluated, distinguishing prospective instructions issued before an action from retrospective labels written after observing it. We develop ADVLAT-Engine to synchronize spoken instructions, road-scene video, and vehicle trajectories, and compare three navigation apps on 48 replayed routes. On 87 maneuvers announced by both Google Maps and a passenger, the passenger uses more scene-dependent language. We evaluate four open driving VLAs before and after fine-tuning on prospective navigation-app drives. Fine-tuning reduces passenger-drive trajectory error by 23-59%, but instruction effects remain inconsistent. At real intersections, counterfactual instructions reveal stronger responsiveness: fine-tuned Alpamayo~2 Super gains 31 and 18 accuracy points at 15 and 5~m, while Qwen-Drive follows alternative maneuvers more reliably with a discrete command than with a sentence. We additionally collected retrospective annotations from four people for the driving recordings and describe controlled comparisons of annotation content, timing, and downstream learning; those analyses are pending. Our findings separate maneuver prediction from instruction-following and establish a framework for comparing prospective and retrospective supervision.

</details>


### [6] [VoxSAOR: Semantic-Aware Occupancy Robustification via Aleatoric Uncertainty Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20507)
*Mengfei Duan,Yuheng Zhang,Guoqiang Zhao,Yuanfan Zheng,Kailun Yang,Zhiyong Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 VoxSAOR，一种语义感知的三维占据鲁棒化框架，通过特征空间代理建模偶然不确定性，提升视觉三维语义占据预测的鲁棒性、长尾识别与校准性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉三维占据预测易受遮挡、深度歧义和投影误差影响，导致体素特征可靠性在空间上不一致；现有方法通常直接将体素表示映射为语义预测，未显式建模这种观测引起的特征变化。

Method: VoxSAOR 先量化局部语义一致性（LSC）得到体素级结构稳定性先验，并利用跨视角语义共识过滤不可靠语义对应；在同一语义类别内，将空间匹配的稳定体素与模糊体素构成稳定到模糊的特征转移，用类条件对角高斯分布建模特征位移与离散度；基于该代理分布，AREL 使用逐级增强尺度的特征扰动训练独立参数化的预测头，扰动强度由结构可靠性调制；推理时关闭代理建模与采样，所有预测头处理同一观测特征并平均输出概率，形成确定性共识预测。

Result: 在多个数据集和占据预测骨干网络上，VoxSAOR 提升了语义占据预测、长尾识别和预测校准效果。

Conclusion: VoxSAOR 能有效建模观测诱导的体素特征不确定性并增强语义占据预测鲁棒性，源代码将公开。

Abstract: Vision-based 3D occupancy prediction provides a dense semantic representation of scenes for autonomous driving and embodied perception, yet remains vulnerable to occlusion, depth ambiguity, and projection errors that induce spatially varying reliability in voxel features. Existing methods typically map voxel representations to semantic predictions without explicitly modeling such observation-induced feature variations. To address this limitation, we propose VoxSAOR, a semantic-aware occupancy robustification framework that models aleatoric uncertainty through feature-space proxies. It first quantifies Local Semantic Consistency (LSC) to derive a voxel-wise structural stability prior and uses cross-view semantic consensus to filter unreliable semantic correspondences. Within each semantic category, spatially matched stable and ambiguous voxels form stable-to-ambiguous feature transitions, which are modeled by a class-conditional diagonal Gaussian distribution, capturing both feature displacement and dispersion. Guided by these proxy distributions, Aleatoric Regime Ensemble Learning (AREL) trains independently parameterized prediction heads using feature perturbations at progressively increasing scales, with perturbation strength modulated by structural reliability. At inference, proxy modeling and sampling are disabled, and all heads process the same observed features; their output probabilities are then averaged to produce a deterministic consensus prediction without requiring sampling. Experiments across multiple datasets and occupancy backbones demonstrate the effectiveness of VoxSAOR in improving semantic occupancy prediction, long-tail recognition, and predictive calibration. The source code will be made publicly available at this https URL .

</details>


### [7] [Aerial Person Detection under Edge Constraints: Held-Out Evaluation and Edge-Ground Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20127)
*Raghu Venkat,Tricha Anjali*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究评估搜救无人机在极小目标、机载算力受限和窄带链路条件下的行人检测：预注册主评估，后续分析标注为探索性；比较轻量对比度门控检测器与YOLO11n，并测试用地面模型重打分Top-16候选框。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 搜索无人机中失踪人员仅占几个像素，机载计算机弱且通信带宽窄，需要在实际部署约束和分布偏移下可靠评估行人检测，并区分预注册结论与探索性分析。

Method: 采用基于热成像帧标识推断的录制块留出评估；提出46,000参数、由对比度线索门控的检测器，并与同折训练的YOLO11n比较；在117 m的4K实地 footage 上评估Top-16命中；再将每帧16个最佳裁剪（154 KB/帧）用842,000参数地面模型重打分，阈值在验证地点冻结，并对15个测试地点做事后配对分析。

Result: 按录制块留出使固定虚警目标下召回从32.7%降至12.3%。门控检测器召回10.4%，同网络全域应用为5.7%。YOLO11n在同折下以约11倍算术量达到54.5%召回；117 m 4K画面中行走人员进入Top-16的比例最高92%，板端为74%。YOLO11n单独运行时，在一个地点设定的阈值在新地点虚警超目标1.7–3.4倍，其最佳高空配置每帧耗4–24次标准前向。重打分将同16裁剪的召回从44.9%提至53.7%，15个测试地点事后配对增益+8.8点[+2.4,+12.5]；汇总虚警从0.86降至0.64/帧，但配对变化与零不可区分。两地检测器对土色静止人员的覆盖都很低。

Conclusion: 轻量门控检测器算力友好但召回很低；YOLO11n精度更强但算力代价高且阈值跨地点迁移差；地面模型重打分可提升召回，但虚警下降不显著。受限无人机行人检测仍受小目标、分布偏移和伪装/静止人员挑战，且部分关键比较为探索性证据。

Abstract: A search drone sees a missing person as a few pixels, carries a small computer, and shares a narrow radio link. We study person detection under those limits; the primary evaluations were declared before they ran, and later analyses are marked exploratory. In an exploratory comparison, holding out recording blocks inferred from thermal frame identifiers cut recall at a fixed false-alarm target from 32.7% to 12.3%. With nothing tuned on test blocks, a 46,000-parameter detector gated by a contrast cue finds 10.4% of people against 5.7% for the same network applied everywhere. A standard detector, YOLO11n, trained under the same folds finds 54.5% at a similar false-alarm rate for about eleven times the arithmetic, and on our 4K field footage at 117 m has a walking person among its top 16 detections in up to 92% of frames against 74% for our board. Run alone, YOLO11n's thresholds set at one location overshot their false-alarm target 1.7 to 3.4 times at new locations, and its best configuration at altitude cost 4 to 24 standard passes per frame. Re-scoring its 16 best crops (154 KB per frame) with an 842,000-parameter ground model, thresholds frozen on a validation location, raised recall from 44.9% to 53.7% against the same 16 crops with YOLO's own scores; in a post hoc paired analysis over 15 test locations the gain was +8.8 points [+2.4, +12.5]. Pooled false detections fell from 0.86 to 0.64 per frame, but that paired change was not distinguishable from zero. Coverage of a still person in earth tones was very low for both detectors in the clips we flew.

</details>


### [8] [ReactBench-AD: Do Driving VLAs and World-Action Models React to Imminent Hazards in Their Path?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20011)
*Nikos Theodoridis,Reenu Mohandas,Sushil Sharma,Ciarán Hogan,Ganesh Sistu,Anthony Scanlan,Tim Brophy,Ciarán Eising*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出 ReactBench-AD 基准，用像素级反事实场景检验自动驾驶 VLA/WAM 对自车路径中突然出现物体的长尾反应能力；发现现有模型反应普遍很弱，但短时间微调可显著改善并泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动驾驶基准多为常规场景，难以验证 VLA/WAM 所宣称的长尾泛化能力；而避让自车路径中新出现物体是安全关键能力，需要可控、因果可隔离的评测方法。

Method: 构建 ReactBench-AD：基于真实 NAVSIM 与 PhysicalAI-Autonomous-Vehicles 场景，在所有相机视角和历史帧中一致地合成横穿行人、站立行人和静止车辆，使每个编辑场景与原始场景构成精确的像素级反事实对，从而隔离插入物体对预测轨迹的因果影响。评测 4 个 VLA 和 2 个 WAM，并对 ReCogDrive 进行短时间微调以学习紧急制动。

Result: 评测显示 4 个 VLA 和 2 个 WAM 对插入物体的反应较弱，常常几乎不反应；对 ReCogDrive 在编辑场景上短时间微调后，其紧急制动反应显著提升，并能泛化到未见物体和完全真实场景，同时对常规驾驶性能影响很小。

Conclusion: 这些结果质疑现有 VLA/WAM 的开箱即用长尾能力，但表明它们可作为强基础，仅需少量额外训练即可获得长尾行为；也凸显了包含长尾行为的真实或合成数据的价值。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action models (VLAs) and World-Action Models (WAMs) have emerged in autonomous driving with the promise of strong generalization to long-tail scenarios. Although these models achieve state-of-the-art results on autonomous driving benchmarks, most existing benchmarks are dominated by nominal scenarios and therefore cannot test this promise. To test one such long-tail capability that is critical for safety, we introduce ReactBench-AD, a benchmark that measures how driving models react to objects that appear in the ego vehicle's path. Built on real NAVSIM and PhysicalAI-Autonomous-Vehicles scenes, ReactBench-AD composites a crossing pedestrian, a standing pedestrian, or a stationary car consistently across all camera views and history frames. Each edited scene thus forms an exact, pixel-level counterfactual pair with its original, which allows us to isolate the causal effect of the inserted object on the predicted trajectory. Evaluating four VLAs and two WAMs, we find that they react weakly, often barely at all. Finally, we test how easily one of the evaluated VLAs, ReCogDrive, can be taught to brake hard for imminent hazards: a short fine-tuning stage on edited scenes substantially improves its reactions, with the learned behavior generalizing to unseen objects and fully real scenes, at minimal cost to nominal driving. These results call into question the out-of-the-box long-tail capabilities of these models, but suggest that they can be a strong foundation for acquiring them with little additional training, highlighting the value of real or synthetic data that demonstrates the long-tail behaviors nominal datasets rarely contain. Code and data will be made publicly available.

</details>


### [9] [The Flip Test: Video World Models Fail to Learn the World State](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19822)
*Mi Luo,Lijun Yu,Adams Wei Yu,Ang Cao,Baochen Sun,Ming-Hsuan Yang,Nan Hua,Yuan Hao,Alex Dimakis,Ya Xu,Ed H. Chi,Xuezhi Wang,Yinxiao Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文用反事实的 FlipTest 系统检验视频世界模型是否真正跟踪控制未来动态的世界状态，而非仅生成合理像素；结果显示当前 11 个视频世界模型均未通过。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频世界模型声称能从当前场景预测未来，但若当前状态发生微小且因果相关的改变，模型应相应改变预测。论文质疑现有模型是否具备这种状态跟踪与因果敏感性。

Method: 构建 FlipTest：基于 10 个仿真任务和 15 个真实任务共 1,680 个初始帧，设计三级递进测试。基础层为视觉最小对，比较真实未来不同的近同帧预测是否跨边界变化；匹配控制检验预测是否只在未来变化处变化；最严格的密集扫描定位预测变化的确切位置。评估 11 个视频世界模型，并与视觉语言模型和测量图像的编码智能体对比。

Result: 所有 11 个视频世界模型均未达标。最佳模型在真实与仿真场景上的成对准确率平均仅 18.3%，低于随机猜测的 25%。模型在未来不变的步上与未来改变的步上翻转频率相近，且密集扫描中响应平坦。相同帧上，视觉语言模型具有选择性并在边界附近呈近 S 形曲线，编码智能体通过测量图像接近真实边界。

Conclusion: 关键信息存在于帧中，而视频世界模型未能利用。论文呼吁重新思考将世界建模等同于纯视频预测，下一帧预测不仅要生成合理像素，还必须识别控制未来动态的因果状态。

Abstract: Video world models promise to predict the future of a scene from its present. Do current models keep this promise? We probe this question counterfactually. When a small change to the present flips the future, such as a box resting a few millimeters before or past a table edge, a model that tracks the state of the world must flip its prediction too. We build FlipTest, a three-level hierarchy of increasingly strict tests over 1,680 initial frames from 10 simulated and 15 real-world tasks. At the base, visual minimal pairs, two nearly identical frames whose true futures differ, test whether predictions change across the boundary; matched controls go further and test whether they change only where the future does; and dense sweeps, the strictest level, locate exactly where they change. All 11 video world models we evaluate fall short. The best averages 18.3% pair accuracy over real and simulated scenes, below the 25% of random guessing. Models flip as often on steps that leave the future unchanged as on steps that change it, and their response stays flat across the sweeps. On the same frames, a vision-language model is selective and traces a near S-shaped curve near the boundary, and a coding agent that measures the image comes close to the true boundary. This implies that the deciding information is in the frame, and video models just miss it. We therefore urge the community to rethink world modeling as pure video prediction. Next-frame prediction must do more than draw plausible pixels; it must also identify the causal states that control future dynamics.

</details>


### [10] [Toward Planning Representations That Separate Task Structure from Visual Content](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19621)
*Aarav Srivastava,Vishwas Sathish,Rajesh P. N. Rao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该文提出在ProcTHOR中受控评估ObjectNav的视觉泛化：保持楼层平面、物体位置、目标和起始状态不变，仅单独或组合改变视觉因素，比较无模型RL、基于模型RL和冻结预训练视觉表征智能体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉泛化评测常同时改变多个因素，难以定位失败来源；需要可控地分离不同视觉变化对导航规划的影响。

Method: 在程序生成的ProcTHOR房屋中构建受控ObjectNav评测，训练与评估共享布局、物体位置、目标和起点，仅改变表面材质、杂物、光照及组合变化；评估无模型RL、基于模型RL和冻结预训练视觉表征的智能体。

Result: 外观变化会损害导航且按因素差异显著：表面材质变化对平均成功率影响最大，杂物和光照影响较小；全局图像统计偏移不能可靠预测性能，仅改变目标物体外观即可显著损害端到端智能体。基于模型并在冻结DINOv2特征上规划的智能体在完整偏移下成功率最高（0.83），但仍对表面外观敏感。

Conclusion: 规划表征需将不变的空间任务结构与外观信息分离，这是视觉泛化ObjectNav的重要要求。

Abstract: Effective planning requires representations that preserve task-relevant geometry and object identity while ignoring visual variation that does not alter the task. Existing evaluations of visual generalization often change several factors simultaneously, making the source of a failure difficult to identify. We introduce a controlled evaluation of object-goal navigation (ObjectNav), the task of reaching an instance of a named object category, in procedurally generated ProcTHOR houses. Training and evaluation houses share floor plans, object locations, goals, and start states, while visual factors are changed individually or in combination. Across model-free reinforcement learning (RL), model-based RL, and agents using frozen pretrained visual representations, our results show that appearance changes impair navigation, but their effects differ sharply by factor. Surface material changes account for most of the average change in success, whereas clutter and lighting have little effect. The magnitude of a global image statistics shift does not reliably predict performance: changing only the goal object's appearance can substantially impair an end-to-end agent despite a small image-level change. We found that a model based agent that plans over frozen features from the pretrained DINOv2 image encoder achieves the highest success (0.83) under the full shift, but remains sensitive to surface appearance. Our experiments reveal an important requirement for planning representations: they must separate invariant spatial task structure from appearance.

</details>


### [11] [AcousticBEV: Acoustic BEV Object Localization for Intelligent Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19381)
*Ziang Liu,Shiming Wang,Joseph Adeola,Julian F.P. Kooij*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种车载水平麦克风阵列作为车辆“耳朵”，构建同步声学-激光雷达数据集 AcousticScenes，并提出 AcousticBEV 方法，从麦克风阵列信号估计邻近运动车辆在 BEV 中的中心位置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代自动驾驶与驾驶辅助系统主要依赖相机、雷达和激光雷达，但相机受光照限制、激光雷达受恶劣天气影响；声学传感尤其是麦克风阵列具有互补感知潜力，但在自动驾驶中仍研究不足。

Method: 设计水平排列的车载麦克风阵列，采集包含同步空间声学信号、激光雷达点云和 3D 边界框标注的真实驾驶数据集 AcousticScenes（2,117 个静态样本和 2,863 个动态样本）；提出学习型方法 AcousticBEV，以自车速度为条件，从麦克风阵列信号估计运动交通参与者在前视鸟瞰图（BEV）中的中心位置，并与经典信号处理基线和其它学习方法对比。

Result: AcousticBEV 优于经典信号处理基线和其它学习方法，在“Easy”场景取得 47.7 ± 1.2% 的 F1 分数，在“Difficult”场景取得 45.1 ± 1.4% 的 F1 分数。

Conclusion: 研究表明，低成本车载麦克风阵列的声学信号能够在方位角和距离上定位邻近运动车辆，为自动驾驶环境感知提供互补能力。

Abstract: Modern autonomous driving and driver assistance systems rely predominantly on cameras, radars and LiDARs for environmental perception. However, these modalities face different environmental limitations, including poor illumination for cameras and adverse weather for LiDAR. Acoustic sensing, particularly using microphone arrays, could provide complementary perception capabilities for autonomous driving, but has remained largely underexplored. In this work, we design a horizontally arranged microphone array that functions as the "ears" of a vehicle, enabling localization of nearby moving vehicles in a bird's-eye view (BEV) representation that captures both their relative direction and distance. Using this array, we collect a real-world driving dataset, AcousticScenes, which contains 2,117 static and 2,863 dynamic samples with synchronized spatial acoustic signals, LiDAR point clouds and 3D bounding box annotations. Building on this dataset, we propose AcousticBEV, a learning-based method for acoustic BEV object localization, which estimates the center locations of moving traffic participants in BEV from the microphone-array signals, conditioned on the ego vehicle's own speed. Experimental results demonstrate that AcousticBEV outperforms both classical signal-processing baselines and other learning-based methods, achieving F1 scores of $47.7 \pm 1.2$% on "Easy" scenarios and $45.1 \pm 1.4$% on "Difficult" cases. Our findings demonstrate that acoustic signals from a low-cost, vehicle-mounted microphone array can localize nearby moving vehicles in both azimuth and distance.

</details>


### [12] [Frame First, Decode Once: Object-Centric Coordinates and Single-Pass Decoding for System-1 World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18761)
*Abu Anas Ibn Samad*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究评估单遍基元解码器作为System-1世界模型时的关键因素：在PushT任务中，将输入输出表达在物体初始位姿的SE(2)坐标系，以及采用单遍解码，是影响决策后悔的两个主要因素；软令牌期望配合端到端2D Kabsch投影取得最低轨迹误差与精确刚体一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者关注：当LocateAnything、VFlash等单遍基元解码器被用作快速、动作条件化的System-1世界模型来对候选推动动作排序时，哪些设计因素真正决定预测与决策质量。

Method: 在ManiSkill3 PushT-v1（512个留出测试族、8192次rollout）与lerobot/pusht人类演示（2384个测试窗口）上，使用匹配检查点、分层bootstrap置信区间和Holm校正，比较不同解码器、训练损失、刚体投影与坐标框架。

Result: 两个因素影响最大且量级相近：以物体初始位姿的SE(2)框架表达输入输出，使五种解码器的决策后悔降低1.9-2.2倍，且物体框架模型用128族训练即可匹配世界框架模型用512族训练；单遍解码优于迭代解码，物体框架下单遍头达到0.020-0.021后悔、每决策1.6-1.8 ms，而10步DDIM为0.046（10.3 ms）、KV缓存自回归为0.059（12.9 ms）。单遍头之间的选择对后悔无可测影响，且“框架效应大于解码器差异”的注册假设未获支持。软令牌期望加端到端2D Kabsch投影给出最低轨迹误差（ManiSkill3 11.35 px，lerobot/pusht 3.78 px）、精确刚体一致性，并在4-50步DDIM各预算下后悔低于扩散且延迟低3-29倍。

Conclusion: 作为System-1世界模型，单遍基元解码器的关键收益主要来自物体中心SE(2)坐标框架与单遍解码本身，而非具体单遍解码头；软令牌期望与2D Kabsch投影可进一步提升几何一致性和效率。

Abstract: Single-pass primitive decoders such as LocateAnything and VFlash predict all quantized coordinate tokens of a geometric primitive in one forward pass, enabling fast, System-1 visual planning. We study what matters when such a decoder serves as a System-1 world model: a fast action-conditioned predictor that ranks candidate pushes by their predicted outcome. On ManiSkill3 PushT-v1 (512 held-out test families, 8,192 rollouts) and on human demonstrations from lerobot/pusht (2,384 test windows), we compare decoders, training losses, rigid-body projections and coordinate frames, using matched checkpoints, hierarchical bootstrap confidence intervals and Holm correction. Two factors of similar size dominate. First, expressing inputs and outputs in the SE(2) frame of the object's initial pose lowers decision regret by 1.9-2.2x for each of five decoders, and the object-frame model trained on 128 families matches the world-frame model trained on 512. Second, single-pass decoding outperforms iterative decoding: in the object frame, single-pass heads reach 0.020-0.021 regret at 1.6-1.8 ms per decision, against 0.046 for 10-step DDIM diffusion and 0.059 for KV-cached autoregression at 10.3 and 12.9 ms. The choice among single-pass heads has no measurable effect on regret, and our registered hypothesis that the frame effect exceeds the decoder spread is not supported. Soft-token expectation with an end-to-end 2D Kabsch projection gives the lowest trajectory error (11.35 px on ManiSkill3, 3.78 px on lerobot/pusht), exact rigid-body consistency, and lower regret than diffusion at every budget from 4 to 50 DDIM steps, at 3-29x lower latency.

</details>


### [13] [GOOSELoc: A Multimodal Benchmark for Ground-to-Aerial Localization in Unstructured Off-Road Terrain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16162)
*Fabian Deuser,Peter Mortimer,Robin-Nico Kampa,Anna Bößendörfer,Mirko Mählisch,Martin Werner,Norbert Oswald*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 GOOSELoc 多模态地面到航拍定位基准，覆盖森林、田野和乡村道路等非结构化环境，并提供融合环视图像与 LiDAR 几何的定位基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有跨视角定位基准（如 VIGOR、cross-view KITTI）主要覆盖城市和道路场景，缺乏越野地形；在 GNSS 不可靠场景下，需要评估现有方法在非结构化环境中的可迁移性。

Method: GOOSELoc 包含德国慕尼黑周边四季采集的 9,356 个场景，配对地理参考航拍正射影像、环视 RGB 图像和 3D LiDAR 点云，并提供铺装/非铺装标签；作者适配并微调四个先进跨视角定位基线，同时提出融合环视图像与 LiDAR 几何的多模态基线。

Result: 即使进行任务特定微调，现有先进基线在越野环境中的性能仍显著下降；所提出的多模态基线提升了定位性能。

Conclusion: GOOSELoc 揭示了非结构化地形和季节外观差异带来的挑战，为未来地面到航拍定位研究提供了更强参考，并公开数据与基线。

Abstract: Cross-view pose estimation estimates a ground camera's geographic pose by matching onboard sensor data with geo-referenced overhead imagery. This is particularly relevant when GNSS is unreliable, for example under forest canopy or in urban canyons. Existing metric cross-view benchmarks, such as VIGOR and cross-view KITTI, are dominated by urban and road scenes with strong human-made structure without off-road terrain. We introduce GOOSELoc, a multimodal benchmark for ground-to-aerial localization in unstructured environments, including forests, fields, and rural tracks. GOOSELoc comprises 9,356 scenes recorded across all four seasons around Munich, Germany, pairing geo-referenced aerial orthophotos with surround-view RGB imagery and 3D LiDAR point clouds. Paved/unpaved labels enable analyzing the impact of terrain structure, while the seasonal recordings allow studying the appearance gap to mostly summer-captured orthophotos. To assess the transferability of existing methods, we adapt and fine-tune four state-of-the-art cross-view localization baselines on GOOSELoc. Despite task-specific fine-tuning, the performance degrades substantially in off-road environments. We therefore propose a multimodal baseline combining surround-view imagery and LiDAR geometry, improving localization performance and providing a stronger reference for future work. Data and baselines are released at this https URL .

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [14] [Robustness as a Transferable Skill: Cross-Task Adversarial Defense for Vision-Language-Action Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18702)
*Jinquan Zhang,Run Yang,Dongfu Yin,Qiang Lai,Yufeng Yan,Pengshuai Yin,F. Richard Yu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究VLA策略的对抗视觉鲁棒性跨任务迁移：无需目标任务的对抗训练，即可利用源操作任务的防御更新提升独立专用目标策略的鲁棒性，并提出SN-TRV进行源更新组合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA策略易受对抗视觉扰动影响，而现有防御通常依赖任务特定的对抗微调，成本高且难以复用到独立训练的目标策略。作者希望验证源任务上学到的鲁棒性是否能跨任务保护目标策略，并解决多源防御更新如何有效聚合的问题。

Method: 作者先证明单个源防御更新即可提升留出任务上的鲁棒性，从而确立跨任务防御复用的可行性。随后提出Source-Norm Transferable Robustness Vector（SN-TRV）：组合多个源更新时保留其均值方向，并利用源更新范数校准幅度。该更新直接作用于目标视觉编码器，不需要目标对抗数据、目标侧梯度优化或额外推理模块。

Result: 在三种VLA骨干、固定与自适应攻击以及不同源防御配方下，SN-TRV较朴素平均取得更好的鲁棒性，并揭示了攻击覆盖范围的局限。真实PiPER试验中，打印对抗补丁下的平均成功率由20.3%提升至45.8%。

Conclusion: 源任务防御更新可跨任务复用，SN-TRV为无需目标对抗训练的组合式鲁棒性迁移提供了有效方案，但在攻击覆盖上仍存在边界，需进一步扩展。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) policies are vulnerable to adversarial visual perturbations, while defenses often require task-specific adversarial fine-tuning. We investigate whether robustness learned on source manipulation tasks can protect independently specialized target policies without target-side adversarial training. We first show that even a single source defense update improves robustness on held-out tasks, establishing cross-task defense reuse independently of multi-source aggregation. To combine source updates, we introduce Source-Norm Transferable Robustness Vector (SN-TRV), which retains the direction of their mean while calibrating its magnitude using source-update norms. The resulting update is applied to the target visual encoder without target adversarial data, target-side gradient optimization, or additional inference modules. Evaluations across three VLA backbones, fixed and adaptive attacks, and different source defense recipes show improved robustness over naive averaging and identify limits in attack coverage. Physical PiPER trials increase average success under printed adversarial patches from 20.3% to 45.8%.

</details>


### [15] [ReuseMem: One Memory, Multiple Goals in Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21115)
*Yoonseok Kang,Dayeon Jung,Seunghoon Jeon,Wonjib Kim,Dongchul Park*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ReuseMem 让机器人用同一份视觉记忆支持多种后续操作目标：记录与未来目标无关的视觉特征，按当前请求检索互补证据，并与当前场景结合后用于操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人与人类协作时，观察阶段往往不知道同一经验未来会用于识别物体还是将物体归位。因此需要一种不依赖未来目标、可按不同请求复用同一视觉记忆的机制。

Method: ReuseMem 独立于未来目标记录视觉特征；针对每个请求检索互补证据，并通过轻量动作接口与当前场景融合；检索过程由候选证据对专家动作预测的支持程度监督，从而把记忆选择与其在操作中的使用联系起来；重复视觉特征只存储一次，动作生成中不变的记忆计算被复用。

Result: 在四个仿真任务和两个物理场景中，ReuseMem 展示了同一记录可跨不同请求复用。在匹配动作适配下，其在基准任务上的联合目标完成率比统一记忆高 24.5 个百分点。

Conclusion: 单一视觉记忆可在场景变化中支持多种后续操作目标；将记忆选择与操作使用联合学习，并通过特征去重和计算复用，可提升多目标操作中的复用性与联合目标完成率。

Abstract: Robots that work alongside people should be able to use what they have seen when new requests arise. The same experience may later support identifying an object or returning it to its original location, although these uses were unknown during observation. ReuseMem learns to use one visual memory for multiple later manipulation goals. It records visual features independently of future goals, retrieves complementary evidence for each request, and combines it with the current scene through a lightweight action interface. Retrieval is supervised by how well candidate evidence supports expert-action prediction across different current states, linking memory selection to its use in manipulation. Repeated visual features are stored once, and memory computations that remain unchanged during action generation are reused. Experiments on four simulation tasks and two physical scenarios demonstrate reuse of a common recording across different requests. With matched action adaptation, ReuseMem improves joint goal completion on the benchmark tasks by 24.5 percentage points over uniform memory. These findings show that one memory can support multiple later manipulation goals as the scene changes.

</details>


### [16] [ForceHarness: Turning Force into Action and Physics for Frozen Frontier Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21099)
*Ke Sun,Kevin Yuchen Ma,Yike Zhang,Yanzhe Chen,Mike Zheng Shou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ForceHarness 让仅通过 API 访问的冻结多模态模型无需演示或微调即可在机器人操作中利用接触力，并通过自终止力原语与物理量翻译层解决力控闭环速度和原始力不可解释的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿多模态模型已能零样本从图像控制机器人，但许多操作任务依赖图像无法呈现的接触力；将力引入 API-only 冻结模型面临两个挑战：接触力变化远快于模型决策速度，且冻结模型无法直接理解原始力序列。

Method: 提出 ForceHarness：先从腕部传感器或仅关节扭矩标定手部力；用自终止力原语在模型之下自主调节力并在接触事件返回，实现快速力闭环；再用物理翻译层将每条力轨迹转换为带不确定性的物理量作为工具结果供模型推理。为公平评估，构建含隐藏物理变量的七任务 MuJoCo 基准 HiddenPhys。

Result: 在无腕部力传感器的真实机械臂上，ForceHarness 将 GPT-6 Astra 的零样本成功率从 3.3% 提升到 90.0%；仿真中在公开 ForceBench 上提升 17.5 个百分点，在 HiddenPhys 上提升 34.3 个百分点，超过基于演示训练的力感知策略，并在 HiddenPhys 上匹配手工专家控制器。消融表明，较小模型接收物理量而非原始力值时决策显著更好。

Conclusion: ForceHarness 表明冻结多模态模型可通过合适的力接口实现强零样本力感知操作，关键在于将快速力控下放至原语层、并将力信息转化为模型可推理的物理量。

Abstract: Frontier multimodal models can now control robots zero-shot from images, yet many manipulation tasks depend on contact forces that images cannot show. Bringing force to an API-only frozen model raises two challenges. First, contact evolves much faster than an API model can decide, so the system must close the loop on force beneath the model. Second, a frozen model cannot interpret raw force streams, so force must be returned in a form the model can reason about. Therefore, we present ForceHarness, a harness that lets frozen models use force without demonstrations or finetuning. After calibrating hand force from a wrist sensor or joint torques alone, ForceHarness addresses the first challenge with self-terminating force primitives that regulate force on their own and return at a contact event. To address the second challenge, a physics translation layer turns each force trace into physical quantities with uncertainty as the tool result. To evaluate ForceHarness against expert force controllers and learned policies under identical conditions, we also build HiddenPhys, a seven-task MuJoCo benchmark with hidden physical variables. On a real arm without a wrist force sensor, ForceHarness raises the zero-shot success rate of GPT-6 Astra from 3.3% to 90.0%. In simulation, it adds 17.5 percentage points on the public ForceBench suite and 34.3 percentage points on HiddenPhys, surpassing force-aware policies trained on demonstrations on both benchmarks and matching hand-written expert controllers on HiddenPhys. Ablations further show that smaller models make significantly better decisions when given physical quantities rather than raw force values.

</details>


### [17] [CoopDistill: Equilibrium-Guided Multi-Agent Policy Distillation for Cooperative Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21069)
*Shiyu Fang,Sheng Zhao,Jiarui Zhang,Chengkai Xu,Xiaocong Zhao,Peng Hang,Jian Sun*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出CoopDistill——一种 equilibrium-guided 多智能体策略蒸馏框架，将势博弈固定路径纵向参考与残差MARL策略结合，并通过困难案例课程和对比式rollout选择器提升协同驾驶安全性与效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 协同驾驶需从单车决策扩展到联合机动。优化方法虽可施加安全约束，但常固定路径以简化多车规划，限制空间灵活性；MARL虽通过交互探索替代方案，却面临协调学习与安全执行难题；二者集成还受探索效率、经验分配和执行可靠性制约。

Method: CoopDistill以势博弈（PG）提供固定路径纵向参考，残差MARL策略学习纵向加速度、冲突接近时机与横向路径修正；采用困难案例课程将经验集中于PG未解决或显著延迟的交互；在执行前用对比式rollout选择器评估学习修正与PG的联合后果并选择动作。

Result: 在汇入、环岛和交叉口场景中，于四种网联自动驾驶车辆渗透率下与六个基线比较：相对PG，成功率提升2.1–27.1个百分点，延迟降低7.3–27.3%，未观察到碰撞。

Conclusion: CoopDistill通过均衡引导的残差策略蒸馏、困难案例经验集中与执行前联合后果评估，在保持无碰撞的同时显著提升协同驾驶成功率并降低延迟，验证了优化参考与MARL修正结合的有效性。

Abstract: Cooperative driving extends autonomous driving from individual vehicle decisions to joint maneuvers that improve traffic safety and efficiency. Optimization-based methods impose safety constraints but often fix paths to simplify multi-vehicle planning, limiting spatial flexibility. Multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL) explores alternatives through interaction, but coordinated learning and safe execution remain challenging. Their integration nevertheless faces challenges in exploration efficiency, experience allocation, and execution reliability. To address these challenges, this paper proposes CoopDistill, an equilibrium-guided multi-agent policy distillation framework. A potential-game (PG) formulation provides a fixed-path longitudinal reference, around which a residual MARL policy learns longitudinal acceleration, conflict-approach timing, and lateral path corrections. A hard-case curriculum concentrates experience on interactions that PG leaves unresolved or substantially delayed. A comparative rollout selector evaluates the joint consequences of learned corrections against PG before choosing the executed action. Experiments in merging, roundabout, and intersection scenarios compare CoopDistill with six baselines at four connected automated vehicle penetration levels. Relative to PG, success improves by 2.1--27.1 percentage points and delay decreases by 7.3--27.3\%, with no observed collisions. Code is available at this https URL

</details>


### [18] [Multi-CONDOR: Learning Multistable Motion Primitives from Demonstration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21059)
*Gabriele Giuseppe Di Marzo,Mariano Ramírez Montero,Maximilian Stölzle,Rodrigo Pérez-Dattari,Cosimo Della Santina*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 multi-CONDOR 框架，用于学习具有多个离散吸引子或连续吸引目标轮廓的机器人运动基元，并结合模仿学习、潜在参考系统一致性损失与 Lyapunov 收敛性分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于动力系统的运动策略具有稳定性与收敛性，但许多操作任务存在多个不同目标或连续可接受结果，现有统一框架难以在连续自主动力学中同时保持这些替代目标结构。

Method: 在 CONDOR 基础上提出 multi-CONDOR：将模仿学习与损失函数结合，使学习到的任务动力学与编码期望吸引结构的潜在参考系统保持一致；并对学习动力学的收敛性质进行基于 Lyapunov 的分析。

Result: 在离散目标仿真中，相比由状态机选择的分别学习的单目标运动基元，轨迹 RMSE 降低约 72%；在双目标和三目标任务中，将评估初始状态落在目标容差外的比例分别从 StableNODE 的 0.31% 和 7.29% 降至 0%；Franka Emika 机械臂实验表明可从示教学习并成功抓取容器的任一手柄。

Conclusion: multi-CONDOR 在统一框架中兼顾运动复现能力与收敛到多个有效替代结果的灵活性。

Abstract: Dynamical-system-based motion policies provide a framework for learning reactive robot motions with stability and convergence properties. Many manipulation tasks, however, admit several distinct goals or a continuous set of acceptable outcomes, requiring motion primitives that preserve these alternatives. Accommodating both goal structures within a unified framework with continuous, autonomous dynamics remains an open challenge. We propose a CONDOR-based framework, multi-CONDOR, for learning motion primitives with either multiple discrete attractors or a continuous attracting goal contour. The approach combines imitation learning with losses that encourage agreement between the learned task dynamics and latent reference systems encoding the desired attraction structure. We provide a Lyapunov-based analysis of the convergence properties of the learned motion dynamics. We evaluate the framework on simulated benchmarks with discrete goals, qualitative examples with continuous goal contours, and robotic grasping experiments. In simulation, multi-CONDOR reduces trajectory RMSE by approximately 72% compared with separately learned single-goal motion primitives selected by a state machine. For tasks with two and three goals, respectively, it reduces the fraction of evaluated initial states ending outside the goal tolerance from StableNODE's 0.31% and 7.29% to 0% in both settings. Experiments with a Franka Emika robot arm demonstrate successful grasping of a container by either of its two handles using motions learned from demonstrations. Together, these results show multi-CONDOR combines motion reproduction with the flexibility to converge to alternative valid outcomes.

</details>


### [19] [WiTac: Wrist-Informed Tactile Representations for Contact-Rich Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21057)
*Haotian He,Yiyang Chen,Congsheng Xu,Wenchao Yue,Mingze Ye,Shuai Chen,Yi Yang,Yaping Li,Haonan Wang,Weinan E,Wenzhao Lian,Zhijie Deng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: WiTac 是一种预训练触觉表征编码器，在动作生成前将触觉、手部运动与腕部视角深度融合，为下游 VLA 策略提供更具视觉语境和动作可读性的接触表征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相似触觉接触因手、物体与目标配置不同而需要不同修正；单独提供触觉与腕部视觉不如在策略注入前融合，能更好将触觉特征锚定到视觉语境中，从而获得更准确的接触表示。

Method: 提出 Wrist-Informed Tactile Representations（WiTac），在 SharpaDex 约 300 小时数据上预训练，将触觉信号与手部运动、腕部视角深度结合形成综合接触表征；再以该表征条件化下游 VLA 策略（GR00T N1.7 与 π0.5）。

Result: 在四个接触丰富操作任务上，条件化 WiTac 后相较纯视觉基线平均成功率分别提升 28.8% 和 15.0%；相较无腕部视觉的触觉编码器分别再高 18.8% 和 7.5%。离线分析显示其接触描述更准确、任务-接触联合判别更强、触觉噪声下特征更稳定，并能从相似接触中更清晰读出动作方向。

Conclusion: 在策略注入前融合触觉、手部运动与腕部视觉，可显著提升接触丰富操作中的表征质量与下游 VLA 策略成功率，腕部视觉对触觉表征至关重要。

Abstract: Similar tactile contacts can call for different corrections depending on the configuration of the hand, object and target. Rather than supplying touch and wrist vision separately, fusing them before policy injection better grounds tactile features in visual context, yielding more accurate contact representations. We introduce Wrist-Informed Tactile Representations (WiTac), a pretrained encoder that learns such representations before action generation. Pretrained on nearly 300 hours of SharpaDex data, WiTac deeply fuses tactile signals with hand motion and wrist views into a comprehensive contact representation. Conditioning downstream VLA policies (GR00T N1.7 and $\pi_{0.5}$) on WiTac substantially improves task success over vision-only base policies across four contact-rich manipulation tasks (+28.8% and +15.0% on average), and further outperforms a wrist-free tactile encoder by 18.8% and 7.5%, respectively. Offline analyses further show that incorporating wrist vision yields more accurate contact descriptors, stronger joint task-contact discrimination, greater feature stability under tactile noise, and clearer action-direction readout from similar this http URL page: this https URL .

</details>


### [20] [FlowRow: Learning Navigation Behaviors in Agricultural Environments with Long-Horizon Goal-Conditioned Flow Matching Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21035)
*Utkarsh Bajpai,Cédric Pradalier,Stéphanie Aravecchia*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FlowRow 将半结构化农业环境中的跨行导航建模为以机器人为中心的长时域目标导向导航，用基于流匹配的局部规划器根据目标与深度感知生成 SE(2) 路径，并在新基准 RowNavBench 上显著优于生成式行为克隆基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 果园和葡萄园中的自主监测与干预需要机器人完成行内行驶、行端转向与跳行、行间穿越等多样行为；现有学习式行内导航依赖先验田间几何来指定和调节行间转换，难以实现任意行间切换。

Method: 作者把跨行导航表述为目标导向导航任务：长时域目标相对于机器人表达，规划器结合该目标与机载深度观测生成局部路径。提出 FlowRow，一种基于流匹配的局部规划器，将多样农业导航行为建模为 SE(2) 路径分布；同时构建 RowNavBench，在 Isaac Sim 中覆盖葡萄园、柠檬园和苹果园，包含不同行长度、行距与株距、植株位置和杂草生长，共 900 个回合。

Result: FlowRow 达到 87% 成功率；在相同演示数据上重训的最强生成式行为克隆基线为 44%。

Conclusion: 结果表明，将半结构化农业环境导航转化为目标导向局部规划可避免对田间几何先验和手工行间转换策略的依赖，支持更灵活的行间穿越；作者将在论文接收后发布基准、数据和代码。

Abstract: Autonomous monitoring and intervention in semi-structured agricultural environments, such as orchards and vineyards, require mobile robots to navigate to locations across diverse field configurations. This entails performing navigation behaviors including in-row driving, row turning and skipping, and inter-row traversal. Recent learning-based approaches have enabled robust in-row navigation from onboard perception, but navigation across rows typically requires a priori knowledge of field geometry to specify and tune row-transition maneuvers, preventing arbitrary transitions between rows. We address this challenge by framing navigation in these environments as a goal-directed navigation task, where a long-horizon goal is expressed relative to the robot and a planner generates local paths based on the goal and the robot's perceptual observations. We present FlowRow, a flow-matching-based local planner which models the distribution of diverse agricultural navigation behaviors as $\mathrm{SE}(2)$ paths using the long-horizon goal and depth perception. To enable systematic evaluation, we introduce RowNavBench, a navigation benchmark for semi-structured agricultural environments developed in Isaac Sim, comprising 900 episodes across grape vineyards, lemon orchards, and apple orchards with varying row lengths, row and plant spacings, plant positions, and weed growth. FlowRow achieves 87% success, compared to 44% for the strongest generative behavior cloning baseline retrained on the same demonstrations. We will release our benchmark, data, and code upon acceptance.

</details>


### [21] [Inverse Kinematics in Endoscopic Scenarios using MoveIt](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20978)
*Marc Bestmann,Timon Engelke,Rebecca Rodeck,Gerko Wende*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对受限场景中标准MoveIt逆运动学求解器性能不足的问题，本文基准测试现有求解器并比较多种实用改进，发现将IK问题拆分为两部分效果最佳，在内窥镜罐场景中实现100 ms内100%成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 逆运动学对机器人应用至关重要，但基于MoveIt的标准求解器在受限场景中表现不佳，因此需要系统评估现有求解器并寻找实用改进方案。

Method: 对可用IK求解器进行基准测试，并比较多种实际修改策略；其中核心方法是将逆运动学问题拆分为两个部分分别求解。

Result: 拆分IK问题的方法取得最佳性能，在内窥镜罐场景中实现100 ms以内100%的求解成功率。

Conclusion: 将IK问题分解为两部分是一种有效的实用改进，可显著提升受限场景下MoveIt相关逆运动学求解器的性能与成功率。

Abstract: Inverse kinematics are critical in many robotic applications, yet standard MoveIt-based solvers struggle in confined scenarios. This paper provides a benchmark of the available solvers and compares multiple practical modifications for improving the performance of the solvers. Splitting the IK problem into two parts results in the best performance, leading to a 100% success rate under 100 ms in an endoscopic tank scenario.

</details>


### [22] [ROSE: A Shared Cross-Embodiment Facial-Motion Latent Space for Humanoid Robot Expression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20950)
*Yongji Fu,Man Cao,Yi Zhou,Fei Lin,Mark F. Hansen,Guanqun Cao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出ROSE框架，将仿生高密度硬件、跨本体共享面部运动表征学习与多模态运动生成结合，实现紧凑人形机器人头部的自然全脸动态表情。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人面部表情系统多局限于唇部运动或预设脚本；紧凑头部难以容纳密集且独立可控的驱动，而基于视频、关键点或面部参数再反解执行器命令的渲染—反演路径会引入延迟和伪影，且难以可靠迁移到实体机器人。

Method: 硬件上采用柔性腱鞘传动结构，在19.5 cm仿生头部集成34个执行器，通过解耦执行器布置与面部作用点实现紧凑体积内的协同全脸表情。算法上，ROSE-VAE从人类、数字人和机器人观测中学习身份无关的共享面部运动隐空间；ROSE-Flow将文本、音频或其组合直接映射为该运动空间中的轨迹，不以视频作为生成器输入；ROSE-ACT再将轨迹转换为受约束的执行器命令。

Result: 摘要报告了ROSE系统的构建与物理机器人表情演示，说明其可完成跨本体学习、多模态生成和实体表情输出；未给出定量性能指标或对比实验结果。

Conclusion: 高密度仿生硬件与可执行的共享面部运动表征相结合，可统一跨本体学习、多模态运动生成和物理机器人面部表达，为自然、连贯的全脸动态表情提供可行路径。

Abstract: Natural, coherent full-face dynamic expressions are central to making humanoid robots socially approachable, yet existing systems commonly realize only limited lip motion or scripted expressions. The limitation is both physical and algorithmic. A compact head leaves little room for densely packed, independently controllable actuation; meanwhile, learned systems often generate an avatar video, landmarks, or facial parameters and then infer actuator commands through visual retargeting. This render-and-invert path adds latency and artifacts, and it does not reliably transfer generated behavior to a physical robot. We present ROSE (RObot facial expression through Shared motion Embeddings), a framework that integrates biomimetic hardware, cross-embodiment motion representation learning, and multimodal motion generation. On the hardware side, we build a flexible tendon-sheath transmission structure that integrates 34 actuators into a 19.5 cm biomimetic head. By decoupling actuator placement from facial action points, it enables coordinated full-face expression within a compact volume. On the algorithmic side, ROSE-VAE learns an identity-independent shared facial-motion latent space from human, digital-human, and robot observations. ROSE-Flow directly maps text, audio, or their combination to trajectories in this motion space without using video as a generator input. ROSE-ACT then converts these trajectories into constrained actuator commands. Our system demonstrates how high-density biomimetic hardware and a shared executable facial-motion representation can jointly unify cross-embodiment learning, multimodal generation, and physical robot expression.

</details>


### [23] [TacBridge: Learning Unified Tactile Representations through Cross-Sensor Correspondence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20939)
*Yongji Fu,Yijiong Lin,Yi Zhou,Yi Qi,Guanqun Cao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TacBridge 通过跨传感器对应关系与掩码触觉视频预训练，从多传感器触觉视频中学习统一表征，以缓解不同光学触觉传感器观测差异导致的表征迁移与多传感器数据联合使用难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GelSight、DIGIT、GelSlim、TacTip 等光学触觉传感器在成像几何、背景外观、光照、分辨率和形变响应上存在差异，使同一接触事件产生传感器相关观测，限制表征迁移和多传感器数据联合利用。

Method: 提出 TacBridge 框架：先进行多传感器掩码触觉视频预训练以学习跨传感器时空表征；随后利用匹配接触事件的成对源/目标视频，将同一掩码模型联合训练于同传感器重建与跨传感器重建，从而建立跨传感器对应关系。

Result: 在跨传感器翻译和下游感知任务上的实验表明，所学触觉表征具有跨传感器泛化能力和任务可迁移性。

Conclusion: TacBridge 可通过跨传感器对应学习统一触觉表征，提升多光学触觉传感器之间的表征迁移与联合数据利用能力。

Abstract: Optical tactile sensors record tactile videos that capture local deformation and its temporal evolution during contact, supporting robot manipulation, force estimation, and tactile representation learning. Differences in imaging geometry, background appearance, illumination, resolution, and deformation response lead to sensor-dependent observations of the same contact across GelSight, DIGIT, GelSlim, TacTip, and other optical tactile sensors. This variability limits representation transfer and the joint use of multi-sensor data. We introduce TacBridge, a framework for learning unified representations from multi-sensor tactile videos through cross-sensor correspondence. Specifically, multi-sensor masked tactile-video pre-training learns spatiotemporal representations across sensors. We then train the same masked model jointly on same-sensor and cross-sensor reconstruction, using paired source and target videos of matched contact events. Experiments on cross-sensor translation and downstream perception demonstrate the cross-sensor generalization and task transferability of the learned tactile representations.

</details>


### [24] [Transferable Neural CBFs via Generalized Closure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20929)
*Nishant Jayesh Bhave,Parthib Khound*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该文研究如何让单个神经控制屏障函数（Neural CBF）跨不同障碍物环境复用：通过将状态表示为相对各障碍物的坐标，提出广义闭合条件、残差刻画、提升定理与秩检验，并在双积分器、平面飞行器和12状态四旋翼上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典CBF难以针对欠驱动载具手工设计且安全集过于保守；神经CBF虽可从数据学习更具信息量的屏障，但通常只针对单一固定障碍物配置验证，缺乏跨环境复用保证。

Method: 将状态表达为相对每个障碍物的坐标，并针对相对坐标可能丢失其自身演化所需信息的问题，给出广义闭合条件，将缺失信息表示为残差；提出提升定理，把已验证的相对坐标屏障迁移到同一族中的每个障碍物；同时给出秩检验，判断仅靠对称性是否足以支持迁移。

Result: 在双积分器、平面飞行器和12状态四旋翼上验证：未通过秩检验的坐标会导致障碍物穿透或悬停附近屏障条件失效；四旋翼的体坐标系坐标可精确闭合。所得屏障无需重新训练即可迁移到新地图，并在部署与快速启动实验中有效。

Conclusion: 该工作为神经CBF的跨障碍物复用提供了可验证的条件与迁移框架，表明相对坐标设计需满足闭合/秩条件，且体坐标系对四旋翼可实现精确闭合与免训练迁移。

Abstract: Classical control barrier functions (CBFs) provide safety constraints for controllers, but designing them by hand is difficult for underactuated vehicles and the resulting safe sets can be overly conservative. Neural CBFs can learn more informative barriers from data, yet a learned barrier is usually verified only for one fixed obstacle configuration. We study whether one neural barrier can be reused across environments by expressing the state relative to each obstacle. The key gap is that these relative coordinates may discard information needed to predict their own evolution. We give a generalized closure condition that represents this missing information as a residual and a lifting theorem that transfers a verified relative-coordinate barrier to every obstacle in a family. A rank test also identifies when symmetry alone cannot justify the transfer. We validate the conditions on a double integrator, a planar aircraft, and a 12-state quadrotor. Coordinates that fail the test lead to obstacle penetration or to a barrier condition that fails near hover, whereas body-frame coordinates close exactly for the quadrotor. The resulting barrier transfers to new maps without retraining and is effective in the reported deployment and fast-start experiments.

</details>


### [25] [Driving the Style: Velocity-Conditioned Whole-Body Control from Motion Demonstrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20888)
*Murad Dawood,Oezge Drama Berkenkamp,Antonio Gonzales Marin,Nutan Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该工作提出一种仅以紧凑运动指令和机器人观测为输入的人形机器人策略：用全身跟踪奖励训练，但参考动作只用于奖励和特权评论器，不向演员提供参考关节轨迹，也无需教师蒸馏，从而在部署时复现示范的全身风格。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对抗模仿能提供风格信号但难以迁移到硬件；全身跟踪强化学习部署可靠且模仿忠实，但策略依赖运行时未来参考轨迹，遇到训练分布外动作容易失败。作者希望在保持可部署性的同时，把示范风格编码进仅由运动指令驱动的策略中。

Method: 直接用全身跟踪奖励训练人形机器人策略，同时限制演员输入为紧凑的运动指令和机器人观测。参考动作仅用于监督奖励和特权评论器，不向演员提供参考关节轨迹，也不使用教师蒸馏。示范风格通过跟踪奖励编码到策略中，使机器人按指令速度复现全身风格。实验仅用一段随机行走动作训练，并用来自留出动作的速度曲线进行指令测试。

Result: 在仿真中成功率至少98%，并在Unitree G1上完成所有试验。对留出的慢速行走，全身跟踪成功率降至2%，指令条件蒸馏为56%，两者在真实机器人上均失败。在训练过的动作上，该策略关节位置误差保持在全身跟踪的8%以内，且风格覆盖率高于AMP；AMP在无安全吊架时在真实机器人上摔倒。

Conclusion: 将参考动作与演员输入解耦，可以在部署时仅凭运动指令复现示范风格，并获得可靠的实机表现；但其泛化主要限于训练动作下的速度指令，对未见动作仍会显著失效。

Abstract: Achieving human-like behavior on humanoid robots has advanced rapidly by leveraging motion-capture demonstrations rather than hand-crafted cost functions. Adversarial imitation supplies a style signal that lets a policy fulfill a task while adhering to a desired style, but is difficult to transfer to hardware. Whole-body tracking through reinforcement learning instead deploys reliably and imitates demonstrations faithfully, but the resulting policies are conditioned on the future reference, so they require a reference trajectory at runtime and can fail when the motion differs from the training data. We train a humanoid policy directly with whole-body tracking rewards while restricting its actor to compact locomotion commands and robot observations. Reference motion supervises the reward and privileged critic, without supplying reference joint trajectories to the actor or requiring teacher distillation. The demonstrated style is thereby encoded in the policy through the tracking reward, so the robot reproduces the whole-body style under commanded velocities. Trained on a single random-walk motion and commanded with velocity profiles from held-out motions, our policy reaches at least 98\% success in simulation and completes every trial on the Unitree G1. On held-out slow walking, whole-body tracking drops to 2\% success and command-conditioned distillation to 56\%, and both fail on the real robot. On the trained motions, the joint position error of our policy stays within 8\% of whole-body tracking, and it has a higher style coverage than adversarial motion priors (AMP), which fell on the real robot without a safety crane.

</details>


### [26] [QTOP: Scaling Humanoid Motion Tracking with Quantile Critics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20791)
*Markus Peschl,Dana Kianfar,Daniel Dijkman*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该文提出QTOP，用状态-参考对条件下的分位数分布式优势估计提升人体运动跟踪强化学习的样本效率，并在仿真和真实场景中验证其优于同策略与异策略基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人体全身运动跟踪策略的强化学习训练需扩展到数千GPU小时和大量数据才能高保真泛化到未见运动；作者假设运动跟踪本质上是计算受限的，因为多样运动训练会像多任务强化学习一样引入学习干扰。

Method: 提出分位数跟踪优化（QTOP），通过引入以状态和参考运动对为条件的分布式优势估计来提高样本效率，从而缓解多样运动带来的学习干扰。

Result: QTOP在多个数据集的仿真和真实世界跟踪场景中优于同策略和异策略强化学习基线，提升最高达10%；作者还建立了QTOP的缩放律，发现其性能可预测扩展，且在中等GPU小时区间内数据集规模对性能影响很小。

Conclusion: QTOP表明通过改进分布式优势估计可有效提升运动跟踪强化学习的样本效率，并为该任务的计算扩展与数据规模作用提供了缩放律证据。

Abstract: Learning human motion tracking policies through reinforcement learning (RL) has emerged as a core capability that underpins a majority of recent progress in humanoid whole-body control. However, achieving generalization to unseen motions at high fidelity requires jointly scaling training to thousands of GPU and dataset hours. This work hypothesizes that motion tracking is inherently compute-bound because training on a diverse set of motions introduces learning interference, similar to multi-task RL. To tackle this, we propose Quantile Tracking Optimization (QTOP), which improves sample efficiency by introducing a distributional advantage estimate conditioned on state and reference pairs. QTOP outperforms on and off-policy RL baselines both in simulation and real-world tracking scenarios on multiple datasets by up to 10\%. Furthermore, we establish a scaling law for QTOP and find that, aside from scaling predictably, dataset size has minimal impact on performance for moderately sized GPU hour regimes.

</details>


### [27] [Non-Imaging Optical Tactile Sensors for Robotics: Transduction Principles, System Integration, and Robotic Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20770)
*Hao Wu,Yu Feng,Haotian Guo,Huichan Zhao,Changsheng Wu,Jianshu Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该综述提出统一框架，将机械载荷传递、光学编码、读出与触觉推断同机器人任务需求关联，系统评估非成像光学触觉传感器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非成像光学触觉传感器依赖光强、波长、光谱或光子数变化编码接触；其有效性取决于能否在耦合机械与环境效应下可靠区分接触变量，并及时提供信息指导动作。

Method: 综述反射与波导器件、微纳光纤、光纤光栅、干涉与谐振结构及力致发光复合材料；从光学灵敏度与变量可辨识性、系统级空间覆盖与反馈时序、器件表征/离线识别/闭环机器人性能的证据层级三方面建立评估框架，并用代表性研究说明力估计、接触定位、滑移校正和分布式交互。

Result: 高光学灵敏度仅在相关接触与校准条件下目标变量可辨识时才能支持准确触觉估计；空间覆盖和反馈时序需在复用、采集、解调与推断的系统层面评估；器件表征、离线识别与闭环性能提供不同层级证据，需采用不同评价标准。

Conclusion: 提出校准迁移、可靠接口、可扩展读出与任务级验证等优先方向，为选择和开发面向机器人的非成像光学触觉架构提供依据。

Abstract: Non-imaging optical tactile sensors encode contact through changes in optical intensity, wavelength, spectra, or photon counts, offering diverse architectures for robotic touch. Their usefulness depends on whether contact variables can be reliably distinguished from coupled mechanical and environmental effects, and whether the resulting information can be delivered in time to guide action. This review develops a unified framework linking mechanical load transmission, optical encoding, readout, and tactile inference to robotic task requirements. It examines reflective and waveguide-based devices, micro/nanofibers, fiber gratings, interferometric and resonant structures, and mechanoluminescent composites. Three considerations emerge across these approaches. First, high optical sensitivity supports accurate tactile estimation only when target variables remain identifiable under the relevant contact and calibration conditions. Second, spatial coverage and feedback timing must be assessed at the system level, accounting for multiplexing, acquisition, demodulation, and inference. Third, device characterization, offline recognition, and closed-loop robotic performance provide distinct levels of evidence and require different evaluation criteria. Representative studies illustrate how these considerations shape force estimation, contact localization, slip correction and distributed interaction. This review concludes with priorities for calibration transfer, reliable interfaces, scalable readout, and task-based validation, providing a basis for selecting and developing non-imaging optical tactile architectures for robotics.

</details>


### [28] [Robotic Reward Models in the Foundation-Model Era](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20741)
*Guoqiang Liang,Yiqi Lin,Shangchen Zhou,Wenzheng Zeng,Yanzhe Chen,Zechen Bai,Mike Zheng Shou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该综述将114种机器人奖励模型按原生输出回答的问题分为五类：结果、绝对进展、相对比较、时间价值和混合；并提出RRM-Bench-1K基准与统一评测，发现最终分数可用于执行排序，但稠密信用更贴近执行进程而非任务状态，且现有评测难以证明奖励真正跟踪任务完成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人奖励模型（RRM）试图用基础模型替代逐任务奖励工程，但同名方法实际回答不同问题，且现有评测多依赖与演示顺序的一致性及下游策略收益，无法说明奖励是否反映任务完成情况。

Method: 作者按输出语义将114种方法归入Outcome、Absolute Progress、Relative、Temporal Value和Hybrid五类，分析模型设计、监督来源与评测实践；构建RRM-Bench-1K，包含1,117段真实机器人闭环执行视频及任务状态和相机证据标签，并设计四个对比套件以分离被信用条件与其常见线索；对27个评测器进行统一研究，并开源RRM-Hub以应用14个现有RRM和测试新评测器。

Result: 最强评测器的最终分数足以用于整段执行的选择与过滤；但稠密信用更跟随执行进程而非任务状态，且没有评测器能提示关键视角缺失。

Conclusion: 奖励模型所需保真度取决于其驱动的决策；未来需从真实失败监督出发，并设计能区分任务状态与时间进程的指标，同时通过RRM-Hub支持可复现的统一评测。

Abstract: Robotic reward models (RRMs) use foundation models to replace per-task reward engineering with reusable, task-conditioned evaluation. Yet under one name, their outputs answer different questions, from whether a task succeeded to which execution is better. This survey organizes 114 methods into five semantic families by the question their native outputs answer: Outcome, Absolute Progress, Relative, Temporal Value, and Hybrid. Within this taxonomy, we review how model design determines what an evaluator can judge, how raw experience becomes supervision, and how existing evaluation practice tests the result. The review exposes a gap: agreement with demonstration order and downstream policy gains do not show whether a reward tracks task fulfillment. We therefore introduce RRM-Bench-1K, 1,117 real-robot videos of closed-loop executions with task-state and camera-evidence labels, and four contrast suites that separate the credited condition from its usual cues. A unified study of 27 evaluators shows that the strongest final scores rank whole executions well enough for selection and filtering. Dense credit, however, follows the progression of an execution more closely than its task state, and no evaluator signals when the decisive view is missing. How much fidelity a reward model needs therefore depends on the decision it drives. We outline directions from real-failure supervision to metrics that separate task state from chronology, and open-source RRM-Hub, a toolbox in which 14 existing RRMs can be readily applied and new evaluators tested on the same capabilities.

</details>


### [29] [Selective Sensor Acquisition for Multimodal Place Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20730)
*Margaux Edwards,Tobias Fischer,Michael Milford*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出选择性、可控制的多传感器采集策略：仅在主传感器性能估计较差时启用第二传感器，并通过在线阈值调整满足用户指定的第二传感器使用率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态位置识别虽能提升鲁棒性，但现有系统对每个查询都刚性读取并处理所有传感器，即使主传感器已足够，这会带来能耗、第二传感器更新率及被动隐身等领域约束。

Method: 用户指定期望的第二传感器使用率；系统根据主传感器近期性能在线调整阈值，仅在主传感器估计性能不足时采集额外传感器。方法只需PR方法输出匹配分数，适用于单帧和序列PR，且无需针对传感器模态、PR方法或环境做专门校准。

Result: 在多个数据集、PR描述子和传感器组合上，第二传感器仅用于25%查询时可恢复55%的可用PR性能增益，用于50%查询时可恢复81%。论文还分析了其他采集决策机制和使用率控制方法，并考察第二传感器强于、相当于或弱于主传感器时的表现。

Conclusion: 该方案实现了可控制、可泛化的传感器使用率与PR性能权衡，将额外传感集中于融合最可能有益的查询，并避免针对具体模态、方法或环境的校准。

Abstract: Multimodal Place Recognition (PR) can improve robustness, but most systems rigidly read and process every sensor for every query, even when the primary sensor is sufficient, with implications for energy consumption, second sensor update rate, and domain-specific considerations such as passive stealth. We introduce selective, controllable sensor acquisition, where a user specifies a desired second sensor usage rate and the system acquires the additional sensor only when the primary sensor's estimated performance is sufficiently poor. An online threshold adjustment achieves the requested usage rate by adapting to recent primary sensor performance, directing additional sensing towards queries where fusion is most likely to help without calibration specific to sensor modality, PR method, or environment. The approach requires only a PR method that produces a match score and applies to both single-frame and sequence-based PR. Our system, across multiple datasets, PR descriptors, and sensor combinations, recovers 55% of the available PR performance gain while using the second sensor on only 25% of queries, and 81% while using it on 50%. We further analyse alternative acquisition decision mechanisms and usage-rate control methods, and examine performance when the second sensor is stronger than, comparable to, or weaker than the primary sensor.

</details>


### [30] [ScaleVLA: Scaling Vision-Language-Action Models to 552 Billion Parameters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21607)
*Ruixi Yu,Dairu Liu,Xuchuan Chen,Zekun Qi,Zekai Li,Zhuo Cao,Luyang Niu,Yingsheng Zhang,Sikai Liang,Ke Fan,He Wang,Li Yi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ScaleVLA将552B DeepSeek-V4.1-Flash视觉语言骨干接入可训练动作DiT，并在统一训练设置下系统比较2B至552B VLM，证明前沿规模VLM可作为高效、可泛化的机器人策略表示基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Vision-Language-Action策略多依赖较小VLM骨干，未充分利用前沿VLM在广泛预训练中获得的物理行动相关知识；作者探究前沿规模表示能否为机器人学习提供更强基础。

Method: 提出ScaleVLA：连接552B DeepSeek-V4.1-Flash骨干与可训练动作DiT，在共享训练设置下比较2B到552B的VLM，并评估机器人基础模型的样本效率、泛化性和精细操作三项能力。

Result: 仅10条演示即超过2B骨干用50条演示的效果（87.8% vs. 85.6%）；LIBERO-PRO成功率达45.1%，小骨干最高31.3%；真实充电器插入从4/10提升至9/10，增益集中在接触敏感对齐；每动作块中位延迟216 ms。

Conclusion: 前沿VLM不仅可作为推理智能体，也可作为高效且可泛化机器人策略的强大表示基础；规模带来的收益在样本效率、泛化和精细操作上均显著。

Abstract: Frontier Vision-Language Models (VLMs) such as GPT-6 Astra have demonstrated remarkable capabilities in robot control, suggesting that broad pretraining provides knowledge valuable for physical action. Yet most Vision-Language-Action (VLA) policies still rely on much smaller VLM backbones, leaving this potential largely untapped. We ask whether frontier-scale representations can provide a stronger foundation for robot learning. We introduce ScaleVLA, which connects a 552B DeepSeek-V4.1-Flash backbone to a trainable action DiT, and systematically compare VLMs from 2B to 552B under a shared training setup. We evaluate three fundamental capabilities of robot foundation models. In sample efficiency, just 10 demonstrations outperform a 2B backbone trained with 50 (87.8% vs. 85.6%). In generalization, LIBERO-PRO success reaches 45.1%, compared with at most 31.3% for smaller backbones. In precise manipulation, real-world charger insertion improves from 4/10 to 9/10 successes, with gains concentrated in contact-sensitive alignment. Despite its scale, ScaleVLA achieves 216 ms median latency per action chunk. Our findings suggest that frontier VLMs can serve not only as reasoning agents, but also as powerful representational foundations for efficient and generalizable robot policies.

</details>


### [31] [SADL: On the Demand-Supply Geometry of Representation Collapse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21596)
*Junhan Sun,Yigui Feng,Mingzhe Liu,Haoyan Xu,Lei Ye,Ziqi Han,Hanru Bai,Yanzhao Zhang,Hao Zhao,Guofeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该文指出JEPA中仅约束投影器输出不能防止表征坍缩，并提出SADL：比较投影器输出所“需求”的表征差异与编码器可“供给”的差异，仅在供给不足时直接约束编码器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从初始化开始，编码器表征被不同输入共享的分量主导；批归一化投影器会移除该分量，因此投影器之后的正则化无法纠正编码器问题，表征差异会缩小直至坍缩。

Method: SADL（Supply And Demand Loss）保留原训练目标，在需要时直接约束编码器：比较投影器输出要求的表征差异与编码器能保留并传给投影器的差异，仅在供给不足时加入约束；兼容不同Demand Regularizers，且不引入辅助网络、额外可训练参数或架构改动。

Result: 在单任务及四任务联合训练的LeWM与INTACT实验中，SADL将十个随机种子的坍缩率从50%降至0%；训练一个epoch后，编码器有效秩为基线的3.4–6.4倍，潜在预测误差降至基线的1/20–1/303；损失权重跨数量级仍稳健；多种Demand Regularizers下INTACT平均无搜索控制成功率超过95%；在四个图像数据集上也持续提升准确率与随机种子稳定性。

Conclusion: 将正则化器所要求的表征差异与编码器可供给的差异相匹配，可在控制与图像分类任务中实现更快、更稳定的表征学习。

Abstract: In Joint-Embedding Predictive Architectures (JEPAs), a well-constrained projector output does not by itself prevent representation collapse. We find that, from initialization, the encoder representation is dominated by a component shared by different inputs. A batch-normalized projector removes this component, so regularization after the projector cannot correct it, and the differences between representations shrink until they collapse. We propose SADL (Supply And Demand Loss), which keeps the original training objective and constrains the encoder directly when needed. SADL compares the representation differences that the projector output demands with the differences the encoder can retain and pass on to the projector, and adds a constraint on the encoder only when supply falls short of demand. It works with different Demand Regularizers and introduces no auxiliary networks, additional trainable parameters, or architectural changes. In single-task and jointly trained four-task LeWorldModel (LeWM) and INTACT experiments, SADL lowers the collapse rate over ten random seeds from 50% to 0%. After one epoch of training, encoder effective rank is 3.4-6.4 times that of the corresponding baseline without SADL, and latent prediction error falls to 1/20-1/303 of it. Without additional tuning, SADL stays robust as its loss weight spans orders of magnitude, and with several Demand Regularizers INTACT exceeds 95% mean search-free control success. On four image datasets, SADL also consistently improves accuracy and stability across random seeds. These results show that matching the representation differences a regularizer demands with the differences the encoder can supply leads to faster and more stable representation learning in control and image classification.

</details>


### [32] [Lazy-MOSAIC: Accelerating Skill-Centric Manipulation Planning with Learned Skill-Effect World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21517)
*Itamar Mishani,Mukund Krishnakumar,Oren Salzman,Maxim Likhachev*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Lazy-MOSAIC 是一种面向长时程操作任务的懒惰规划框架：先用廉价学习到的技能效果模型预测候选技能可行性与结果状态，仅对高潜力技能进行仿真验证，并在假设失效时局部修复搜索，从而减少仿真查询并加速规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程操作需要在参数化技能库构成的巨大混合空间中规划技能序列及其参数；用物理仿真等高保真世界模型评估每个候选技能的成本很高。现有 MOSAIC 虽能利用技能引导非序列搜索，但仍对每个参数化技能支付完整仿真成本。

Method: Lazy-MOSAIC 改为在假设的技能结果上进行懒惰搜索：学习一个技能效果世界模型，以亚毫秒级预测技能可行性和产生的世界状态；据此将昂贵仿真集中在有希望的候选技能上；当假设被证伪时，仅修复搜索中受影响的部分；同时保证返回计划中的每个技能都经过仿真验证。

Result: 在难度递增的杂乱货架取物任务和真实机器人上评估，Lazy-MOSAIC 相比基线规划显著更快、仿真器查询更少，并在所有设置中达到匹配或更高的成功率。

Conclusion: 通过用廉价技能效果预测筛选候选并局部修复搜索，Lazy-MOSAIC 能在保证计划仿真验证的前提下显著提升长时程技能规划效率，适用于复杂操作场景。

Abstract: Long-horizon manipulation tasks require a robot to perform a sequence of physical interactions with its environment, typically combining several different skills. Planning such behaviors efficiently is challenging: given a library of parameterized skills, solutions lie in a vast hybrid space of skill sequences and their parameters, and evaluating each candidate skill's feasibility and effect with a high-fidelity world model such as a physics simulator is computationally expensive. MOSAIC, a recent skill-centric approach, lets the skills guide a non-sequential search toward the regions of this space where they are likely to succeed; however, it pays the full cost of simulation for every parameterized skill it considers. In this work, we introduce Lazy-MOSAIC, a lazy planning framework that instead searches over hypothesized skill outcomes. A cheap learned skill-effect world model predicts, in a fraction of a millisecond, a skill's feasibility and the world state it produces. Lazy-MOSAIC then uses these predictions to focus expensive simulation on promising candidate skills and, when a hypothesis is invalidated, repairs only the affected part of the search, while ensuring that every skill in a returned plan is validated in simulation. We evaluate Lazy-MOSAIC on cluttered-shelf retrieval tasks of increasing difficulty and demonstrate it on a physical robot, showing that it plans substantially faster than baselines with fewer simulator queries, while matching or exceeding their success rate in every setting. Visit this http URL .

</details>


### [33] [GHySSeg: Real-Time Geometric Ground and Road Segmentation for Hybrid Solid-State LiDAR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21507)
*Martín Palos,Fernando García*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出纯几何地面与道路分割方法 GHySSeg，面向量产混合固态 LiDAR 的无环、稠密、前向视场点云，并发布首个相关公开基准 HySoLiD-Drive。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合固态 LiDAR 已进入量产车，但其点云结构不同于传统 360° 旋转机械 LiDAR：无环、稠密、杂乱且集中于前向视场。依赖环-列有序结构的经典几何方法易退化或失效，学习型方法计算重、难认证且多基于旋转 LiDAR 数据训练。

Method: GHySSeg 从单帧混合固态 LiDAR 点云中逐条扫描曲线追踪，利用两个几何特征——高度阶跃与距离回折——进行纯几何的地面与可行驶道路分割；无需训练，也不依赖 GPU。为评测，发布 HySoLiD-Drive 基准，包含两辆车真实驾驶采集的 100 帧人工标注与 7000 帧未标注数据。

Result: 在 HySoLiD-Drive 上，GHySSeg 在地面与道路分割上均优于几何与学习型 SOTA 基线；道路 IoU 达 0.907，地面 IoU 达 0.896，精度超过 0.96，并在单核上约 22 ms/帧实时运行。

Conclusion: 该工作表明混合固态 LiDAR 的稠密逐曲线几何可被转化为方法优势，GHySSeg 以无需训练、无需 GPU 的实时纯几何方案弥合产业首选传感器与自动驾驶研究之间的数据与方法鸿沟，并提供专用公开基准。

Abstract: Hybrid solid-state LiDAR sensors are making their way into production vehicles, yet the data they produce differs structurally from that of the established spinning mechanical sensors behind today's autonomous-driving datasets: it is ring-free, dense, cluttered, and concentrated in a frontal field of view. The classical geometric methods that autonomous vehicles rely on for drivable space computation and navigation assume the ordered ring-and-column structure of a 360-degree spinning LiDAR and degrade or fail on this data, while learned alternatives are computationally heavy, hard to certify, and trained almost exclusively on spinning-LiDAR data. We demonstrate that the same dense, per-curve geometry is instead an opportunity to close the divide between the industry's preferred sensors and autonomous-driving research. We present GHySSeg, a purely geometric method that segments ground and drivable road from a single hybrid solid-state LiDAR frame by tracing each scan curve and reading two geometric signatures, a step in height and a fold-back in range, with no training and no graphics processing unit. To evaluate it, we release HySoLiD-Drive, the first public benchmark dedicated to ground and road segmentation on an automotive hybrid solid-state LiDAR, comprising 100 manually labeled and 7000 unlabeled frames recorded by two vehicles in real driving. On this benchmark, GHySSeg surpasses both geometric and learned state-of-the-art baselines across ground and road segmentation, reaching an intersection-over-union of 0.907 for road and 0.896 for ground at over 0.96 precision, while running in real time at about 22 milliseconds per frame on a single core.

</details>


### [34] [Safe Physical Interaction of All-Electric Heavy-Duty Robotic Manipulators via Control Barrier Functions and Variable Impedance Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21309)
*Amir Hossein Barjini,Mohammad Bahari,Jouni Mattila*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种面向EMLA驱动重型机械臂的安全感知物理交互架构，将VIC与CBF叠加于VDC框架，并验证其力交互安全性与约束保持能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全电重型机械臂需在隧道壁、设备等附近执行大载荷任务，仅靠精确运动控制无法满足安全与物理交互需求，因此需要兼顾力能力、接近/接触风险与执行器/工作空间约束的安全控制。

Method: 在VDC框架上加入VIC与CBF：VIC依据预测接近与接触风险连续调节任务空间刚度和阻尼；关节速度CBF滤波器约束笛卡尔工作空间边界及闭链升降/倾仰机构带来的物理行程限制；利用关节速度跟踪误差实测上界收紧CBF不等式以补偿内层VDC/EMLA动态的非理想速度实现；在工作空间、执行器限制和物理交互场景下对比VDC、VIC+VDC、CBF+VDC、VIC+CBF+VDC四种配置。

Result: VIC使环境交互力峰值和RMS分别降低92.59%和96.33%；两种鲁棒CBF配置均保持正的工作空间与EMLA安全裕度；VIC+CBF+VDC相比CBF+VDC将CBF干预指标降低10.30%。

Conclusion: VIC+CBF+VDC可在保持工作空间和执行器安全裕度的同时显著降低物理交互力，并减少CBF干预，说明该架构能提升重型机械臂近结构作业的安全感知物理交互性能。

Abstract: All-electric heavy-duty robotic manipulators are increasingly expected to operate close to tunnel walls, equipment, and other structures while retaining the force capability required for large-payload tasks. Accurate motion control alone does not address the resulting safety and interaction requirements. This paper develops a safety-aware physical-interaction architecture that combines variable impedance control (VIC) with control barrier functions (CBFs) on top of an established virtual decomposition control (VDC) framework for electromechanical linear actuator (EMLA)-driven manipulators. VIC continuously adapts the task-space stiffness and damping according to predicted proximity and contact risk, whereas a joint-velocity CBF filter enforces Cartesian workspace boundaries and the physical stroke limits induced by the closed-chain lift and tilt mechanisms. To account for nonideal realization of the filtered velocity by the inner VDC/EMLA dynamics, the CBF inequalities are tightened using a measured bound on the joint-velocity tracking error. Four configurations, namely VDC, VIC+VDC, CBF+VDC, and VIC+CBF+VDC, are evaluated under workspace, actuator-limit, and physical-interaction scenarios. VIC reduces the peak and RMS environment-interaction forces by 92.59% and 96.33%, respectively; both robust CBF configurations maintain positive workspace and EMLA safety margins, while VIC+CBF+VDC reduces the CBF intervention metric by 10.30% compared with CBF+VDC.

</details>


### [35] [Hybrid Optimization-Generative Motion Planning for Multiagent Affine Formations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21264)
*Zhonggang Li,Hongyu Zhou,Raj Thilak Rajan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该文提出一种面向仿射编队的混合优化-生成式运动规划框架，通过单智能体扩散先验与投影梯度下降交替，实现高维多智能体编队轨迹的高效、安全规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体编队在受限环境中协同导航依赖高质量且动态可行的参考轨迹，但现有编队控制器通常需要先验轨迹，而传统基于约束优化的编队运动规划在高维联合轨迹上面临计算挑战。

Method: 将规划任务建模为约束最大后验估计，解耦多智能体目标以利用预训练单智能体扩散先验保证动态可行性、避障和平滑性；再将生成过程与投影梯度下降交错执行，以严格满足仿射几何约束并提供安全保证。

Result: 仿真表明该框架能生成高度平滑、无碰撞且易于常见分布式控制器跟踪的规划轨迹，并在多项指标上优于现有先进规划器。

Conclusion: 所提混合优化-生成框架有效缓解高维编队运动规划的计算难题，为仿射编队提供安全、平滑且可跟踪的规划方案。

Abstract: Formation maneuver control enables a multiagent system to cooperatively navigate through constrained environments in collective motion. While various formation controllers exist, they rely heavily on high-quality and dynamically feasible reference trajectories provided a priori, making formation motion planning (FMP) essential. To address the computational challenges for high-dimensional joint trajectories faced by conventional constrained optimization-based solutions, this paper introduces a hybrid optimization-generative framework with a focus on affine formations, which provide the necessary spatial flexibility. By formulating the planning task as a constrained Maximum-a-Posteriori estimation, we decouple multiagent objectives to leverage pre-trained single-agent diffusion priors for dynamic feasibility, obstacle avoidance, and smoothness.
