We then interleave the generative process with projected gradient descent (PGD) to strictly enforce affine geometric constraints with safety guarantees. Simulations demonstrate that our framework generates highly smooth, collision-free plans that are readily trackable by common distributed controllers and outperforms state-of-the-art planners in various metrics.

</details>


### [36] [ThinkWAM: Rethinking Latent Futures for Action Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21258)
*Haoyun Tang,Baqiao Deng,Enjin Zhou,Songwen Tian,Keyao Xu,Bowen Jing,Kun Wang,Ke Ma,Zhandong Mei,Huibin Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ThinkWAM 是一种潜在世界动作模型，通过“提议-反思-精炼”循环在动作执行前建立双向反馈：先提出完整动作块，再用该提议校正多 horizon 未来潜在表示，并将校正后的潜在表示注入同一动作头的第二遍推理以生成可执行动作块。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界动作模型将动作生成与未来状态预测耦合，但初始动作块可能有缺陷；若未来预测不以该动作块为条件，就无法反映拟议动作将引起的视觉表示变化，从而限制执行前精炼的有效性。

Method: ThinkWAM 采用 proposal-rethink-refine 循环：策略首先生成完整动作块；该提议通过学习到的残差校正多 horizon 未来潜在表示；校正后的潜在表示被注入同一动作头的第二遍前向过程，生成最终可执行动作块。方法还引入受预测编码启发的 affordance-guided observation-action memory 与 near-fine、far-coarse 多 horizon 预测方案，且所有预测均在潜在空间完成。

Result: ThinkWAM 在 LIBERO 上达到 98.70% 成功率，在无 OOD 微调的 LIBERO-Plus 上达到 84.76%，在 RoboTwin 2.0 Clean/Random 上分别为 85.75%/65.85%，在 RoboCasa24 上达到 65.17%；在三个真实世界 OOD 条件下平均成功率为 64.4%。每个动作块的两遍推理平均延迟为 LIBERO 153.48 ms、RoboTwin 164.61 ms、Galaxea A1Z 101.2 ms。消融显示 rethinking 与 affordance guidance 具有互补增益；诊断表明以提议为条件的校正同时改变预测未来与动作轨迹。

Conclusion: ThinkWAM 通过执行前基于动作提议校正未来潜在表示，并以校正后的未来表示反向精炼动作生成，形成有效的双向反馈；诊断结果支持校正后的未来表示能够改善最终动作，且方法在仿真与真实 OOD 场景中均有较强表现与可接受延迟。

Abstract: World-action models (WAMs) couple action generation with predictions of future states. An initial action chunk may be flawed. A future prediction that is not conditioned on the chunk may fail to reflect the changes that the proposed actions would induce in visual representations, limiting its usefulness for refinement before execution. We introduce ThinkWAM, a latent WAM that establishes bidirectional feedback before execution through a proposal-rethink-refine loop: the policy first proposes a complete action chunk, the proposal corrects multi-horizon future latents through a learned residual, and the corrected latents are injected into a second pass of the same action head, which generates the executable chunk. An affordance-guided observation-action memory and a near-fine, far-coarse multi-horizon prediction scheme, both motivated by predictive coding, support this loop, and all prediction stays in latent space. ThinkWAM achieves 98.70% success on LIBERO, 84.76% on LIBERO-Plus without OOD fine-tuning, 85.75%/65.85% on RoboTwin 2.0 Clean/Random, and 65.17% on RoboCasa24. ThinkWAM achieves 64.4% mean success across three real-world OOD conditions. The mean two-pass inference latency per action chunk is 153.48 ms on LIBERO, 164.61 ms on RoboTwin, and 101.2 ms on the Galaxea A1Z. Ablations show complementary gains from rethinking and affordance guidance. Diagnostics indicate that proposal-conditioned corrections change both predicted futures and action trajectories, showing that the corrected future representations improve the final action.

</details>


### [37] [BoBDeLaN: Bounded-Bandwidth Context Adaptation for Deep Lagrangian Robot Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21209)
*Lucas Schulze,Sucheth Shenoy,Jan Peters,Oleg Arenz*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 BoBDeLaN：用收缩且增益有界的循环平衡网络（REN）上下文编码器结合稳定线性滤波，塑造跨频率的扰动敏感度，并在 MPC 中验证其提升操作任务鲁棒性与平滑性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 上下文感知的 Deep Lagrangian Networks 虽能保留拉格朗日结构并适应不确定负载和未建模动力学，但该结构本身不能约束模型对测量扰动的敏感度，影响预测精度与控制稳定性。

Method: 将收缩、增益有界的 Recurrent Equilibrium Network 作为上下文编码器，并与稳定线性滤波结合，显式整形不同频率下的扰动敏感度；理论保证循环初始化指数遗忘，并给出上下文扰动的有限时域频率加权界，在上下文条件动力学模型满足 Lipschitz 连续性假设下推广到残余力矩预测。采用低通实现并嵌入模型预测控制，在仿真和真实硬件操作任务中评估，并在真实机器人数据上做频率敏感度分析。

Result: 与使用相同输入滤波的 LSTM 编码器相比，BoBDeLaN 的高频上下文敏感度更低、残余力矩预测误差更小、运动更平滑，且在注入扰动下性能退化更少。

Conclusion: BoBDeLaN 通过将稳定滤波与收缩有界上下文编码相结合，为上下文感知物理模型提供了可分析的频率相关扰动敏感度约束，并改善真实机器人控制中的预测精度、平滑性与鲁棒性。

Abstract: Learned physics-based models provide structure for robot control, but uncertain payloads and unmodeled dynamics limit their predictive accuracy. Context-aware Deep Lagrangian Networks enable adaptation through a latent context inferred from measurement histories while retaining Lagrangian structure for each fixed context. However, this structure alone does not constrain sensitivity to measurement disturbances. We introduce BoBDeLaN, which combines a contracting, gain-bounded Recurrent Equilibrium Network context encoder with stable linear filtering to shape disturbance sensitivity across frequencies. This architecture guarantees exponential forgetting of the recurrent initialization and admits a finite-horizon, frequency-weighted bound on context perturbations, which extends to residual torque prediction under a Lipschitz continuity assumption on the context-conditioned dynamics model. We evaluate a low-pass realization within model predictive control on simulated and real hardware manipulation tasks, together with frequency-sensitivity analysis on real-robot data. Compared with an LSTM encoder using the same input filter, BoBDeLaN exhibits reduced high-frequency context sensitivity, lower residual torque prediction error, and smoother motion, with less degradation under injected disturbances.

</details>


### [38] [Cooperative Mobile Manipulation using Learned Dynamical Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21198)
*Saray Bakker,Wendelin Böhmer,Javier Alonso-Mora*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出CALDS框架，将单机器人从示教中学到的动力学系统行为适配为多移动操作机器人的协同执行，在保证全身无碰撞的同时保持任务相关行为与收敛性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 协同移动操作要求机器人协调运动、实现全身避碰并保留任务相关行为；而已学习的单智能体行为无法直接用于多机器人协同。

Method: CALDS基于分别从示教中学习到的动力学系统（DS），包含两个关键组件：Cooperative Policy Selection用于识别可兼容的学习策略组合；Temporal Adaptation用于在保持原始行为空间结构与收敛性质的同时实现无碰撞协同。

Result: 在辅助穿衣、餐厅清洁与上菜、抽屉打开与物品放置三个场景中评估；定量结果表明其提升任务成功率，实现安全、协调的同步执行，并保持对示教行为的保真度。

Conclusion: CALDS能有效将学习到的单智能体行为适配为安全协调的多移动操作机器人协同执行，兼顾任务成功、避碰安全与行为保真。

Abstract: Cooperative mobile manipulation requires robots to coordinate their motions while ensuring whole-body collision avoidance and preserving task-relevant behaviors. We propose CALDS (Cooperative Adaptation of Learned Dynamical Systems), a framework that enables mobile manipulators to adapt learned single-agent behaviors for coordinated execution. Building on individual dynamical systems (DSs) learned from demonstrations, CALDS enables cooperative adaptation through two key components: Cooperative Policy Selection, which identifies compatible combinations of learned policies, and Temporal Adaptation, which ensures collision-free cooperation while preserving the spatial structure and convergence properties of the original behaviors. We evaluate CALDS across three diverse scenarios: assistive dressing, restaurant cleaning and serving, and drawer opening and object placement. Quantitative results demonstrate its effectiveness in improving task success and enabling safe, coordinated simultaneous execution while maintaining fidelity to demonstrated behaviors.

</details>


### [39] [Polygraph: Large-Scale Multi-Session Trajectory Optimization using Pose Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21163)
*Petar Lukovic,Tian Yi Lim,Marc Pollefeys,Pablo Speciale,Hermann Blum,Zuria Bauer*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Polygraph 是一种在线多会话轨迹优化流水线，按关键帧和空间簇增量处理，无需构建3D地图，即可将多个漂移且坐标系独立的轨迹融合为全局一致集合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人、车辆和可穿戴设备会多次经过同一环境，每次产生局部准确但随时间漂移且坐标系不相关的轨迹；现有大规模多会话轨迹优化方法多为离线，需预先提供所有会话、固定一个参考帧并优化单一融合地图，难以满足在线应用需求。

Method: Polygraph 逐关键帧、逐空间簇在线处理会话，从不构建3D地图；将关键帧分组为局部簇，簇闭合时通过学习式图像检索与其他所有会话匹配，候选对经稀疏特征匹配、单目度量深度和鲁棒位姿估计验证；采用两级共识门控，仅在独立证据 corroborate 后将跨会话边加入共享位姿图；使用低成本增量求解保持图最新，并周期性执行鲁棒批量求解以剔除离群值。

Result: 在立体室外驾驶、HoloLens与iPad室内遍历、Boston Dynamics Spot腿足室内运动以及大规模室外车辆和手持路线等场景评估；与同一GPU型号上最接近的先前工作按其报告时间相比，Polygraph 运行更快、提交的更少闭环边，并达到或优于其精度。

Conclusion: Polygraph 证明了在线、增量式、无3D地图的多会话轨迹优化可行，并在速度、闭环边数量与精度上优于或匹配现有离线方法。

Abstract: Robots, vehicles, and wearable devices often traverse the same environment many times. Each pass produces its own trajectory from onboard visual or visual-inertial odometry. These trajectories are locally accurate but drift over time and live in unrelated coordinate frames. Turning many such independent trajectories into one globally consistent set is the large-scale multi-session trajectory optimization problem. Existing solutions largely work offline. All sessions must be available up front, one session is fixed as the reference frame, and a single fused map is then optimized. This paper presents Polygraph, a multi-session trajectory optimization pipeline that processes sessions online, one keyframe and one spatial cluster at a time, and never builds a 3D map. Keyframes are grouped into local clusters. When a cluster closes, it is matched against every other session through learned image retrieval. Candidate pairs are verified with sparse feature matching, monocular metric depth, and robust pose estimation. A two-tier consensus gate admits a cross-session edge into the shared pose graph only once independent evidence corroborates it. A cheap incremental solve keeps the graph current, and a periodic robust batch solve prunes outliers. We evaluate Polygraph on stereo outdoor driving, HoloLens and iPad indoor traversals, legged indoor locomotion with a Boston Dynamics Spot, and large-scale outdoor vehicle and handheld routes. Against the closest prior work on the same GPU model, using its reported timings, Polygraph runs faster, commits fewer loop-closure edges, and matches or improves on accuracy.

</details>


### [40] [WinDEX: Metric 3D Hand Motion Reconstruction in the Wild for Dexterous Teleoperation and Policy Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21143)
*Hongyu Ma,Hairong Qu,Shiqi Zhao,Yongsong Yang,Kai Shi,Jiahao Ai,Xin You,Peng Yin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: WinDEX 提出低成本立体惯性采集、几何-时序优化标注与鱼眼原生手部重建网络，构建大规模野外人手操作数据集，实现高精度离线重建、实时流式推理及机器人强化学习与遥操作应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人手操作数据广泛用于训练机器人实现类人灵巧性，但野外低成本、大规模采集仍困难；现有方法在度量尺度几何重建、真实世界泛化和推理速度方面存在不足。

Method: WinDEX 包含四部分：(1) 从野外第一视角双目视频中进行几何-时序优化，结合视觉惯性 SLAM 实现世界坐标系度量 3D 手部标注；(2) WinDEX-Net 为端到端鱼眼原生网络，融合标定双目、局部细节与时序上下文，并采用模型辅助标注与再训练的数据-模型飞轮；(3) 蒸馏模型支持离线重建与因果流式推理，服务强化学习与遥操作；(4) 低成本 CyberCap 双目惯性采集设备，构建含 1000 小时训练标注及野外 2D 与独立光学动捕评测集的 WinDEX-Hand 数据集。

Result: 离线重建达 43.61 FPS，因果流式达 171 FPS；在留出数据上，野外重投影误差为 14.5 像素，结合视觉惯性里程计的世界坐标关节误差为 37.29 mm。相比使用 WiLoR 和 HaWoR 运动参考训练的强化学习策略，任务成功率分别提升至 7.6 倍和 3.6 倍，并实现实时臂-手遥操作。

Conclusion: WinDEX 证明低成本采集与数据-模型闭环可扩展野外人手操作数据，并兼顾度量精度、推理速度和真实世界泛化，为机器人类人灵巧操作学习与实时遥操作提供有效支撑。

Abstract: Human manipulation data are widely used to train robots for human-like dexterity. However, low-cost, large-scale collection in the wild remains challenging. Existing methods remain limited in geometrically grounded metric-scale reconstruction, real-world generalization and inference speed. We present WinDEX, integrating (1) geometric-temporal optimization for metric 3D hand annotation from in-the-wild egocentric stereo videos, combined with visual-inertial SLAM for world-frame reconstruction; (2) WinDEX-Net, an end-to-end, fisheye-native network combining calibrated stereo, local detail and temporal context, with a data-model flywheel of model-assisted annotation and retraining; (3) distilled models for offline reconstruction and causal streaming, supporting reinforcement learning and teleoperation; and (4) low-cost CyberCap stereo-inertial capture, forming the WinDEX-Hand dataset with 1,000 hours of training annotations and held-out human-annotated in-the-wild 2D and independent optical motion-capture evaluation sets. Offline reconstruction runs at 43.61 FPS and causal streaming at 171 FPS. On held-out data, offline reconstruction achieves state-of-the-art accuracy: 14.5 pixels in-the-wild reprojection error and, with visual-inertial odometry, 37.29 mm world-frame joint error. We achieve 7.6 and 3.6 times the task success rates of reinforcement learning policies trained with WiLoR and HaWoR motion references, respectively, and demonstrate real-time arm-hand teleoperation. Project website: this https URL .

</details>


### [41] [Linglong Agent Service: A Runtime for Centralized Agent Reasoning and Distributed Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21129)
*Hua Zhang,Min Jiang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出 Linglong Agent Service（agentsvc）：一个自托管 HTTP 服务与可嵌入 Python 运行时，将“执行站点”与模型、工具、上下文并列为可配置维度；父服务负责推理编排、工具描述和会话历史，每次推理请求选择一个子环境执行文件、shell、终端和 MCP 操作。还提出 online_debugger 作为执行站点分离的进程级特例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM agent 虽可使用远程工具和云沙箱，但如何集成推理、会话状态与异构执行站点仍是运行时设计问题，缺少统一的运行时抽象与可执行契约。

Method: agentsvc 由父服务保留推理编排、工具描述和对话历史，并按请求选择子环境执行文件、shell、终端与 MCP 操作；直接 HTTP 与子端发起的 WebSocket 隧道承载相同应用接口，支持可移植请求上下文和集中更新。核心路径不依赖第三方 Python 包，仅 Windows 伪终端为可选依赖。online_debugger 将异常证据提交、会话级检查工具执行和同一诊断会话恢复放在事故进程内；默认提示要求源码修改具备失败后通过的复现证据；运行时补丁激活与失败调用恢复尚未实现。作者用约束期望效用推理解释设计，并与 Hermes Agent、Pi、Codex、Claude Code 比较具体机制。

Result: 报告了实现与回归测试证据，证明可执行契约成立；但未证明性能优势、修复准确率或生产环境恢复能力。源码以 MIT 许可证开源。

Conclusion: 主要贡献是执行站点可配置的 agent 运行时分离设计及 online_debugger 的进程级案例；当前证据限于接口与契约层面，部分恢复机制未实现，尚缺性能与修复效果验证。

Abstract: LLM agents can use remote tools and cloud sandboxes, but integrating reasoning, session state, and heterogeneous execution sites remains a runtime design problem. We present Linglong Agent Service (agentsvc), a self-hosted HTTP service and embeddable Python runtime that treats execution site as a configurable dimension alongside model, tools, and context. A parent service retains inference orchestration, tool descriptions, and conversation history; each inference request selects one child environment for file, shell, terminal, and MCP operations. Direct HTTP and child-initiated WebSocket tunnels carry the same application interfaces, including portable request context and centrally managed updates. The normal core path requires no third-party Python packages, with an optional Windows pseudo-terminal dependency. We further present online_debugger as the process-scale special case of execution-site separation: an instrumented program submits exception evidence, executes session-scoped inspection tools inside the incident process, and resumes the same diagnostic session. Its default prompt requires failing-then-passing reproduction evidence for source changes, while runtime patch activation and failed-call recovery remain unimplemented. We explain the design through constrained expected-utility reasoning, compare concrete mechanisms with Hermes Agent, Pi, Codex, and Claude Code, and report implementation and regression-test evidence. The current evidence establishes executable contracts, not performance advantages, repair accuracy, or production recovery. The source code is open source under the MIT license at this https URL .

</details>


### [42] [AVD: A Multi-Sensor Teleoperated Wheelchair Platform with Safety-Aware Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20720)
*Namai Chandra,Madhur Thareja,Boby George*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出AVD——一种面向电动轮椅的低成本、可依赖遥操作辅助移动架构，统一感知、通信、嵌入式控制与远程监督，并给出安全导向设计框架与传感栈实测验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电动轮椅主要依赖手动摇杆，严重运动和视觉障碍者难以独立操作，存在关键移动障碍；需要一种低成本、可部署且安全的远程/辅助驾驶方案。

Method: AVD基于商用硬件构建统一平台；控制权限采用静态层级：手动急停和乘员摇杆优先于远程命令，车载感知层仅有停车权限；计算设计划分主功能与安全功能，并针对故障情形规定心跳驱动的安全状态转换；对感知栈进行实测表征，包括逐通道采集延迟、覆盖五种目标配置的2025次扫描LiDAR退化实验（水雾、粉尘、直接遮挡及对照）以及至47度静态坡度的姿态测量。

Result: 最差汇总环境条件仍保留清晰基线前向扇区产量的76.6%，且无扫描完全丢失该扇区；记录到振动导致惯性通道掉线时LiDAR与相机记录仍持续，体现通道互补性；形成有文档的原型与面向可部署轮椅实现的明确验证路径。

Conclusion: AVD提供了一种安全分层、低成本、可验证的电动轮椅遥操作架构，其原型与验证路径为后续可部署实现奠定基础。

Abstract: Electric wheelchairs predominantly rely on manual joystick interfaces, which present critical barriers to independent mobility for individuals with severe motor and visual impairments. This paper presents AVD (Autonomous Vehicle Drive), a dependable teleoperation architecture for assistive mobility that unifies perception, communication, embedded control, and remote supervision in a single low-cost platform built entirely from commercially available hardware. The central engineering contribution is a safety-oriented design framework: control authority follows a static hierarchy, a manual emergency stop and the occupant's joystick pre-empt remote commands, and the on-board sensing layer has stop-only authority. The compute design partitions primary and safety functions and specifies a heartbeat-driven safe-state transition for the fault cases considered. The sensing stack this framework depends on is characterized against measured data: per-channel acquisition latency, a 2,025-scan LiDAR degradation campaign spanning five target configurations and a control under water mist, airborne dust, and direct aperture occlusion, and attitude measurements across static inclines to 47 degrees. The lowest pooled environmental-condition mean retained 76.6% of the clear-baseline forward-sector yield, and no scan lost the sector completely. A logged vibration-induced inertial dropout occurred while LiDAR and camera logging continued, illustrating complementary channel behavior. The result is a documented prototype and an explicit validation path for a deployable wheelchair implementation.

</details>


### [43] [ACiT: Proprioceptive-Prior Action Chunking with Inverted Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20719)
*Haixiang Wu,Zhihao Guan,Yang Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该文提出ACiT，将本体感觉中的历史关节耦合作为动作预测的首要条件，视觉仅用于后续校准，从而提升视觉运动策略的成功率与执行效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉运动策略将历史本体感觉序列与高维视觉等权融合，导致富含动态先验的本体感觉信号被淹没；分析发现强关节间耦合是决定动作的关键结构，且符合本体感觉先形成身体中心先验、视觉随后经顶叶重映射校准的神经机制。

Method: 提出Proprioceptive-Prior Action Chunking with Inverted Transformers（ACiT），包含三个模块：Inverted State Encoding通过逐关节token与关节间自注意力构建本体感觉先验；State-Visual Grounding让关节token查询视觉证据，使视觉仅用于目标与校准；Inverted Action Chunking独立解码逐关节轨迹。

Result: ACiT在LIBERO、RoboTwin 2.0及真实世界多数任务上提升成功率，在需要动态与协调的任务上增益最大；将相同条件设计迁移至VLA主干pi_0-ACiT后获得显著增益并具有更好的执行效率。

Conclusion: 以本体感觉关节耦合为先验、视觉为后置校准的条件设计能更有效利用本体感觉动态信息，并可作为通用设计提升视觉运动策略及VLA模型性能。

Abstract: Visuomotor policies condition action prediction on visual and proprioceptive observations. While historical proprioceptive sequences carry rich dynamic priors, current methods treat them as equal to high-dimensional vision, causing the proprioceptive signal to be overwhelmed. To identify what is lost in equal-conditioning fusion, we analyze proprioceptive data and find that strong inter-joint coupling is the key action-determining structure. This finding aligns with the neural sensorimotor loop, in which proprioception first forms a body-centered prior and vision is later calibrated via parietal remapping. Inspired by this, we propose ACiT, Proprioceptive-Prior Action Chunking with Inverted Transformers, which reinforces historical joint-coupling as the primary condition with vision as a later calibration. This design is instantiated through three modules: Inverted State Encoding builds a proprioceptive prior via per-joint tokens and inter-joint self-attention. State-Visual Grounding lets joint tokens query visual evidence, making vision goal- and calibration-only. Inverted Action Chunking decodes per-joint trajectories independently. ACiT improves success rates on most LIBERO, RoboTwin 2.0, and real-world tasks, with the largest gains on those that require dynamics and coordination. The same conditioning design transferred to the VLA backbone pi_0-ACiT yields significant gains with better execution efficiency.

</details>


### [44] [TaF-Foundation: Wrench-Grounded Cross-Sensor Tactile Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20718)
*Jiaming Jiang,Yuzhe Huang,Baokun Yang,Hao Liang,Li Gao,Haoran Guo,Pei Lin,Shengcheng Luo,Chenxi Xiao,Wanlin Li,Ziyuan Jiao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出以六维力/力矩（wrench）作为跨触觉传感器的共同物理参考，构建超1亿条配对触觉观测与力测量数据集，学习共享触觉表征并用于机器人操作策略TaF-Policy。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 触觉传感机制和观测格式高度异构，导致跨硬件迁移困难；而物理接触受牛顿力学约束，因此可用统一的六维wrench为不同触觉传感器提供共享物理监督。

Method: 搭建专用采集平台，从覆盖四类传感原理的24种传感器收集超1亿条配对触觉观测与wrench测量；框架结合传感器特定输入适配与共享触觉编码器，通过重建wrench将不同传感器观测映射到基于接触物理的共享连续特征空间；TaF-Policy进一步融合触觉特征、视觉观测和机器人状态以生成动作块。

Result: 在核心基准上，单帧力RMSE相对最强基线降低17.1%，跨传感器检索R@1提升13.11个百分点；操作实验中，TaF-Policy在三个仿真任务上平均成功率为51.33%（最强基线47.33%），在四个真实任务上为46.25%（最强基线23.75%）。

Conclusion: 以wrench为共享物理监督可有效学习跨传感器可迁移的触觉表征，并显著提升仿真与真实机器人操作任务的成功率。

Abstract: Tactile sensing is essential for contact-rich manipulation, yet heterogeneous sensing mechanisms and observation formats hinder cross-hardware transfer. Since physical contact is governed by Newtonian mechanics, we ground tactile representations in six-dimensional wrenches, establishing a common physical reference across sensing mechanisms. Specifically, we pair tactile observations from all sensors with wrench measurements, enabling shared physical supervision without requiring direct tactile-to-tactile pairing between sensors. To support learning at scale, we develop a dedicated acquisition platform and collect over 100 million paired tactile observations and wrench measurements from 24 sensors spanning four families of sensing principles. Building on this dataset, our framework combines sensor-specific input adaptation with a shared tactile encoder. By learning to reconstruct wrenches, the encoder maps observations from different sensors into a shared, continuous feature space grounded in contact physics. To leverage these representations for manipulation, we introduce TaF-Policy, which integrates tactile features with visual observations and robot states to generate action chunks. We evaluate the learned representations through wrench estimation, cross-sensor retrieval, few-shot adaptation to unseen sensors, and robotic manipulation. On the core benchmark, our method reduces single-frame force RMSE by 17.1% and improves cross-sensor retrieval R@1 by 13.11 percentage points over the strongest baseline. In manipulation experiments, TaF-Policy achieves average success rates of 51.33% across three simulated tasks and 46.25% across four real-world tasks, compared with 47.33% and 23.75%, respectively, for the strongest baselines evaluated on the same task sets. Project page: this https URL .

</details>


### [45] [SG-ODOM: Event-Driven Proprioceptive State Estimation with Stability-Gated Contact Updates for Legged Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20710)
*Francesco Iotti,Chiyun Noh,Franco Angelini,Manolo Garabini,Ayoung Kim,Maurice Fallon,Frank Dellaert*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SG-ODOM 提出一种基于稳定性门控接触选择的足式机器人本体感知里程计框架，仅在足端达到稳定支撑条件后触发运动学校正，并用超时更新限制 IMU 连续传播时长；在四足和人形机器人上取得有竞争力或更优的精度，GrandTour 户外序列 RPE 最多降低 25%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统本体感知里程计将支撑相足端视为静止参考，但物理触地并不必然提供可靠静止参考，尤其在冲击和载荷转移阶段；同一接触相内重复施加运动学校正会引入冗余或不可靠信息。

Method: 融合 IMU 与腿编码器运动学；在物理触地后通过稳定性门控判断足端是否达到足够稳定的支撑条件，满足后接受该接触并触发估计器校正；同时设置超时更新，以限制无校正 IMU 传播的最大持续时间。

Result: 在多种四足和人形机器人平台上与代表性最先进本体感知估计器对比，SG-ODOM 在评估序列中表现出有竞争力或更优的估计精度，GrandTour 户外序列上相比基线将相对位姿误差 RPE 最多降低 25%。

Conclusion: 通过稳定性门控的接触选择来调度运动学校正，可减少不可靠接触信息影响并提升足式机器人本体感知里程计的可靠性与精度。

Abstract: Reliable proprioceptive state estimation for legged robots fuses inertial sensing with kinematic sensing from leg encoders. The latter assumes a foot is stationary during the stance phase; however, physical foot touchdown does not necessarily provide a stationary reference for odometry, particularly during impact and load transfer. Repeatedly applying kinematic corrections throughout the same contact phase can introduce redundant or unreliable information. We present SG-ODOM, a proprioceptive odometry framework that uses stability-gated contact selection to schedule kinematic updates. Following physical touchdown, a stability gate accepts the contact for state estimation only after the foot reaches a sufficiently stable support condition. The resulting validated touchdown event triggers an estimator correction, while additional timeout updates bound the maximum duration of uninterrupted IMU propagation. The framework is evaluated on multiple legged platforms, both quadrupedal and humanoid robots, against representative state-of-the-art proprioceptive estimators. Across the evaluated sequences, SG-ODOM demonstrates competitive or superior estimation accuracy, reducing Relative Pose Error (RPE) by up to 25% compared to the baseline estimator on outdoor sequences from the GrandTour dataset. Source code: this https URL . Video: this https URL

</details>


### [46] [Design of the Secure Smart Scooter with Automatic Lane Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20700)
*Hamid Reza Ghaeini,Nils Ole Tippenhauer*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该文展示了一款利用计算机视觉识别行驶车道并自动调整车辆配置的电动滑板车原型，并讨论其安全约束与安全防护原型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电动滑板车可在人行道、自行车道或机动车道行驶，而不同车道受政府限速约束；具备计算机视觉的滑板车可自动识别车道并调整配置，但其安全性与信息安全仍令人担忧。

Method: 作者首先介绍车辆平台设置，随后给出带有安全约束的滑板车实现，并展示面向该类电动滑板车的安全防护原型。

Result: 实现了可识别车道并自动修改车辆配置的电动滑板车原型，加入安全约束，并构建了安全防护原型；摘要未提供定量实验结果。

Conclusion: 计算机视觉可用于实现车道感知的电动滑板车自动配置，但此类车辆系统的安全与信息安全仍需进一步研究。

Abstract: The authors present a prototype of an electric scooter that uses computer vision to determine driving lanes and automatically modify vehicle configuration. Scooters steer on sidewalks, bike paths, or car lanes. The government imposes speed limits on the lanes in which electric scooters drive. Featuring computer vision, electric scooters can determine driving lanes and automatically modify vehicle configuration. However, such systems' safety is still a concern, and research into such vehicle systems' safety and security aspects is proceeding. First, we will present our vehicular setup. Then, we will present our implemented scooter with safety constraints. Finally, we present our security prototype for such an electric scooter.

</details>


### [47] [ARMFUL: Learning Whole-Arm Grasp Configurations for Single-Arm Pickup of Large Objects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20692)
*Xinyi Yu,Yunshuang Li,Daniel Seita,Gaurav S. Sukhatme*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出首个大规模单臂整臂抓取数据集WAG，并基于此构建ARMFUL流程，实现对大型物体的整臂包裹式拾取，在仿真和真实Franka机械臂上均取得较高成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 储物箱、篮子、椅子等日常物体常因尺寸或重量过大而无法用夹爪抓取，但单臂可通过环绕支撑完成拾取；此类整臂包裹运动难以遥操作，且现有数据集缺乏整臂拾取数据。

Method: 构建WAG数据集：在关节空间采样1.7百万个关节配置，覆盖793个物体，在仿真中执行挤压与抬升原语，并通过主动抖动和外部力进行压力测试。提出ARMFUL流水线：用WAG训练的扩散模型从物体点云生成整臂抓取，配合运动规划器无碰撞到达抓取位姿，再执行相同的挤压与抬升。

Result: 仿真中ARMFUL在训练物体上达到85.4%抬升成功率，在留出物体上达到69.9%；在真实Franka机械臂上对10个IKEA物体进行100次试验，成功抬升70次。

Conclusion: WAG数据集和ARMFUL流程能够有效学习并执行大型物体的整臂包裹式拾取，并在未见物体和真实环境中表现出良好泛化能力。

Abstract: Many everyday objects, such as storage boxes, baskets, and chairs, are too large or heavy for a gripper to grasp, yet a single arm can still pick them up by wrapping around and supporting them. Such grasps are hard to learn: wrapping motions are difficult to teleoperate, and existing datasets contain little whole-arm pickup data. We introduce WAG (Whole-Arm Grasp Dataset), the first large-scale dataset for single-arm pickup of large objects, with 1.7~million joint configurations across 793 objects. We sample configurations directly in joint space, execute each through squeeze and lift primitives in simulation, and stress-test survivors under active shaking and external forces. To demonstrate the utility of WAG and accomplish the whole-arm pickup task, we develop ARMFUL, a whole-arm pickup pipeline that pairs a diffusion model trained on WAG, generating whole-arm grasps from the object's point cloud, with a motion planner that reaches them collision-free before the same squeeze-and-lift execution. In simulation, ARMFUL reaches 85.4\% lift success on training and 69.9\% on held-out objects, and it lifts the object in 70 of 100 trials on a physical Franka arm across 10 IKEA objects.

</details>


### [48] [Learning and Verifying Progress: Closed-Loop Agentic Planning with Progress-Enhanced Execution for Long-Horizon Vision-Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20676)
*Kangjie Zhou,Sukai Wang,Yuhan Xiao,Qinghang Liu,Zhejia Wen,ChunXuan Jiao,Wei Shan,Guanghui Ren,Maoqing Yao,Chang Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出LeaP-VLN，通过“计划—执行—验证—恢复”闭环，将进度感知执行与独立进度验证结合，提升长时程视觉语言导航的鲁棒性与子任务完成判断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程VLN中执行器易因策略诱导的状态偏移而累积局部错误，且停止信号不能可靠指示子任务完成，因此需要跟踪指令进度并在错误传播前恢复。

Method: LeaP-VLN显式引入进度验证器，在推进前验证子任务完成，并在路线偏离时触发双阶段恢复；隐式地通过联合训练执行器进行进度推理与动作预测来增强进度意识和纠错控制。为适配闭环接口，采用两阶段进度增强课程：先基于专家演示行为克隆，再在演示、专家动作纠正和进度推理监督上联合训练，并利用策略生成轨迹暴露子任务内漂移与子任务间状态偏移。

Result: 在R2R-CE和RxR-CE上，在未使用额外导航数据集训练的单目方法中取得最先进成功率；真实世界实验进一步验证了长时程导航中的有效性。

Conclusion: LeaP-VLN通过闭环进度验证与进度增强训练，有效缓解长时程VLN中的误差传播和不可靠停止问题，并展现出较强的实际部署潜力。

Abstract: Long-horizon vision-language navigation (VLN) requires agents to track instruction progress and recover from local errors before they propagate across subtasks. However, executors trained on expert trajectories encounter policy-induced state shifts during deployment, and their stop signals do not reliably indicate subtask completion. We introduce Learning and Verifying Progress for VLN (LeaP-VLN), an agentic VLN system that couples progress-aware execution with independent progress verification through plan--execute--verify--recover cycles. LeaP-VLN leverages progress both explicitly, through a progress verifier that verifies subtask completion before the agent advances and triggers dual-phase recovery upon route deviation, and implicitly, by co-training the executor on progress reasoning and action prediction to enhance the executor's progress awareness and corrective control. To adapt the executor to this closed-loop interface, we propose a two-stage progress-enhanced curriculum: behavior cloning on expert demonstrations, followed by joint training on demonstrations, expert action corrections, and progress-reasoning supervision. Policy-generated trajectories expose the executor to both intra-subtask drift and inter-subtask state shifts, aligning training with the deviations encountered during closed-loop execution. Extensive experiments on R2R-CE and RxR-CE demonstrate state-of-the-art success rates among monocular methods trained without additional navigation datasets, while real-world experiments further validate the effectiveness of LeaP-VLN in long-horizon navigation.

</details>


### [49] [JEPTERA: A Platform Agnostic JEPA-Assisted Model Predictive Planner for Urban UAV Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20593)
*Gibran Fathoni Belva,Mansur Arief,Ahmad Alfan Alfian Irfan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出JEPTERA城市无人机导航架构，将JEPA、MPC、PID与安全层集成，并在多仿真器中验证可行性与可移植性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 城市无人机导航需同时处理环境几何、车辆动力学和移动障碍物，现有规划需兼顾预测、控制与安全性。

Method: JEPTERA以JEPA预测无人机对候选指令的运动响应，供MPC选择动作；安全层结合占用空间和预测障碍运动检查轨迹；通过仿真器适配器和平台专用YAML配置解耦规划核心与仿真接口。开展6种配置、3个gym场景共180次消融实验，并进行多城市地图与Gazebo/ROS 2、MuJoCo、CARLA集成测试。

Result: 完整架构取得最高总体成功率，30次中完成28次；在三张城市地图上完成一条路线且无碰撞；在Gazebo/ROS 2、MuJoCo和CARLA中无需修改核心规划逻辑即可运行。

Conclusion: 结果表明该集成架构用于仿真城市无人机导航具有可行性，并具备跨仿真器接口的可移植性。

Abstract: Urban navigation for Unmanned Aerial Vehicles (UAVs) requires planning that accounts for environmental geometry, vehicle dynamics, and moving obstacles. This paper presents JEPTERA, a navigation architecture that integrates a Joint-Embedding Predictive Architecture (JEPA), Model Predictive Control (MPC), PID control, and a safety layer. JEPA predicts the UAV's motion response to candidate commands for MPC action selection, while the safety layer checks trajectories against occupied space and predicted obstacle motion. Simulator adapters and platform-specific YAML configurations separate the planning core from simulator interfaces. An ablation study evaluated six configurations across three gym scenarios, totaling 180 runs. The complete architecture achieved the highest aggregate success rate, completing 28 of 30 runs. Additional tests completed one route across three city maps without collisions. Separate integration tests demonstrated operation in Gazebo/ROS 2, MuJoCo, and CARLA without modifying the core planning logic. These results support the feasibility of the integrated architecture for simulated urban UAV navigation and its portability across simulator interfaces.

</details>


### [50] [Stability Principle Inherent in Wheel Gait of Planar X-shaped Walker Generated Using Constant Torque Drive and Mechanical Stoppers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20587)
*Fumihiko Asano,Cong Yan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文通过平面X形带髋关节机械限位器的步行机器人，从理论上解释机械限位约束如何通过分阶段状态误差演化提升下坡恒力矩驱动轮式步态的整体稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管被动动态与极限环步行研究已表明髋、膝关节机械限位器有助于稳定行走，但机械约束如何增强整体步态稳定性的理论研究仍不充分。

Method: 构建带髋关节机械限位器的平面X形步行机器人，在下坡上施加恒力矩驱动生成轮式步态；将单步运动划分为四个阶段，基于线性化模型推导各阶段状态误差转移函数矩阵的近似解析解，并用最大奇异值等指标分析状态误差范数的增减。

Result: 数值仿真表明，四个阶段中有两个阶段在状态误差范数意义下不稳定，另两个阶段稳定，二者共同作用使整体步态具有渐近稳定性；机器人仅靠前倾跌倒并利用限位器约束至目标碰撞姿态，即可生成高度稳定的轮式步态。

Conclusion: 髋关节机械限位器并非简单限制运动，而是通过分阶段误差动态中的稳定与不稳定阶段组合，实现整体渐近稳定，从而为机械约束提升被动动态步态稳定性提供了理论解释。

Abstract: Since the late 19th century when the first walking toys were developed, it has been known that mechanical stoppers at the hip joint are crucial for generating stable passive dynamic walking. Recent research on passive-dynamic and limit-cycle walkers has also confirmed that mechanical stoppers at the hip and knee joints are effective for generating stable walking motion, but theoretical research on how this mechanical constraint enhances the overall gait stability remains insufficient. This paper introduces a planar X-shaped walker equipped with mechanical stoppers at the hip joint, and investigates the effect of the mechanical constraint on the stability of the wheel gait generated by constant torque drive on a downslope. By simply falling forward while using the stoppers to constrain itself to the target impact posture, the robot can generate a highly stable wheel gait. We divide the motion of one step into four phases, derive approximate analytical solutions for the state error transition function matrix in each phase using a linearized model, and analyze the increase or decrease in the state error norm using metrics such as its maximum singular value. Numerical simulations demonstrate that while two phases are unstable in terms of the increase in the state error norm, the remaining two phases are stable, resulting in overall asymptotic stability.

</details>


### [51] [ExtremeDrive: A Real-World Dataset and Benchmark for Critical Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20574)
*Xinhan Li,Yuhong Jiang,Shiyue Zhao,Junzhi Zhang,Chengkun He,Ruihai Ma,Shijie Zhao,Wenxuan Cheng,Jinbo Lv,Tao Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出ExtremeDrive真实世界数据集与基准，覆盖安全关键场景和极端环境，包含589个驾驶场景、多传感器对齐数据与审核标注，并建立基于仿真执行的轨迹评估基准；实验表明加入关键驾驶数据可降低轨迹预测误差并提升关键场景平均成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动驾驶数据集和基准多聚焦常规驾驶，缺乏覆盖安全关键场景与极端环境条件的真实数据，限制相关学习与评估。

Method: 从真实驾驶记录中筛选589个场景，涵盖紧急机动、复杂交通交互、湿滑或积雪道路等，提供校准对齐的多传感器数据和经审核的道路结构及交通参与者标注；构建通过模拟车辆执行规划轨迹的基准，度量安全相关指标与驾驶性能。

Result: 在相同训练集规模下，纳入关键驾驶数据可降低轨迹预测误差，并提高关键场景中的平均关键成功率。

Conclusion: ExtremeDrive为安全关键场景和极端环境下的自动驾驶学习与评估提供统一资源，体现关键驾驶数据对提升轨迹预测与安全性能的价值。

Abstract: Existing autonomous driving datasets and benchmarks provide extensive resources for routine driving, yet real-world data covering safety-critical scenarios and extreme environmental conditions remain limited. To address this gap, we present ExtremeDrive, a real-world dataset and benchmark covering emergency maneuvers, complex traffic interactions, and adverse conditions such as slippery or snow-covered roads, alongside routine driving. The dataset comprises 589 driving scenes curated from a larger pool of real-world driving recordings, with calibrated and aligned multisensor data and reviewed annotations of road structure and traffic participants. We further establish a benchmark to evaluate planned trajectories through simulated vehicle execution, measuring safety-related metrics and driving performance. Experiments at equal training set sizes show that incorporating critical driving data reduces trajectory prediction errors and improves mean critical-success rates in critical scenarios. ExtremeDrive provides a unified resource for learning and evaluating autonomous driving in safety-critical scenarios and extreme environmental conditions.

</details>


### [52] [OrthoMark: Place Recognition with Point-Anchored Orthographic Height Mark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20570)
*Haichuan Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种无需训练、无需3D配准的LiDAR地点识别方法：用BEV极坐标傅里叶谱做平移旋转不变检索，用相位相关估计平面位姿，并以点锚定高度列进行验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LiDAR地点识别为SLAM提供回环。作者指出，重力对齐扫描从Z轴看是场景的正交地图，重访时该地图在平面内刚性移动；极坐标傅里叶谱忽略该运动而相位相关可恢复。垂直方向高度对结构描述关键，但观测到哪些高度依赖距离，因为传感器垂直视场会截断近处结构，因此高度作为全局键会误导，但在两扫描对齐并逐单元格比较重叠区域时具有决定性。

Method: 方法基于一个原则：两个LiDAR扫描只能通过由二者相同几何表面计算出的特征进行比较。检索阶段使用鸟瞰图（BEV）的平移和旋转不变极坐标谱，每关键帧仅1.9 KB；验证阶段通过相位相关估计候选的平面位姿，并用“点锚定高度列”评分：每个BEV单元格保留其自身回波高度的16位掩码；同一会话内，分数按由两扫描距离计算的 sensing range 预期重叠加权。

Result: 在KITTI开发序列上，该方法在无需3D配准的无训练方法中取得最高AP；在多机器人所有序列上，以RING++描述子大小的1/350达到或超过其性能，并在所有测试中比学习的BEVPlace++闭合更多机器人间回环。

Conclusion: 垂直高度信息在验证中有效但在检索中有害，因为高度切片内容随到结构的距离变化；所提方法通过BEV极坐标谱检索与点锚定高度列验证，实现了紧凑、无需训练且跨机器人有效的LiDAR地点识别。

Abstract: LiDAR place recognition provides the loop closures of Simultaneous Localization and Mapping(SLAM). Perspective from Z-axis, a gravity-aligned scan is an orthographic map of the scene, and a revisit from another position and heading moves this map rigidly in the plane, a motion that a polar Fourier spectrum ignores and phase correlation recovers. Along the vertical, the heights of the returns describe the structures, but which heights are observed depends on the range, because the sensor's vertical field of view cuts nearby structures low; height thus misleads a global key, yet is decisive once two scans are aligned and compared cell by cell where they overlap. We present \method, a training-free method built on one principle: two LiDAR scans can only be compared through features computed from the same geometric surfaces in both scans. \method retrieves candidates with a translation- and rotation-invariant polar spectrum of the bird's-eye view (BEV), 1.9,KB per keyframe, and verifies each candidate by estimating its planar pose through phase correlation and scoring it with \emph{point-anchored height columns}: every BEV cell keeps a 16-bit mask of the heights of its own returns; within a session, the score is weighted by the expected overlap of the two scans' sensing ranges, computed from their distance. We show that this vertical link pays off in verification but hurts in retrieval, where what a height slice contains depends on the range to the structure. For single robot, \method has the highest AP of the training-free methods without 3D registration on the KITTI development sequences; across robots, on all multi-robot sequences, it matches or exceeds RING++ at 1/350 of its descriptor size and closes more inter-robot loops than the learned BEVPlace++ on all tests.

</details>


### [53] [sqrtVINS-Based 360 Visual-Inertial SLAM Systems: Hilti-Trimble SLAM Challenge 2026 Entry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20568)
*Jungwoo Lee,Hogyun Kim,Jiwon Choi,Jinyong Jeong,Younggun Cho*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文是2026 Hilti-Trimble SLAM Challenge参赛系统报告，提出仅使用双鱼眼图像流与IMU的360相机视觉惯性SLAM流水线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对长时室内序列中的暗区、重复走廊、动态物体和几何视差不足等挑战，构建可复现的ROS2 SLAM系统。

Method: 先对原始图像进行低光增强与静态/动态掩码预处理，再送入sqrt(VINS)前端作为局部里程计，采用SuperPoint特征、ELSED线段和LightGlue匹配；后端基于GTSAM进行紧耦合因子图优化，结合SALAD全局描述子和视觉BA，执行局部到全局的无结构BA、内外参紧正则化 refined，以及利用已知相机间基线和跨视角约束的全局尺度调整。

Result: 摘要未给出定量指标；Fig. 1显示所提方法在多个室内序列中与楼层平面图对齐良好。

Conclusion: 该系统面向暗区、重复结构、动态干扰和低视差的长时室内场景，并在提供的ROS2工作空间中保持可复现性，展示出与平面图一致的对齐效果。

Abstract: This report describes our submission to the 2026 Hilti-Trimble SLAM Challenge. The system is a 360-camera visual-inertial pipeline that uses only the provided dual-fisheye image streams and IMU measurements. First, raw images are preprocessed with low-light enhancement and static/dynamic masking, and then fed into a $\sqrt{\text{VINS}}$ frontend as the local odometry, with deep learning-based SuperPoint features, ELSED line segments, and LightGlue matching. The resulting trajectory is refined using a GTSAM-based backend that performs tightly-coupled factor graph optimization with SALAD global descriptor and visual bundle adjustment (BA). The backend performs local-to-global structureless BA, tightly regularized intrinsic and extrinsic refinement, and global scale adjustment supported by the known inter-camera baseline and cross-view constraints. The design targets long indoor sequences with dark regions, repetitive corridors, dynamic objects, and limited geometric parallax, while keeping the pipeline reproducible in the provided ROS2 workspace. Fig. 1 demonstrates that our proposed method aligns well with the floorplan across several indoor sequences.

</details>


### [54] [Vision-Language Grounded Semantic Navigation for Autonomous Mobile Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20558)
*Ravindran Vasoor Saravanan,Chittin Varunav Ravi,Madhumita Singh,Thirulok Sundar Mohan Rasu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一个完整ROS2系统，将前沿探索、Grounding DINO开放词汇目标检测、基于深度的3D定位、语义建图与Nav2导航结合，在Gazebo/TurtleBot3仿真中实现端到端视觉语言接地导航，无需任务特定训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言引导移动机器人需在非结构化环境中理解自然语言并导航到用户指定目标，但开放词汇视觉感知与经典机器人建图、定位和导航管线的高效集成仍具挑战。

Method: 构建ROS2系统：使用前沿探索自主建图，利用Grounding DINO根据文本提示从RGB图像检测开放词汇目标，基于深度估计目标在世界坐标系中的3D位置，构建语义地图，并通过Nav2栈执行导航目标；在Gazebo室内环境和TurtleBot3机器人上测试“go to the yellow box”等自然语言指令。

Result: 系统自主探索房间，在5个目标中检测到3个，并成功执行两项自然语言导航命令，且无需任务特定训练；性能受检测不确定性、深度定位误差和探索覆盖不完整限制。

Conclusion: 开放词汇接地模型可集成到经典机器人导航管线中，验证了轻量级CPU-only架构部署视觉语言接地导航的可行性，同时揭示当前在感知、定位和探索方面的局限。

Abstract: Language-guided navigation enables mobile robots to interpret natural language commands and navigate toward user-specified targets in unstructured environments. However, integrating open-vocabulary visual perception with classical robotics pipelines for mapping and navigation remains a challenging problem. In this work, we present a complete ROS2-based system that combines frontier-based exploration, open-vocabulary object detection using Grounding DINO, depth-based 3D localization, and semantic mapping to enable end-to-end vision-language grounded navigation in simulation. The system accepts natural language commands such as ``go to the yellow box,'' detects candidate objects from RGB images using text prompts, estimates their positions in the world frame, and generates navigation goals executed by the Nav2 stack. In a Gazebo-based indoor environment with a TurtleBot3 robot, the system autonomously explored the room, detected three of the five target objects, and successfully executed both natural language navigation commands tested, without task-specific training. The results indicate that open-vocabulary grounding models can be integrated into classical navigation pipelines, though performance is limited by detection uncertainty, depth-based localization error, and incomplete exploration coverage. These findings highlight both the feasibility and current limitations of deploying vision-language grounded navigation systems using lightweight, CPU-only architectures.

</details>


### [55] [AsymEEF: Bimanual Manipulation with Asymmetric End Effectors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20557)
*Xiaokang Sun,Ningyue Wang,Kunqi Xu,Guangqi Jiang,Jianglong Ye,Yong Wu,Xueyan Zou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AsymEEF提出跨末端执行器的双臂演示生成与统一策略学习框架，可将少量源演示迁移到二指、三指、五指等异构末端组合，无需额外遥操作，并支持统一双臂视觉运动策略训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 遥操作演示采集成本高，且因不同末端执行器的运动学与动作空间差异而难以复用；双臂操作中每种末端组合都可能需要重新采集数据，负担更大。

Method: 方法利用运动学教师锚点提供几何监督，并用稀疏真实世界X-Calib锚点校正抓取执行偏差；通过共享编码器将腕部位姿和执行器状态映射到与形态无关的潜空间，再由形态条件解码器重建目标命令；生成轨迹经回放过滤以保证任务级可执行性，同时该表示支持跨多种末端组合的统一双臂视觉运动策略学习。

Result: 在4个仿真和6个真实世界任务中，AsymEEF相较适配迁移基线提升了回放成功率，在仿真中取得平均16.6倍的回放有效数据扩展增益，并在真实机器人上通过下游策略训练进行了评估。

Conclusion: AsymEEF能够以少量源数据实现跨异构末端执行器的可执行双臂演示生成，并复用统一表示进行多末端组合的策略学习，降低双臂遥操作数据采集与复用成本。

Abstract: Teleoperated demonstrations are costly to collect and rarely reusable across end-effectors with different kinematics and action spaces. This burden grows in bimanual manipulation, where each end-effector pair may require new data. We present AsymEEF, a framework for executable cross-end-effector demonstration generation and unified bimanual policy learning. Given a small source dataset, AsymEEF transfers demonstrations to heterogeneous combinations of two-, three-, and five-fingered end-effectors without additional teleoperation. Kinematic teacher anchors provide geometric supervision, while sparse real-world X-Calib anchors correct discrepancies in grasp execution. A shared encoder maps wrist poses and actuator states to a morphology-agnostic latent space, and a morphology-conditioned decoder reconstructs target commands. Generated trajectories undergo replay filtering for task-level executability. The representation also supports a unified bimanual visuomotor policy trained across multiple pairs. Across four simulated and six real-world tasks, AsymEEF improves replay success over adapted transfer baselines, achieves a mean replay-valid expansion gain of 16.6 times in simulation, and is evaluated through downstream policy training on real robots. Project website: this https URL .

</details>


### [56] [WTR-Drive: World-Task Decomposition with Structured Risk Supervision for End-to-End Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20510)
*Haoyu Xing,Shaobing Ding,Guanglei Zhao,Guanhua Zhao,Nawu Peng,Mingyang Hou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 WTR-Drive，一种基于 Drive-JEPA 的规划器，将共享场景估计与候选轨迹条件推理解耦，并在 NAVSIM 上取得优于 Drive-JEPA 的 PDMS/EPDMS。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有候选轨迹条件场景预测缺乏跨候选的共同对象级参考，聚合规划分数也使违规关联的对象与时间不明确，难以诊断候选质量与选择效果之间的差距。

Method: WTR-Drive 使用与候选无关的 World Memory 估计对象状态、地图结构和未来运动，并在每轮细化时重读原始视觉特征；候选反馈仅更新残差 Task 分支且不回写 World Memory，通过候选特定检索和门控视觉反馈引导细化，最终 Task readout 提供候选条件特征用于评分。方法还从已有候选评分 rollout 中导出结构化风险目标，无需额外模拟器调用，针对自车责任碰撞和 TTC 违规编码事件存在性、与共享对象槽的关联及首次违规时间箱。

Result: 在 NAVSIM navtest 划分上，WTR-Drive 在 v1 达到 94.66 PDMS，在 v2 达到 88.24 EPDMS（专家帧顺序评估），分别比已发表 Drive-JEPA 高 0.96 和 0.44 分。候选级分析显示，最佳候选分数较高可与所选轨迹分数较低同时出现。

Conclusion: 结果表明分离共享场景估计与候选条件推理及引入结构化风险目标可提升规划性能，同时揭示了候选质量与选择有效性之间存在差距。

Abstract: Proposal-conditioned scene predictions can lack a common object-level reference across candidate trajectories. Aggregate planning scores also leave the objects and times associated with violations implicit. We present WTR-Drive (World-Task-Risk Driving), a Drive-JEPA-based planner that separates shared scene estimation from proposal-conditioned reasoning. A proposal-independent World Memory estimates object states, map structure, and future motion by re-reading the original visual features at each refinement round. Proposal feedback updates a residual Task branch without writing back to World Memory. This branch guides refinement through candidate-specific retrieval and gated visual feedback. A final Task readout supplies candidate-conditioned features for scoring. We derive structured risk targets from existing candidate-scoring rollouts without additional simulator calls. For at-fault collision and time-to-collision violations, the targets encode event existence, association with shared object slots, and the first violation time bin. On the NAVSIM navtest split, WTR-Drive achieves 94.66 PDMS on v1 and 88.24 EPDMS on v2 (expert-frame sequential evaluation). These scores are 0.96 and 0.44 points above the published Drive-JEPA values, respectively. Candidate-level analysis shows that higher best-candidate scores can coexist with lower selected-trajectory scores, revealing a gap between candidate quality and selection effectiveness.

</details>


### [57] [Lift, See, Act: Hierarchical Robot Policy Pretraining with 3D Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20481)
*Yiyuan Ge,Changxing Ding,Ziyu Hao,Zijie Zheng,Xiangmin Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: LSA 是一个基于人类视频的三阶段机器人策略预训练框架（Lift、See、Act），通过密集 3D 表征学习和显式空间/交互引导提升机器人操作策略的泛化与精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于人类视频预训练机器人策略对泛化至关重要，但无标注视频缺乏显式动作相关表征；现有方法多只利用 3D 基础模型估计的稀疏手部运动轨迹作为监督，忽略了轨迹提取过程中丰富的细粒度信息。

Method: 提出 LSA 分层预训练框架：Lift 阶段将 2D 视频观测提升为密集 3D 表征，自适应地将策略浅层特征与多个 3D 基础模型的中间表征对齐；See 阶段通过双重引导赋予策略显式几何与交互感知，包括深度图重建以理解 3D 空间布局，以及利用手部区域线索显式监督编码器关注任务相关交互热点；Act 阶段基于密集 3D 表征学习与精确空间引导执行机器人操作。

Result: 在多种仿真和真实世界任务上的大量实验表明，LSA 显著优于当前最先进方法。

Conclusion: LSA 通过充分利用 3D 基础模型中间表征并引入深度重建与手部交互线索监督，实现了更鲁棒、准确的机器人操作策略预训练，验证了密集 3D 表征学习在人类视频机器人策略预训练中的有效性。

Abstract: Robot policy pretraining based on human videos is crucial for improving the policy's generalization ability. One main challenge for this task is the lack of explicit action-relevant representations in such unlabeled data. Recent works tend to estimate 3D hand motion trajectories from videos using 3D foundation models (3D FMs). However, they keep solely the sparse trajectories as supervision, ignoring the rich, fine-grained information produced during the trajectory extraction process. To address this issue, we propose LSA, a hierarchical robot policy pretraining framework that includes three phases: Lift, See, and Act. Specifically, the Lift phase elevates 2D video observations into dense 3D representations by adaptively aligning features in the policy's shallow layers with the intermediate representations extracted from multiple 3D FMs. Building upon these lifted representations, the See phase equips the policy with explicit geometric and interactive awareness through dual guidance. It introduces depth-map reconstruction to help the model comprehend 3D spatial layouts and utilizes hand region cues to explicitly supervise the encoder's attention toward task-relevant interaction hotspots. Empowered by the dense 3D representation learning and precise spatial guidance, our policy achieves robust and accurate robotic manipulation in the Act phase. Extensive experiments on diverse simulation and real-world tasks demonstrate that LSA significantly outperforms current state-of-the-art approaches. The project page is available at: this https URL .

</details>


### [58] [ReRead: Wrong Predictions, Useful Representations - Correcting Frozen Robot Policies by Re-reading Their Own Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20465)
*Xu Su,Shunlong Xiao,Qintao Yi,Mingkuan Zhao,Xinmiao Yu,Yizhe Li,Zihan Wang,Bokui Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 ReRead：冻结预训练机器人策略主干，仅用轻量校正器读取当前及前两帧缓存来预测动作残差，证明策略失败常源于动作读出而非表征不足，并在多个任务上显著提升成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练机器人策略在困难任务上失败时，常见做法是重训主干、增加外部视觉编码器或引入记忆模块。作者认为错误预测不等于错误表征：策略可能已保留所需信息，但未能将其正确读出为动作。

Method: ReRead 在主干冻结条件下，让校正器不接收图像，仅读取当前和两个历史缓存并预测动作残差；校正器仅用全部 50 个 RoboTwin 2.0 任务的离线示范训练，仅增加 4.17% 延迟。通过消融与干预验证校正依赖主干自身表征。

Result: 在 11 个最难的 RoboTwin 2.0 任务上，ReRead 将 LingBot-VLA、π0.5 和 Fast-WAM 成功率分别提升 23.45、13.09 和 8.00 个百分点；在全部 50 个任务配对评估中，将 Fast-WAM 平均成功率从 90.73% 提升至 92.66%；在 Flexiv 双臂机器人阶段歧义任务上从 0% 提升至 36%。四种外部视觉编码器均无额外增益，替换为其他轨迹的历史缓存会显著增加动作误差。

Conclusion: 结果表明，校正主要依赖主干自身表征，策略失败更多是读出问题而非表征问题；ReRead 是一种无需重训主干或外接视觉/记忆模块的低成本有效补救方法。

Abstract: When a pretrained robot policy fails on challenging tasks, common remedies include retraining the backbone, adding an external visual encoder, or introducing a memory module. We argue that wrong predictions do not imply wrong representations: a policy can retain the required information yet fail to read it out as the correct action. To test this view, we propose ReRead: with the backbone frozen, a corrector re-reads the current and two previous caches to predict an action residual. The corrector sees no images, is trained solely on offline demonstrations from all 50 RoboTwin 2.0 tasks, and adds only 4.17% latency. Ablations and interventions show that the correction relies on the backbone's own representations: none of four external visual encoders adds further gain, and swapping in past caches from other trajectories sharply increases action error. On the 11 hardest RoboTwin 2.0 tasks, ReRead improves the success rates of LingBot-VLA, $\pi_{0.5}$, and Fast-WAM by 23.45, 13.09, and 8.00 percentage points, respectively. In paired evaluation on all 50 tasks, it raises Fast-WAM's average success rate from 90.73% to 92.66%. On a Flexiv dual-arm robot, it raises success on a phase-ambiguous task from 0% to 36%.

</details>


### [59] [Stochastic Self-Distillation for Flow-Based Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20460)
*Jingru Guo,Alessandra Russo,Joey Bose,Christos Ziakas*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 VLA-SSD，将预训练 flow-matching 动作头自蒸馏为基于布朗路径条件化的随机单步策略，从而降低 on-policy RL 的推理延迟并提升 rollout 多样性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于 flow 的 VLA 在机器人模仿学习中表现良好，但用 on-policy RL 微调受限：GRPO 等组式方法每个 rollout 步骤需多步 ODE 积分，推理延迟高；且采样器随机性有限，导致 rollout 组方差低。

Method: VLA-SSD 通过将预训练 flow-matching 动作头自蒸馏为随机单步策略：以布朗路径为条件训练单跳生成器，并推导条件动作密度，使策略可用似然比类 RL 算法进行高效后训练。

Result: 从 π0 VLA 蒸馏得到 VLA-SSD 后，动作头前向耗时降低 7.7 倍，但 LIBERO 成功率相对下降 5.5%。利用 VLA-SSD 在组生成中的更高样本多样性，对 π0.5 VLA 使用 GRPO 后训练，获得 20.6% 的相对提升。

Conclusion: VLA-SSD 以少量成功率代价显著降低动作头推理延迟并增强采样多样性，可作为提升组式 on-policy RL 后训练效率与效果的单步随机策略方案。

Abstract: Flow-based vision-language-action (VLA) models achieve strong performance in robotics through imitation learning, yet fine-tuning them with on-policy reinforcement learning (RL) is limited. In group-based RL methods like Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO), every rollout step requires multi-step ODE integration, and the sampler's limited stochasticity leads to rollout groups with low variance. We propose VLA-SSD, which addresses both high inference latency and limited stochasticity. In particular, our method self-distills a pretrained flow-matching action head into a stochastic one-step policy by conditioning a single-jump generator on a Brownian path. We derive a conditional action density via our stochastic one-step policy, enabling efficient post-training via likelihood-ratio RL algorithms. We show that distilling VLA-SSD from the $\pi_0$ VLA reduces the action head's forward-pass time by a factor of 7.7, at the cost of a 5.5% relative decrease in the success rate on the LIBERO benchmark. However, by leveraging the increased sample diversity of VLA-SSD during group generation, post-training a $\pi_{0.5}$ VLA using GRPO yields a 20.6% relative improvement.

</details>


### [60] [XMimic and XSoma: Learning Humanoid Motion Tracking and Locomotion in Under a Minute](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20452)
*Nico Bohlinger,Jan Peters*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 XMimic 与 XSoma，在不到一分钟训练内实现人形机器人运动跟踪与崎岖地形行走，通过 JAX 编译完整训练循环并利用多 GPU 并行缩短 SuperMimic 的串行计算链。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 SuperMimic 实现每次训练迭代由大量串行的小型 GPU 操作组成，形成长计算链，无法有效利用更大规模 GPU，训练效率受限。

Method: 两者基于 SuperMimic 的机器人世界模型与短 rollout 解析梯度优化策略；将完整训练循环（含 MuJoCo Warp 仿真器）用 JAX 编译，缩短计算链，并在最多四块 GPU 上并行执行每步；XSoma 仅将机器人动力学交给世界模型，其余包括脚下地形高度均精确计算，采用一秒预测时域且无需 critic。

Result: 在四块 H100 上，XMimic 用 0.86 分钟学会 Unitree G1 跟踪全部 40 个 LAFAN1 片段，比单 GPU 的 BlinkMimic 快 3.7 倍；XSoma 用 0.77 分钟达到 Holosoma 为 FastSAC 设定的跟踪目标，比单 GPU FastSAC 快 12.3 倍，且仅需其八分之一环境步数。

Conclusion: 通过端到端 JAX 编译、多 GPU 并行和精确计算非动力学部分，可显著加速人形机器人运动跟踪与崎岖地形行走学习，并在极少训练时间和环境步数下达到目标性能。

Abstract: We present XMimic and XSoma, which learn humanoid motion tracking and locomotion on rough terrain in under a minute of training. Both follow SuperMimic, which learns a world model of the robot and optimizes the policy with analytic gradients through short rollouts imagined in this model. Existing implementations of this method do not make use of larger GPUs, as every training iteration is a long chain of small GPU operations that run one after another. XMimic and XSoma therefore shorten this chain and run the steps of every iteration at the same time on up to four GPUs. We compile the complete training loop with JAX, including the MuJoCo Warp simulator. For locomotion, XSoma leaves only the dynamics of the robot to the world model and computes everything else exactly, including the terrain height under the feet. It learns with a horizon of one second and without a critic. On four H100 GPUs, XMimic learns to track all 40 LAFAN1 clips with the Unitree G1 in 0.86 minutes, 3.7 times faster than BlinkMimic on one GPU. XSoma reaches the tracking target that Holosoma's tests set for FastSAC in 0.77 minutes, 12.3 times faster than FastSAC on one GPU, while needing only an eighth of its environment steps.

</details>


### [61] [Making Koopman Control Guarantees Hold on Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20402)
*Abulikemu Abuduweili,Changliu Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文揭示标准Koopman控制器可能只稳定潜空间线性模型却无法稳定非线性对象，并提出通过嵌入切空间一阶匹配保证稳定迁移的TIK方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Koopman控制器在部署时会持续重编码实测物理状态，因此潜空间闭环稳定并不等价于原非线性系统稳定；需要明确稳定性迁移条件，并设计满足该条件的控制器。

Method: 作者证明：当学习闭环与真实闭环在目标点嵌入切空间上一阶一致时，名义闭环稳定性可迁移到非线性对象，并给出近似匹配的鲁棒裕度；同时指出稳定开环潜模型不能精确匹配不稳定平衡点。提出的Tangent-Invariant Koopman（TIK）利用离线转移估计局部对象线性化并强制一阶匹配，在目标点恢复拟合LQR增益，并用双线性预测器刻画大机动中的状态相关驱动。

Result: 在四个不稳定仿真基准中，40个标准Koopman控制器均具有稳定的潜空间闭环，但部署后的非线性系统局部不稳定；40个TIK控制器均能局部稳定对应非线性对象。基于经验曲率的离线稳定性测试可在两个cart-pole系统上识别稳定TIK控制器，但高噪声下结论不确定。在显式模型误差与曲率界下，不变终端集保证进入后局部反馈满足约束。TIK在0.25 ms规划预算下实现88%的约束cart-pole swing-up成功率，并以每步1-5 ms控制仿真七关节机械臂。

Conclusion: 潜空间闭环稳定不足以保证Koopman控制器在原非线性系统上的稳定性；切空间一阶匹配是稳定迁移的关键，TIK据此弥补标准Koopman控制器的部署稳定性差距，并在不稳定基准、约束摆起和七关节机械臂控制中展现出有效性与实时性。

Abstract: A Koopman controller can stabilize its learned latent-space linear model yet fail to stabilize the nonlinear plant. The learned model propagates predictions in latent space, while the deployed controller repeatedly re-encodes the measured physical state. We show that nominal closed-loop stability transfers to the nonlinear plant when the learned and true closed-loop dynamics agree to first order on the embedding's tangent space at the target. We derive a robustness margin for approximate matching and show that a stable open-loop latent model cannot exactly match an unstable equilibrium. We introduce Tangent-Invariant Koopman (TIK), which estimates a local plant linearization from offline transitions and enforces first-order matching to this estimate. TIK recovers the fitted LQR gain at the target, while a bilinear predictor captures state-dependent actuation during large maneuvers. Across four simulated unstable benchmarks, all 40 Standard Koopman controllers have stable latent closed loops, but their deployed nonlinear systems are locally unstable. In contrast, all 40 TIK controllers locally stabilize their respective nonlinear plants. An offline stability test using empirical curvature estimates identifies stable TIK controllers on two cart-pole systems, but is inconclusive under high noise. Under explicit model-error and curvature bounds, an invariant terminal set guarantees constraint satisfaction after entry under local feedback. TIK achieves 88\% constrained cart-pole swing-up success with a 0.25 ms planning budget and controls simulated seven-joint arms within 1-5 ms per step.

</details>


### [62] [High-Speed Forward and Reverse Tractor-Trailer Driving with Learned Vehicle Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20324)
*Aaron Chen,David Shen,Taekyung Kim,Kaleb Ben Naveed,Dimitra Panagou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该文提出用于拖拉机-挂车控制的 learned dynamics model，并将其嵌入 MPPI 控制器，与基于 Fiala 轮胎力的降阶解析模型 MPPI 及三种基线控制器进行闭环跟踪对比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拖拉机-挂车系统难以控制，倒车时尤其不稳定，且动力学受路面摩擦、悬架系数等难以测量因素影响；因此需要能应对这些不确定性的控制导向动力学模型。

Method: 建立学习式动力学模型，并与采用 Fiala 轮胎力的降阶解析动力学模型作为解析基线比较；控制器层面比较使用学习模型的 MPPI、使用解析模型的 MPPI，以及三种额外基线控制器；在四个仿真环境中进行闭环跟踪实验。

Result: 在主要闭环跟踪实验中，使用学习动力学模型的 MPPI 在所有测试设定点上的平均速度更接近目标值，且 RMS 铰接角低于解析模型 MPPI；在八种倒车配置中的七种取得更高完成率。

Conclusion: 学习式动力学模型结合 MPPI 可提升拖拉机-挂车系统的闭环跟踪精度与倒车任务完成率，优于基于降阶解析模型的 MPPI 及基线控制器；结果基于仿真实验。

Abstract: Tractor-trailer systems are difficult to control, especially unstable during reverse motion, and their dynamics depend on difficult-to-measure factors, including road friction and suspension coefficients. We introduce a learned dynamics model for tractor-trailer control and compare it with a reduced-order dynamics model with Fiala tire forces as an analytic baseline. Specifically, we compare a model predictive path integral (MPPI) controller that uses the learned dynamics model to an MPPI controller that uses the analytic model, as well as to three additional baseline controllers. In the main closed-loop tracking experiments across four simulated environments, MPPI with learned dynamics achieves mean speeds closer to the target magnitudes and lower root-mean-square (RMS) hitch angles than MPPI with the analytic model at every tested setpoint, with higher completion rates in seven of eight reverse configurations.

</details>


### [63] [NF-VLA: A Normalizing-Flow Baseline for Vision-Language-Action Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20314)
*Ruichen Deng,Qinyu Zhao,Yong-Hyun Park,Mutian Tong,Liang Zheng,Jiatao Gu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: NF-VLA 将条件自回归归一化流与基于分数的动作精炼结合，用单一最大似然目标训练 VLA 策略，在 LIBERO 上取得 98.35% 成功率，并在 LIBERO-Plus 上以同一检查点达到 72.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归归一化流可为连续机器人动作提供可处理密度，但需验证单一最大似然目标能否训练出与领先方法竞争的视觉-语言-动作（VLA）策略，并统一动作生成与推理精炼。

Method: 提出 NF-VLA：采用深-浅架构，将视觉、语言和本体感受前缀条件集中于高容量深层块，浅层无条件块提供共享可逆动作变换；视觉-语言骨干与流在 Gaussian-smoothed action chunks 上通过精确最大似然联合训练。推理时利用同一学习密度进行单次基于分数的校正，以补偿训练时平滑，无需单独训练去噪器。

Result: 从预训练视觉-语言骨干出发、无机器人预训练，在 LIBERO 四个标准套件上达到 98.35% 成功率；同一检查点不额外微调在 LIBERO-Plus 上达到 72.6%。消融显示仅深层前缀条件化和基于分数的精炼均提升闭环成功率。

Conclusion: 结构化自回归流可作为有竞争力的 VLA 控制方法；单一学习密度同时支持策略训练、动作生成与推理精炼。

Abstract: Autoregressive normalizing flows (NFs) provide a tractable density for learning and sampling continuous robot actions. We show that a single maximum-likelihood objective can train vision--language--action (VLA) policies competitive with leading methods on manipulation benchmarks. We present NF-VLA, which combines conditional autoregressive normalizing flows with score-based action refinement. Its deep--shallow architecture concentrates visual, language, and proprioceptive prefix conditioning in a high-capacity deep block, while unconditioned shallow blocks provide a shared invertible action transformation. The vision--language backbone and flow are jointly trained by exact maximum likelihood on Gaussian-smoothed action chunks. The same learned density supplies a single score-based correction at inference, refining generated actions to account for training-time smoothing without a separately trained denoiser. Trained on LIBERO from a pretrained vision--language backbone without robot pretraining, NF-VLA achieves 98.35\% success across the four standard suites and 72.6\% on LIBERO-Plus using the same checkpoint without further fine-tuning. Ablations show that deep-only prefix conditioning and score-based refinement both improve closed-loop success. Together, these results establish carefully structured autoregressive flows as a competitive approach to VLA control, with one learned density supporting policy training, action generation, and refinement.

</details>


### [64] [Ontology-Compiled Control Barrier Functions for Heterogeneous Robot Teams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20306)
*Peng Wu,Seyede Fatemeh Ghoreishi,Nathan Bastian,Mohsen Imani,Tian Lan,Mahdi Imani*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: OntoCBF 将机器人领域本体（类分类、类型化关系、安全公理及各类动力学配置）编译为每智能体一个二次规划的控制屏障函数安全过滤器，用描述逻辑在线推导异构团队约束，并分析制动能力复用、冲突导致的安全违例及策略学习效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异构机器人团队的安全过滤器常因约束缺失、制动能力高估以及个体可满足约束之间的冲突而失效；尤其面对未见过的机器人类别、载荷类别和组合时，手工为设计类编写的过滤器难以泛化。

Method: 构建带类分类、类型化关系、安全公理和每类机器人动力学配置的领域本体，并将其编译为每个智能体一个 QP 的 CBF 过滤器；通过描述逻辑蕴涵在线生成团队约束，包括关系链和按携带物定义的类所导出的约束。证明子类在能产生父类所有速度时可复用父类制动能力，该性质独立于 subsumption；对 box-bounded double integrator 该性质也是必要的，且每次安全丧失始于冲突。进一步分析三机器人交互时成对屏障安全集仍含不可避免碰撞状态，并在更大团队和固定能力削减下持续存在。

Result: 在人类忽略机器人的仿真仓库中，为设计类编写的过滤器在含未见类的 89–100% 回合中违反安全；OntoCBF 在所有场景（含 32 机器人）中安全违例率为 20–70%，且每次均发生在冲突之后，最深侵入中位数为 keep-out 距离的 10–17%。以过滤器在环训练的 MAPPO 策略将不可行 QP 比例（几乎均为冲突）降低 18–41%。

Conclusion: OntoCBF 通过本体与描述逻辑提升了异构团队安全约束的在线覆盖和跨类别泛化，并明确了制动能力复用条件及冲突引发的固有限制；实验表明其违例主要源于多智能体约束冲突而非约束缺失，且与学习策略结合可减少冲突型不可行 QP。

Abstract: Safety filters for heterogeneous robot teams fail because of missing constraints, overestimated braking capabilities and conflicts between individually satisfiable constraints. We present OntoCBF, which compiles a domain ontology with a class taxonomy, typed relations, safety axioms and a dynamics profile per robot class into a control barrier function filter with one quadratic program per agent. Description-logic entailment supplies the constraints of any team online, including those that follow from chains of relations and from classes defined by what a robot carries, so the existing safety axioms cover new robot classes, payload classes and combinations. We prove that a subclass may reuse the braking capability of its parent whenever it can produce every velocity of the parent, a property separate from subsumption. For box-bounded double integrators this property is also necessary, and every loss of safety then begins with a conflict. Once three robots interact, the safe set of pairwise barriers contains states from which a collision is unavoidable, and such states persist in larger teams under any fixed capability reduction. In a simulated warehouse where humans ignore the robots, a filter written for the design classes violates safety in 89-100 % of the episodes with unseen classes. In all scenarios, including one with 32 robots, OntoCBF violates safety in 20-70 % of the episodes, each time after a conflict, with a median deepest intrusion of 10-17 % of the keep-out distance. A multi-agent PPO policy trained with the filter in the loop lowers the rate of infeasible quadratic programs, nearly all of them conflicts, by 18-41 %.

</details>


### [65] [DexFormer 2.0: Towards a Cross-Embodiment Foundation Policy for Dexterous Grasping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20273)
*Lixin Xu,Ke Zhang,Xinyu Liu,Jie Tan,Senming Tan,Jiaju Yin,Yating Feng,Xiaoyi Lin,Xiyue Dong,Renjing Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DexFormer 2.0 提出单一强化学习策略，实现跨机械臂-灵巧手组合的通用抓取，并可零样本迁移到未见过的臂手配对、新手型和夹爪。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统灵巧抓取策略通常针对单一机械臂与灵巧手配对训练，新增配对就需新策略，策略数量随臂和手数量增长；不同机器人关节数量、布局和动力学不同，观测与动作缺乏统一接口，跨实体迁移困难。

Method: 将每个机器人映射到带有效性掩码的规范关节接口；用受运动学树偏置的注意力编码各关节形态；用 Transformer-XL 记忆从近期交互推断实体相关动力学；在4种机械臂与6种灵巧手组成的18种组合上训练单一强化学习策略。

Result: 在已见组合上达到89%稳定成功率，在6个留出臂手配对上达到51%，优于GRU和DexFormer 1.0基线；无需微调即可抓取4种新手型，并可通过重定向用于平行夹爪；消融表明记忆、运动学树偏置和更广配对覆盖均有助于迁移；在物理Flexiv机械臂上，蒸馏学生策略可从仿真零样本迁移到两种手，包括一个留出配对。

Conclusion: 单一策略能够控制多种臂手组合，并迁移到未见配对和新手型；统一关节接口、形态编码、运动学树偏置和记忆机制是实现跨实体灵巧抓取泛化的关键。

Abstract: Dexterous grasping policies are usually trained for one arm paired with one hand. Every new pairing then needs its own policy, and the number of policies grows with every arm and hand added. We ask whether a single policy can control many arm-hand combinations and transfer to pairings and hands it has never seen. This is hard because robots differ in the number, layout, and dynamics of their joints, so their observations and actions share no common interface. We present DexFormer 2.0, a single reinforcement learning policy for cross-embodiment dexterous grasping. It maps every robot to a canonical joint interface with validity masks, encodes each joint's morphology with attention biased by the kinematic tree, and uses a Transformer-XL memory to infer embodiment-dependent dynamics from recent interaction. Trained on 18 combinations of four arms and six hands, it reaches 89% stable success on seen combinations and 51% on six held-out arm-hand pairs, outperforming GRU and DexFormer 1.0 baselines. Without fine-tuning, it also grasps with four new hand types and, through retargeting, with parallel grippers. Ablations show that memory, the kinematic-tree bias, and broader pairing coverage each matter for transfer. On a physical Flexiv arm, distilled students transfer zero-shot from simulation with two hands, including a held-out pair. Project website: this https URL .

</details>


### [66] [Right Goal, Wrong Body: Re-grounding Frozen VLAs under Actuation Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20244)
*Umer Siddique,Peilang Li,Yongcan Cao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ReGround 是一种推理时方法，在机器人执行机构发生变化时保持 VLA 模型冻结，并重新校准其预期运动，从而恢复任务成功率与安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA 模型在理想设置下可产生期望机器人行为，但部署时重装、机械磨损或指令延迟变化等执行机构偏移会导致任务失败和不安全行为；需要无需重训练地应对不可避免的执行偏移。

Method: ReGround 通过任务无关的电机自发运动学习部署机器人的动力学，并在检测到偏移时激活；它跟踪策略的预期运动，同时保持接近策略自身计划，无需奖励、示范或再训练。

Result: 在两个 VLA、八个 LIBERO 任务和五种偏移设置下，ReGround 可恢复接近 oracle 的成功率；例如在 400 ms 延迟下将 π0.5 的成功率从 0.16 提升到 0.90，且比线性校准基线更安全。

Conclusion: ReGround 能在不修改或重训练 VLA 的情况下有效应对执行机构偏移，提升部署鲁棒性与安全性，并提供项目网站和开源代码。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models are capable of producing desired robotic behaviors in ideal settings. However, they often fail when the robot's actuation changes at deployment. For example, remounting, mechanical wear, or command latency changes can lead to failed tasks and unsafe behaviors. To address the inevitable actuation shift, we propose ReGround, an inference-time method that keeps the VLA frozen and re-grounds its intended motion. ReGround learns the deployed robot's dynamics from task-agnostic motor babbling, and then tracks the policy's intended motion while staying close to the policy's own plan. ReGround activates only upon detecting a shift, and needs no rewards, demonstrations, or retraining. We show via two VLAs, eight LIBERO tasks, and five shift settings that ReGround can restore near-oracle success, such as raising $\pi_{0.5}$'s success from 0.16 to 0.90 under 400 ms latency, while remaining safer than a linear-calibration baseline. Project website and open-source code: this https URL .

</details>


### [67] [DAPPT: Domain-Aware Policies from Procedural Trajectories for Sim-to-Real Dexterous Tool Use](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20202)
*Beining Han,Jia Deng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DAPPT 是一个面向灵巧工具使用操作的 sim-to-real 强化学习框架：不仿真非刚性材料，通过领域参数化程序轨迹学习并蒸馏端到端域感知传感器运动策略，在六项任务上实现 1.5–3.8 倍指标提升并零样本迁移到真实世界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 sim-to-real 灵巧操作 RL 依赖精确刚体仿真，难以覆盖变形体与颗粒材料；直接仿真这些材料速度过慢，无法支撑大规模 RL 学习。

Method: DAPPT 学习域感知 RL 策略以跟踪由领域参数参数化的程序化轨迹，并将其蒸馏为端到端域感知传感器运动策略；显式领域表示支持快速选择使下游任务性能最大的领域参数，且无需仿真非刚性材料。

Result: 在沙铲取、橡皮泥棒滚压、刮沙、鼠标滚轮滚动、分配器按压和喷雾触发六项灵巧操作任务上端到端评估；通过最佳领域参数取得 1.5–3.8 倍任务指标提升，并零样本迁移到真实世界。

Conclusion: DAPPT 为涉及变形体和颗粒的灵巧工具使用操作提供可行的 sim-to-real 方案，避免缓慢的非刚性仿真，并实现真实世界零样本部署。

Abstract: Sim-to-real RL has achieved impressive results in dexterous manipulation but remains limited to tasks with accurate rigid-body simulation. However, these methods cannot be applied to skills that involve deformable and particles, with which the simulation speed will become too slow for large-scale RL learning. We present DAPPT, a sim-to-real framework for end-to-end dexterous tool-use manipulation skills, e.g., sand scooping, play-dough rod-rolling, without simulating non-rigid materials. Our method learns a domain-aware RL policy by tracking procedural trajectories parameterized by domain parameters, and distills it into an end-to-end domain-aware sensorimotor policy. Furthermore, explicit domain represen-tation enables fast selection of domain parameters that can maximize downstream task performance. We evaluate DAPPT end-to-end on six dexterous manipulation tasks, including sand scooping, play-dough rod-rolling, sand scraping, mouse-wheel scrolling, dispenser pressing, and spray triggering. Our method achieves 1.5-3.8x in task metrics through the best domain parameter and transfers zero-shot to the real world.

</details>


### [68] [RoboKit: Accelerating Parallel Differentiable Optimization for Robot Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20185)
*Jianglong Ye,Xuanbin Peng,Conor Mc Gartoll,Changwei Jing,Lai Wei,Jiali Chen,Xueyan Zou,Xiaolong Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RoboKit 是一个高性能、通用、并行的可微优化工具包，面向机器人任务，统一支持多种子 GPU 并行求解、几何/渲染/物理仿真以及与学习方法的集成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人优化通常依赖任务专用实现，难以兼顾性能、灵活性、几何/物理仿真与学习集成；作者希望提供一个通用工具包来加速并统一这些能力。

Method: 基于三项原则构建：(i) GPU 并行多种子求解器，将采样与多种优化器结合；(ii) 使用混合及由粗到细表示加速碰撞检测、可微渲染与仿真；(iii) 代价函数可微且仿真并行，从而支持将优化蒸馏为策略并训练强化学习。

Result: 在七个任务上比任务专用基线快 2–60 倍；可在 2.97 秒内优化 4096 个抓取；蒸馏策略在相近精度下快 9–240 倍；在同一仿真器上，RL 达到 GPU 基线分数所需时间最多缩短 2.1 倍。

Conclusion: RoboKit 通过高性能并行可微优化统一了几何、物理仿真与学习，为机器人优化和策略学习提供了高效通用的一体化工具。

Abstract: We present RoboKit, a high-performance, general-purpose toolkit for parallel differentiable optimization in robotics, built on three principles: (i) Performance and flexibility: GPU-parallel multi-seed solvers pair sampling with versatile optimizers, running 2-60$\times$ faster than task-specific baselines across seven tasks; (ii) Geometry, rendering and physics: hybrid and coarse-to-fine representations accelerate collision, differentiable rendering and simulation, optimizing 4096 grasps in 2.97 s; (iii) Integration with learning: costs are differentiable and simulation is parallel, so RoboKit both distills optimization into policies and trains RL; distilled policies run 9-240$\times$ faster at comparable accuracy, and RL reaches a GPU baseline's scores up to 2.1$\times$ sooner on the same simulator.

</details>


### [69] [Cleanly-DINO: Efficiently Learning Visual Task Spaces for Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20137)
*Adrian Röfer,Melika Shekarriz,José Arce y de la Borbolla,Iman Nematollahi,Abhinav Valada*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Cleanly-DINO 从少量示范中学习清洁任务的视觉任务空间，利用任务相关区域掩码和冻结 ViT 特征拟合投影与进度度量，并验证其在跨域迁移、强化学习奖励/观测编码及真实机器人擦拭中的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多操作任务可用物体位姿等抽象描述，但清洁等任务由视觉状态变化定义，任务表示不明确；因此需要从少量示范中学习能捕捉任务完成相关变化的视觉任务空间。

Method: 给定任务相关区域的掩码和冻结的 ViT 特征，Cleanly-DINO 拟合一个投影以隔离任务空间，并在该空间上拟合进度度量；模型在多样化清洁示例上训练。

Result: 摘要报告了模型能够定位未完成任务区域并跨视觉域迁移，还被用作强化学习中的观测编码器和奖励信号，并部署在真实机器人擦拭流程中评估其引导任务执行的效果；未给出具体定量结果。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，基于冻结 ViT 特征学习视觉任务空间可为清洁类任务提供有效的进度表示，并具备跨域迁移和机器人学习/执行应用潜力。

Abstract: Learning manipulation tasks from few demonstrations requires abstractions that capture the changes relevant to task completion, such as object poses in rigid-body manipulation. However, tasks such as cleaning are defined by changes in visual state rather than object pose, making the choice of task representation less clear. In this work, we propose Cleanly-DINO to learn visual task spaces from few demonstrations. Given a mask of a task-relevant region and frozen ViT features, Cleanly-DINO fits a projection to isolate a task space and fit a progression metric to it. We train the model on diverse cleaning examples and evaluate its ability to localize incomplete task regions and transfer across visual domains. We also study its utility for robot learning by using it as an observation encoder and reward signal in a reinforcement learning setting. Finally, we deploy our fitted model within a real-world robotic wiping pipeline to evaluate its use in guiding task execution.

</details>


### [70] [SAFER: Navigating the Exploration-Exploitation Dilemma in World-Model-Based Planning for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20107)
*Yansong Qu,Zilin Huang,Zihao Sheng,Sikai Chen,Samuel Labi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SAFER 是一个结合离线失败学习与在线自我改进的自动驾驶规划框架，利用专家标注的失败数据和分块潜在世界模型进行实时风险感知规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶需要预见罕见且时间关键的碰撞后果，但世界模型仅在训练覆盖范围内可靠，形成探索-利用困境：覆盖内规划难以超越数据，覆盖外模型误差会误导规划；正常驾驶日志缺少失败样本，交互学习又需自身失败且缺少正确示范。

Method: SAFER 离线使用部分训练智能体和脚本化可恢复扰动在仿真中触发失败，并由专家为访问状态标注动作与价值；规划时用分块潜在世界模型快速预测后果，并用风险感知采样规划器以回报减去有界碰撞惩罚评估候选机动；在线阶段持续在自身驾驶超出离线数据覆盖处精炼模型。

Result: 闭环评测中，SAFER 在 CARLA Leaderboard 路线上取得最高路线完成率和每步奖励，基于特权鸟瞰视图分别为 0.95 和 0.77；在 MetaDrive 中取得最高回合奖励，常规交通 53.4，安全关键交通 49.0。

Conclusion: SAFER 通过离线借鉴他人失败并经专家评判、在线从自身驾驶持续学习，缓解世界模型覆盖不足与探索-利用矛盾，在闭环自动驾驶评测中优于对比方法。

Abstract: An autonomous vehicle must foresee the consequences of its actions, especially in the rare, time-critical situations where crashes happen. A world model offers this foresight, but only within the coverage of its training data, which poses an exploration-exploitation dilemma. Within this coverage a planner can hardly improve on the data, and beyond it the model's errors mislead it. Logs of competent driving rarely contain the failures that would widen the coverage, and agents that learn from interaction gain them only by failing themselves, without being shown what to do instead. We propose SAFER, a framework that learns offline from failures that others make, judged by an expert, and keeps learning online from its own driving. Offline, partially trained agents and scripted, recoverable perturbations Stage failures in simulation, and an expert Annotates every visited state with its action and value, so that much of what the planner explores is covered before it drives. In planning, a chunked latent world model Foresees the consequences fast enough for real-time use, and a risk-aware sampling-based planner Evaluates candidate maneuvers by their return minus a bounded collision penalty. Online, a loop continuously Refines the model wherever this driving leaves the coverage of the offline data. In closed-loop evaluation against the compared methods, SAFER reaches the highest route completion and reward per step on the CARLA Leaderboard routes, 0.95 and 0.77 from a privileged bird's-eye view, and the highest episode reward in MetaDrive, 53.4 in regular and 49.0 in safety-critical traffic. Project page: this https URL .

</details>


### [71] [EgoSwarm: Learning Decentralized Robot Swarm Intelligence from Human Swarm Demonstrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20100)
*Ziqiang Liu,Xitong Chen,Min Li,Yiqun Li,Zhuoping Yin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: EgoSwarm 是首个从人类群体第一视角演示中学习去中心化多机器人协作的机器人学习框架，通过 SIP/SIT 恢复跨智能体交互并转化为局部监督，提出 SwarmVLA 在局部观测、历史与有限邻居消息条件下学习群体行为，在三类多机器人协作任务上平均成功率达 72.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从个体具身智能迈向群体具身智能需要多智能体分工、交接与共享物体协作；现有机器人学习方法主要依赖个体演示，难以监督智能体动作间依赖，而大规模采集多机器人演示困难。人类第一视角群体演示可扩展，但多参与者的分布式观测无法直接监督去中心化策略。

Method: EgoSwarm 使用无机器人多人物第一视角系统采集超过 50 小时协作经验；提出 Swarm Interaction Packet (SIP) 与 Swarm Interaction Token (SIT)，从分布式观测中恢复跨智能体交互关系，并将其转化为符合部署时信息约束的局部监督；进一步提出 SwarmVLA，将群体行为知识迁移到以局部观测、局部历史和有限邻居消息为条件的去中心化策略。

Result: SwarmVLA 在三类多机器人协作任务上取得 72.2% 的平均成功率。

Conclusion: 该工作首次实现从人类群体演示学习去中心化多机器人协作，为可扩展的群体具身智能提供了数据采集、交互监督转化与策略学习框架。

Abstract: Advancing from individual embodied intelligence to swarm embodied intelligence is a key step toward scalable, general-purpose embodied intelligence. Complex tasks often require multiple agents to divide responsibilities, coordinate handovers, and manipulate shared objects collaboratively. However, existing robot learning methods rely primarily on individual demonstrations, which provide limited supervision for modeling dependencies among agent actions, while collecting multi-robot demonstrations at scale remains difficult. Human egocentric demonstrations offer a scalable alternative, yet distributed observations from multiple participants cannot directly supervise decentralized policies. We introduce EgoSwarm, the first robot learning framework for learning decentralized multi-robot collaboration from human swarm demonstrations. EgoSwarm collects more than 50 hours of collaborative experience using a robot-free multi-person egocentric system. We introduce the Swarm Interaction Packet (SIP) and Swarm Interaction Token (SIT) to recover cross-agent interaction relations from distributed observations and transform this experience into local supervision consistent with deployment-time information constraints. We further propose SwarmVLA, which transfers knowledge of swarm behavior to decentralized policies conditioned on local observations, local history, and limited neighbor messages. SwarmVLA achieves a mean success rate of 72.2% across three categories of multi-robot collaborative tasks. Project page: this https URL .

</details>


### [72] [Prompt Re-Specification and Target-Mask Availability in Mask-Conditioned Visual Reinforcement Learning for Moving-Platform Landing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20075)
*Ho Tim Tsui,Jongyun Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该工作研究基于二值目标掩码的视觉强化学习无人机着陆策略在目标外观变化时的鲁棒性，并检验仅通过修改文本提示适配 SAM 3 检测器、保持策略权重不变能否恢复性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉RL着陆策略容易过拟合训练中着陆垫的外观；二值目标掩码可去除大部分像素级外观信息但保留目标空间表示，并允许用文本提示指定目标，因此有望提升对新外观目标的泛化能力。
