Method: 在仿真中使用冻结权重的基于掩码的 DrQ-v2 策略，仅改变其微调 SAM 3 检测器的文本提示来应对新着陆垫；另设实验使用在真值掩码上训练的策略，比较补全可见期间缺失掩码、掩码质心校正和面积校正等策略。

Result: 香蕉主题着陆垫使成功率从 75.6% 降至 12.0%，且 SAM 3 在原提示下很少检测到该垫；测试多个候选后选出的修订提示在无权重更新下将成功率恢复至 68.4%。在真值掩码策略实验中，补全着陆垫可见时缺失的掩码比掩码质心或面积校正更能帮助着陆；随时间跟踪着陆垫能更频繁提供掩码，并在确认着陆垫已不在渲染图像中后仍持续产生掩码，但未建立可靠的着陆成功率提升。

Conclusion: 对该方法而言，是否有可用掩码实际到达策略似乎是重要限制因素；仅靠提示适配可显著缓解外观变化问题，但掩码供给的可靠性仍是关键。

Abstract: Visual reinforcement learning (RL) policies that land a drone on a moving platform can fail when the landing pad looks different from the one used in training. Using binary target masks as the visual input removes most pixel-level appearance information while preserving the spatial representation of the target, and lets a text prompt specify the target. In simulation, we test whether a frozen (fixed-weight) mask-based DrQ-v2 policy can handle a new pad when only the prompt of its fine-tuned SAM 3 detector changes. A banana-themed pad lowered landing success from 75.6% to 12.0%, and SAM 3 rarely detected it under the original prompt. A revised prompt, chosen after testing several candidates, restored success to 68.4% with no weight updates. In a separate experiment with a policy trained on ground-truth masks, filling in masks that were missing while the pad was visible helped landing far more than the tested mask-centroid and area corrections. Tracking the pad over time supplied masks more often and also kept producing them after the pad was confirmed absent from the rendered image, but it did not establish a reliable improvement in landing success. Whether a usable mask reaches the policy at all thus appears to be an important limiting factor for this approach.

</details>


### [73] [PlanDiv: Test-Time Scaling with Latent Plans for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20010)
*Hanbo Zhang,Lianlei Shan,Liuhaichen Yang,Zezhi Tang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PlanDiv 通过让 VLA 策略在测试时生成“按计划区分”的多个动作候选，而非仅对同一解码器重采样，从而显著提升测试时扩展收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有测试时扩展从同一解码器多次采样动作候选，候选间主要差异是噪声而非规划，导致增加候选数量收益迅速递减；例如在 CALVIN 上从 4 个增至 8 个候选仅提升 0.06–0.07 个平均完成任务数。

Method: 提出 PlanDiv 动作头：轻量规划器提出 K 个隐计划假设，共享的掩码扩散执行器在单次骨干前向中为每个计划解码一个动作块，策略执行自身似然评分最高的动作块；计划仅从示范中学习，无需计划标签或奖励。

Result: 在骨干、数据和训练配方固定时，CALVIN ABC→D 五任务链成功率由单候选 56.9% 提升至 8 个计划条件候选的 69.9%，而自回归、无计划或同计划重采样的 8 候选最高仅 64.9%；同计划基线为固定计划的同一检查点，说明增益来自计划而非更多采样。LIBERO-Long 上 4 个计划消除 40% 的单候选失败，匹配重采样最多 21%。真实 Franka 上，单解任务与单输出 OpenVLA-OFT+ 基线相当；双解任务中 PlanDiv 成功 51/54，基线为 40/54。

Conclusion: 测试时扩展的关键在于候选多样性应体现不同计划而非同一解码器的噪声；PlanDiv 以无监督学习到的隐计划实现有效多样化，在多解任务和长程任务中带来稳定增益。

Abstract: Test-time scaling improves vision-language-action (VLA) policies by drawing several action candidates and executing the best one. We find that its returns diminish quickly when the candidates are resampled from one decoder: they differ by noise rather than by plan, and for each of three resampling sources, going from four to eight candidates adds only 0.06-0.07 to the average number of completed tasks on CALVIN. We introduce PlanDiv, an action head that makes the candidates differ by plan: a lightweight planner proposes K latent plan hypotheses, a shared masked-diffusion executor decodes one action chunk per plan from a single backbone pass, and the policy executes the chunk that its own likelihood ranks highest. The plans are learned from demonstrations alone, without plan labels or rewards. With the backbone, data and training recipe held fixed, eight plan-conditioned candidates raise five-task chain success on CALVIN ABC$\rightarrow$D from 56.9% to 69.9%, while eight candidates from autoregressive, plan-free or same-plan resampling reach at most 64.9%. The same-plan baseline is PlanDiv's own checkpoint with the plan held fixed, so this gap comes from the plans, not from drawing more samples. On LIBERO-Long, four plans remove 40% of single-candidate failures, against at most 21% for matched resampling. On a real Franka arm the gain appears where a task admits more than one solution: PlanDiv is on par with a single-output OpenVLA-OFT+ baseline on a one-solution task and succeeds in 51 of 54 trials against 40 of 54 on two tasks with two solutions.

</details>


### [74] [MoViC: Real-Time Humanoid Whole-Body Control from Monocular Video Streams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19998)
*Yimin Han,Jiahui Zhang,Wanyue Li,Zeren Luo,Yeke Chen,Liu Zhao,Peizhuo Li,Xiaoyu Ji,Shihao Dong,Xuechao Chen,Zhangguo Yu,Annan Tang,Peng Lu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MoViC 提出一个无需可穿戴设备的实时全身模仿框架，使用单目 RGB 视频作为人形机器人的控制信号，结合人体运动重建、机器人重定向和 transformer 行为基础模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实时全身模仿既需要稳健的通用跟踪器，也需要高质量实时重建；同时希望摆脱可穿戴跟踪设备，并解决低频视觉估计与高频控制器之间的异步执行问题。

Method: 框架包含单目人体运动重建、机器人重定向和 transformer 行为基础模型。策略接收本体感知历史、单个显式下一步参考以及紧凑频域运动表示，以提供时序上下文而不显式堆叠未来参考帧，从而降低输入维度和网络规模；部署时通过外推方法桥接 10 Hz 视觉估计与 50 Hz 控制器。

Result: 硬件实验展示了高动态运动跟踪，以及基于实时单目 RGB 输入的双臂和单臂操作任务无设备遥操作。

Conclusion: MoViC 实现了无需可穿戴设备的实时全身人形机器人模仿与控制，并支持动态运动跟踪和无设备遥操作。

Abstract: Real-time whole-body imitation from monocular video requires both a robust universal tracker and high-quality real-time reconstruction. We present MoViC, a framework that uses live RGB video as a control signal for humanoid robots without wearable tracking devices. The framework combines monocular human motion reconstruction, robot retargeting, and a transformer-based behavior foundation model. The policy receives proprioceptive history, a single explicit next-step reference, and a compact frequency-domain motion representation that supplies temporal context without explicitly stacking future reference frames, which reduces the input dimension and network size. During deployment, our extrapolation method bridges the 10-Hz visual estimation with 50-Hz controller, enabling asynchronous visual processing and policy execution. Hardware experiments further demonstrate highly dynamic motion tracking and device-free teleoperation of bimanual and single-arm manipulation tasks from live monocular RGB input. Project page: this https URL .

</details>


### [75] [Practice Is Not Free: Measuring the Embodied Cost of Reset-Free Robot Practice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19979)
*Vedant Telrandhe,Sushil Sharma*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一个低成本、开放的机器人自主练习测试平台，记录每次尝试的时间、关节行程和人工干预，并将练习调度建模为扣除实体成本后的能力增益优化问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人无人工重置的自主练习中，每次尝试仍消耗墙钟时间、执行器磨损，有时还需人工关注，但现有练习调度器通常把尝试视为免费。

Method: 自建SO-101机械臂运行两个由示范训练的ACT策略互相重置，配有基于视觉的成功检测器和记录每次尝试时间、关节行程与人工干预的账本；形式化成本感知练习调度，按测得能力增益扣除实体成本来奖励任务选择，并预先指定比较实验。

Result: 在硬件上运行51分钟的机器人时间的前两个条件中，固定重试上限未降低失败导致的执行时间占比（36% vs 可行性规则的27%，不显著），也未节省机器人时间；日志显示失败无明显聚集，失败后立即重试与其他尝试成功率相近；重试规则额外人工时间多来自方块被推出可达范围，而非规则本身；在日志上回放成本感知规则可在无手工阈值下恢复合理选择；构建经验还包括覆盖效应：将示范集中在更小工作区使该区域成功率从8/20提升到10/10。

Conclusion: 练习调度应显式计入实体与人工成本，且不能依赖失败聚集假设或固定重试上限；低成本账本式测试平台可用于测量成本并验证成本感知调度策略。

Abstract: When a robot practices without human resets, every attempt still costs wall-clock time, actuator wear, and sometimes a person's attention, yet the schedulers that decide what to practice next treat attempts as free. We present an open, low-cost testbed for measuring these costs: a self-built SO-101 arm runs two demonstration-trained ACT policies that reset each other, with a vision-based success detector and a ledger that records the time, joint travel, and human interventions of every attempt. We formulate cost-aware practice scheduling, in which task selection is rewarded by measured competence gain net of embodied cost, and specify in advance the comparison that will test it. Its first two conditions, run on hardware for 51 minutes of robot time, show that a fixed retry limit did not reduce the share of execution time lost to failures (36%, against 27% for a feasibility rule; not significant) and saved no robot time. The logs suggest why: failures showed no sign of clustering, so a retry right after a failure was as likely to succeed as any other attempt. The ledger also shows that most of the retry rule's extra human time came from cubes knocked out of reach rather than from the rule itself, and a cost-aware rule replayed on the logs recovers the sensible choice without a hand-set limit. We also report lessons from building the system, including a coverage effect in which concentrating demonstrations in a smaller workspace raised success there from 8/20 to 10/10.

</details>


### [76] [Value and Policy Calibration from Execution Feedback for Socially Compatible Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19969)
*Sen Shen,Ning Liu,Xiangrui Kong,Yingwei Li,Dongkun Han*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种世界模型强化学习框架，将想象策略改进与真实交互后的价值/策略校准结合，用于无信号交叉口自动驾驶社会兼容决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶车辆在交叉口的决策会影响周边人类驾驶车辆的延误、制动与运动平顺性；需要把想象交互结果与执行动作反馈关联，在通行效率与HDV交互负担之间取得平衡。

Method: 构建交互感知潜在世界模型支持想象；采用离策略校正与回放更新，利用观测奖励和状态转移同时监督critic与actor，实现价值校准和策略校准。

Result: 无信号交叉口仿真成功率达100%；跟驰HDV加速度变化较PIWM和DreamerV3分别降低65.5%和44.0%；消融表明价值校准降低HDV负担，策略校准提升通行效率；分布外交通偏移下，在相近通行时间时自车jerk和HDV加速度变化低于PIWM。

Conclusion: 该框架能兼顾AV通行效率与HDV交互负担，价值校准与策略校准具有互补作用，并在交通分布偏移下保持较好的运动平顺性。

Abstract: Autonomous vehicle (AV) decisions at intersections affect the delay, braking, and motion smoothness of surrounding human-driven vehicles (HDVs). Learning socially compatible behavior requires connecting imagined interaction outcomes with feedback from executed actions. We propose a world-model reinforcement learning framework combines imagined policy improvement with direct value and policy calibration from finalized interactions, balancing AV progress with HDV interaction burden. An interaction-aware latent model supports imagination, while off-policy correction and replay updates enable observed rewards and transitions to supervise both the critic and actor. Simulation experiments at an unsignalized intersection achieve a 100% success and reductions in following-HDV acceleration variation of 65.5% and 44.0% relative to PIWM and DreamerV3, respectively. Ablations identify complementary roles for value calibration in reducing HDV burden and policy calibration in improving passage efficiency. Tests under out-of-distribution traffic shifts further show lower ego jerk and HDV acceleration variation than PIWM at comparable passage times.

</details>


### [77] [CRAFT: A Robot-Agent Harness with Scope-Aware Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19952)
*Peng Zhang,Jiawen Wang,Cheng Xue,Jochen Renz*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出CRAFT框架，使机器人无需重训练技能即可从人类纠正中学习物体放置的局部要求，并区分动作物理成功与要求满足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人可能已掌握移动物体的技能，但仍需学习物体应放置何处；当人类拒绝某次放置时，机器人必须判断该纠正仅适用于当前情境，还是表达更广泛的规则。

Method: CRAFT将物理执行成功与是否满足要求分开记录；采用“范围阶梯”：在近乎相同且已评判的情境中使用记忆，否则结合记忆、显式规则和用于规则外要求的学习器，并按各来源对既往反馈的预测准确度加权。研究包含受控实验与相机 grounded 的机器人仿真，评估保留所有执行失败和超时。

Result: 在奖励可接受动作并对错误与提问计费的效用指标上，范围阶梯优于仅使用纠正记忆；在仿真中，它比具有持久记忆的语言模型智能体提问更少，但部分语言模型智能体错误动作更少，且在要求变化后成功率更高。

Conclusion: 结果支持将具体记忆与更广泛规则结合，但可靠适应不断变化的要求仍未解决；所有证据均来自使用生成要求和模拟反馈的仿真。

Abstract: A robot may know how to move an object yet still need to learn where it belongs. When a person rejects a placement, the robot must decide whether the correction applies only to that situation or expresses a broader rule. We study this problem in CRAFT (Contextual Requirement Adaptation with Frozen Tools), a framework for learning local requirements without retraining robot skills. It records physical success separately from whether an action meets the requirement. Our scope ladder uses memory when an action has already been judged in a nearly identical situation. Elsewhere, it combines memory, explicit rules and a learner for requirements beyond those rules, weighting their predictions by how well they predicted earlier feedback. Controlled studies show gains over correction memory alone in utility, which rewards acceptable actions and charges for mistakes and questions. In camera-grounded robot simulations, the ladder asks fewer questions than language-model agents with persistent memory, but some of those agents take fewer wrong actions and succeed more often after requirements change. All execution failures and timeouts remain in the evaluation. These results support combining specific memories with broader rules, while leaving reliable adaptation to changing requirements unresolved. All evidence comes from simulation with generated requirements and simulated feedback.

</details>


### [78] [From Companion to Guardian: Designing an Emergency Support System Using a Quadruped Robot for Older Adults](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19932)
*Chunyang Meng,Eduardo B. Sandoval,Josh Andres,Francisco Cruz,Mari Velonaki*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文设计并实现了一款基于Unitree Go1四足平台的家用陪伴机器人系统，将日常陪伴与紧急感知和响应相结合，面向独居老年人提供应急支持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 老年人独居现象增多，而护理与支持劳动力需求预计在2020-21至2049-50年间近乎翻倍，因此需要服务机器人兼顾日常陪伴和在人类支持无法立即到位时的紧急援助。

Method: 系统采用三阶段交互模型：陪伴模式、危险感知模式和紧急模式；使用基于规则的升级策略，融合视觉与语音输入中检测到的迹象来确定当前风险等级，并调整机器人进入紧急模式前等待用户确认安全的时间；检测到额外迹象可缩短等待时间。用户可确认安全、立即求助、停止机器人运动或重置系统。

Result: 通过105次自动化试验和25次分阶段物理试验验证了决策规则与升级时序；额外视觉证据能够缩短等待时间，若等待时间结束仍未确认安全，系统会进入紧急模式。

Conclusion: 该工作展示了四足陪伴机器人作为独居老人日常陪伴与紧急响应平台的可行性，并通过规则化风险升级和可解释的等待确认机制实现了陪伴与应急支持的结合。

Abstract: As older adults increasingly live alone, emergency support becomes an important function for home companion robots. In Australia, demand for the care and support workforce is projected to nearly double between 2020-21 and 2049-50. Service robots such as robot dogs may support older adults by combining everyday companionship with assistance when human support is not immediately available. We present the design and implementation of a domestic companion-robot system on a Unitree Go1 quadruped platform that combines companionship with emergency awareness and response. The system uses a three-stage interaction model consisting of companion, danger-awareness, and emergency modes. A rule-based escalation policy combines signs detected from visual and speech input to determine the current risk level and adjust how long the robot waits for the user to confirm safety before entering emergency mode. Additional detected signs can shorten this waiting time. Users can confirm safety, request immediate help, stop robot motion, or reset the system. We verified the implemented decision rules and escalation timing through 105 automated trials and 25 staged physical trials. Additional visual evidence shortened the waiting time, and the system entered emergency mode when the waiting time ended without confirmation.

</details>


### [79] [Lao Siji: Adapting openpilot to Closed-Loop End-to-End Driving in Neural Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19922)
*Chengzhi Gao,Yongzhi Liu,Jinchang Xu,Weichao Zhuang,Guodong Yin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Lao Siji通过微调开源L2量产模型openpilot而非从零训练，在2026 NVIDIA AlpaSim E2E驾驶挑战赛获第三，训练仅需1 GPU小时、权重相对发布模型仅偏移3.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 避免在通用视觉骨干上从零训练，复用openpilot的量产感知与跟车能力，以低成本适配神经重建场景中的闭环驾驶，并兼顾横向自校正与纵向安全。

Method: 冻结openpilot视觉编码器，微调策略层并加入ego-state adapter与route memory；在闭环rollout中用特权地图教师监督横向自校正，用body-extent loss约束道路与障碍物间隙，同时蒸馏基模型其余输出以保留跟车能力；纵向安全依赖基模型前车估计并置于规则化速度层之后。

Result: 在AlpaSim挑战赛排名第三，超过多数基于基础模型的参赛方案；消融显示自校正样本带来主要增益，无教师时间隙损失可替代；更长训练、模仿人类日志及人类日志速度目标无显著增益或产生负面影响；未额外训练即在HUGSIM与Odyssey上位居前列；最终训练仅1 GPU小时，权重偏移3.9%。

Conclusion: 表明对量产L2模型进行定向微调并结合规则安全层，可在端到端驾驶挑战中取得竞争力，自校正监督与间隙损失是关键设计；代码将在评审后开源。

Abstract: We present Lao Siji, an end-to-end driving policy that ranks third in the 2026 NVIDIA AlpaSim E2E Driving Challenge, ahead of most entries built on foundation models. Instead of training from scratch on a general-purpose vision backbone, we adapt openpilot, an open-source L2 production model. We freeze its vision encoder and fine-tune the policy layers with an ego-state adapter and a route memory. On closed-loop rollouts in neural-reconstructed scenes, we supervise the policy with a privileged map teacher for lateral self-correction and a body-extent loss for road and obstacle clearance, while distilling the base model's remaining outputs to retain car following. Ablations show that self-correction samples carry the gain, while the clearance loss substitutes for them where no teacher exists. Longitudinal safety instead rests on the base model's lead-vehicle estimate behind a rule-based speed layer. Longer training, imitating human logs, and human-log speed targets yield either no measurable gain or adverse effects. Without further training, Lao Siji also places among the leaders on HUGSIM and Odyssey. The final training takes only one GPU-hour and shifts the weights by 3.9% from the shipped model. Code will be released after review.

</details>


### [80] [AgenticJevNav: Agentic Object Search and Navigation via Event-Driven Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19912)
*Shuning Zhang,Shuli Zhang,Yunheng Wang,Wei Lu,Jiaxi Zhang,Yihang Kang,Tao Wang,Xiangyu Chen,Yixiao Feng,Kai Sheng,Renjing Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出AgenticJevNav，将目标规划、证据记忆、事件触发验证与有界执行结合，用于在证据不完整、执行失败和推理延迟下保持目标并调整动作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体导航需在证据不完整、执行失败和推理延迟条件下持续维持目标并调整动作，现有方法难以兼顾目标主导性与执行可靠性。

Method: AgenticJevNav由Agent基于RGB、里程计、记忆和反馈进行目标导向规划并授权有界动作集；Jev在执行阻塞/失败或连续两段低进展后审查结构化证据，并以版本、新鲜度和一次性检查约束决策应用；单一执行器与独立RGB反射约束运动；目标修订时撤销过时请求和动作后再重规划。

Result: 在六类仿真任务、60次试验中达到73.33%成功率，比次优Agent-only聚合控制高21.67个百分点；真实GENISOM L1四足机器人30次试验成功率为70.0%，并包含有界搜索报告。

Conclusion: 仿真与真实机器人结果验证了该架构的有效性；剩余失败表明在 grounding、接近规划和运行时方面仍有改进空间。

Abstract: Agentic navigation must maintain goals and adapt actions from incomplete evidence despite execution failures and delayed inference. We present AgenticJevNav, which links goal-directed planning, evidence memory, event-triggered verification, and bounded execution. The Agent plans from RGB, odometry, memory, and feedback, retaining goal ownership while authorizing a bounded action set. Jev reviews structured evidence after blocked/failed execution or two consecutive low-progress segments. Version, freshness, and once-only checks govern decision application. A single executor and independent RGB reflex constrain motion. Goal revisions revoke obsolete requests and actions before replanning. Across six simulated task families, AgenticJevNav achieves 73.33% SR over 60 trials, 21.67 percentage points above the next-best Agent-only aggregate control under differing historical protocols. A real-world GENISOM L1 quadruped deployment achieves 70.0% success over 30 trials, including bounded search reporting. Together, the simulation and physical-robot results demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed architecture, while remaining failures highlight opportunities to improve grounding, approach planning, and runtime.

</details>


### [81] [DiffWAM: Learning World Action Model from Consecutive Visual Difference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19904)
*Xinheng Zhang,Haoyu Xiong,Chao Jing,Xiaohan Zhang,Linshan Li,Hongbang Fan,Xingyu Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DiffWAM 提出一种基于混合专家（MoE）的世界动作模型，通过联合学习未来状态预测与连续视觉差异预测来增强世界表征，在 LIBERO、RoboTwin 2.0 和真实机器人操作任务上取得领先性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界动作模型从当前观测和指令联合预测未来场景状态与机器人动作，但未来状态中大量任务无关内容（如静态背景）会与任务相关变化纠缠，削弱模型捕捉任务执行关键变化的能力。

Method: DiffWAM 将连续视觉差异定义为相邻观测隐表征之间的差异，作为显式时序变化学习信号；框架包含 World Expert（未来帧预测）、Action Expert（动作生成）和 Diff Expert（轨迹上的隐差异预测）。采用结构化单向注意力掩码，使 Diff、World、Action 专家仅条件于当前帧表征，并阻断专家间注意力；推理时可移除 World 和 Diff 专家，不引入额外推理开销。

Result: 在 LIBERO 和 RoboTwin 2.0 基准上达到 state-of-the-art，并在真实世界操作任务上显著优于 FastWAM；平均成功率分别为 98.8%、92.65% 和 88.3%，相较 FastWAM 分别提升 1.75、1.55 和 15.0 个百分点。

Conclusion: 通过显式建模连续视觉差异并采用可移除的 MoE 专家设计，DiffWAM 能更有效学习任务相关时序变化，在不增加推理开销的情况下提升世界动作模型的预测与操作性能。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) have emerged as an important paradigm in robot learning, jointly predicting future scene states and robot actions from current observations and instructions. However, future states entangle task-related changes with large amounts of task-irrelevant content, such as static backgrounds, which weakens the model's ability to capture changes relevant to task execution. To address this, we propose Difference World Action Model (DiffWAM), a Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) framework that enhances world representations by jointly learning future-state prediction and consecutive visual difference prediction. We define consecutive visual differences as differences between the latent representations of consecutive observations, providing an explicit learning signal for temporal variation. The framework comprises a World Expert for future frame prediction, an Action Expert for action generation, and a Diff Expert for predicting these latent differences along the trajectory. A structured unidirectional attention mask conditions the Diff, World, and Action Experts solely on the current-frame representation while blocking attention between experts. This design allows both the World and Diff Experts to be removed during inference, introducing no additional inference overhead. DiffWAM achieves state-of-the-art performance on the LIBERO and RoboTwin 2.0 benchmarks and substantially improves over FastWAM on real-world manipulation tasks. Specifically, it attains average success rates of 98.8%, 92.65%, and 88.3%, outperforming the state-of-the-art baseline (i.e., FastWAM) by 1.75, 1.55, and 15.0 percentage points, respectively.

</details>


### [82] [EasyNav: A Modular Architecture for Real-Time Navigation Across Heterogeneous Robot Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19894)
*Francisco Martín Rico,Francisco Miguel Moreno,Juan Sebastián Cely Gutiérrez,Esther Aguado,Miguel Ángel De Miguel Paraíso,José Miguel Guerrero*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出以显式共享导航状态为唯一组件交互接口的机器人导航架构，并实现为开源 ROS 2 框架 EasyNav；真实机器人实验表明其相较 Nav2 显著降低反应延迟并减少 CPU 与内存开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人导航栈通常将建图、定位、规划和控制隐式绑定到特定表示与数据流，导致架构难以扩展、难以实验对比，也难以端到端分析时序；在多种空间表示、传感模态和坐标系共存且以不同频率演化的异构场景中，这一问题尤为关键。

Method: 论文设计以显式、共享的导航状态作为模块化组件之间唯一交互接口的导航架构，使建图、定位、规划和控制均围绕该共享状态运行，从而在明确的兼容性与时序契约下形成可互换、表示无关的流水线；该架构实现为开源 ROS 2 框架 EasyNav，并通过真实机器人实验验证，包括激光室内导航以及结合 GPS 与 3D LiDAR 的大规模室内外多楼层路线导航。

Result: 在与 Nav2 匹配条件的对比中，EasyNav 将室内反应延迟从 377 ms 降至 33 ms，将室外反应延迟从 71 ms 降至 8 ms，同时使用更少的 CPU 和内存。

Conclusion: 以共享导航状态为核心的架构能够提升导航系统的模块化、可扩展性与时序可分析性，并在真实异构导航场景中以更低延迟和资源开销取得优于 Nav2 的表现。

Abstract: Most robot navigation stacks implicitly bind mapping, localization, planning, and control to specific representations and data flows, yielding architectures hard to extend, compare experimentally, or reason about end-to-end timing. This is critical in heterogeneous scenarios, where multiple spatial representations, sensing modalities, and coordinate frames coexist and evolve at different rates. This paper presents a navigation architecture centered on an explicit, shared navigation state that serves as the sole interaction interface between modular components. Constraining mapping, localization, planning, and control to operate over this shared state enables interchangeable, representation-agnostic pipelines under explicit compatibility and timing contracts. We realize this architecture as EasyNav, an open source ROS 2 framework, and validate it through real-robot experiments, including indoor navigation with laser sensing and large-scale indoor-outdoor navigation with GPS and 3D LiDAR across multi-level routes. Compared with Nav2 under matched conditions, EasyNav reduces reaction latency from 377 to 33 ms indoors and from 71 to 8 ms outdoors, while also using less CPU and memory.

</details>


### [83] [Drone Navigation for Fast, Safe Emergency Response](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19818)
*Pranav Nedunghat,Jeffrey Mao,Praveen Merumugumala*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 面向灾害救援无人机自主导航，提出一种实时计算最小时间轨迹并保证避障的方法，以“规划时间+遍历时间”为任务时间指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灾害场景中无人机需自主避障并快速抵达幸存者或投送物资；既有时间最优轨迹方法多未考虑避障，难以满足时间敏感任务需求。

Method: 在轨迹优化中同时引入避障约束，实时求解最小时间轨迹，并保证沿轨迹避障；以规划时间与遍历时间之和衡量任务时间，样例中四旋翼穿越峡谷状障碍并大部分时间接近最大推力飞行。

Result: 方法可实时生成带避障保证的时间最优轨迹，并将避障纳入优化以改善总体任务时间；摘要未给出定量性能指标，仅展示穿越多障碍的样例任务。

Conclusion: 将避障与时间最优规划统一，并以规划加遍历总时间评价，有助于无人机在灾害救援中快速、安全地抵达目标，提升时间敏感任务效率。

Abstract: In 2025, there was a total of 358 weather-related disasters worldwide affecting millions of people. For unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs) to autonomously aid these people, they need to be able to navigate around complex obstacles and reach their destinations to deliver supplies or quickly locate survivors in disaster-stricken areas. Our research addresses this need by providing a method to compute minimum-time trajectories in real time while providing guarantees of obstacle avoidance along the trajectory. Previous approaches treat the problem of computing time-optimal trajectories without considering obstacle avoidance. Our work not only includes obstacle avoidance constraints, but also improves overall mission time for navigation. We define mission time as the sum of planning time and traversal time. In time-sensitive emergencies, rapid trajectory planning is essential to ensure the prompt delivery of critical medical aid. As such, we consider the combined planning and traversal time as the key metric for how quickly the vehicle can reach a survivor. We show a sample mission in Fig. 1 where the quadrotor navigates through multiple canyon-like obstacles to deliver aid; during most of this trajectory, it is applying the near-maximum thrust.

</details>


### [84] [DynLimit: A Simulation Benchmark for Dynamics-Critical Planning and Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19785)
*Yang Xiao,Yongzhi Liu,Sunan Zhang,Kang Wang,Guodong Yin,Weichao Zhuang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DynLimit 是一个面向车辆操纵极限实验的端到端仿真平台，将任务请求、闭环执行响应与任务完成判定分离，并提供任务导向评估、回放与诊断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 车辆接近操纵极限时，必须区分“操纵要求什么”“闭环系统执行了什么”和“任务是否完成”，避免用侧偏或轮胎利用率峰值替代任务约束。

Method: 平台由道路、摩擦、障碍与参考对象组合场景；规划器和控制器通过共享观测/控制接口接入；车辆适配器支持免许可四轮模型或外部动力学求解器；同步记录用于任务评估、回放和响应检查。评估方法将给定参考需求与采样通过集及事件对齐诊断配对。

Result: 在8类任务、3种摩擦环境和3条参考辅助基线链上执行450次运行：360次标称、18次局部细化、72次预设初始偏移。方法差异依赖任务族；相同总通过数可来自不同通过单元；局部细化缩小了两个采样过渡但未找到连续边界；两个案例表明侧偏或轮胎利用率峰值不能替代任务约束。

Conclusion: 所有报告运行中三种视角保持区分：结果可从给定需求追溯到执行响应，再由任务规则判定，从而实现可解释的极限操纵评估。

Abstract: Experiments near vehicle handling limits must keep three questions apart: what a maneuver requests, what the closed-loop system executes, and whether the task is completed. We present DynLimit, a simulation platform that organizes this process end to end. Scenarios are composed from road, friction, obstacle, and reference objects; planners and controllers attach through a shared observation and control interface; a vehicle adapter runs either a license-free four-wheel model or an external dynamics solver; and synchronized records feed task evaluation, replay, and response inspection. The platform carries a task-oriented evaluation method that pairs a prescribed reference demand with a sampled passing set and event-aligned diagnosis. Across eight task families, three friction environments, and three reference-assisted baseline chains we executed 450 runs: 360 nominal, 18 locally refined, and 72 with prescribed initial offsets. Method differences depend on the task family, equal aggregate pass counts arise from different passing cells, and local refinement narrows two sampled transitions without locating a continuous boundary. Two case comparisons show why sideslip or tire-utilization peaks cannot replace task constraints. The three views therefore stay distinct in every reported run: an outcome can be traced from the prescribed demand, through the executed response, to the task rule that decided it. Code is available at this https URL

</details>


### [85] [Recovery-Guided Model-Based Diffusion for Safety-Critical Legged Locomotion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19775)
*Lei Zheng,Shivam Sood,Mithun Vanniasinghe,Guillaume Sartoretti,Armin Lederer*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种恢复引导的基于模型扩散（MBD）框架，将学习到的终端恢复评价器嵌入短时域轨迹优化，在保证安全前瞻的同时降低在线计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全关键的高速足式运动需要在安全前瞻与在线计算效率之间权衡；短预测时域虽降低 rollout 成本，但安全前瞻有限，且严格安全约束会严重压缩可行轨迹空间。

Method: 构建恢复引导的MBD框架：学习终端恢复critic以近似指定恢复策略rollout下的最坏情况未来间隙裕度；引入可行性引导细化策略，在每个采样阶段联合考虑时域内约束与终端可恢复性，并保留可行精英样本以缓解采样支撑损失。

Result: 在50组配对随机化Unitree Go2避障试验中，该方法实现零碰撞和98%严格间隙成功率，且计算量显著低于长时域MBD基线。

Conclusion: 将终端恢复评价器与可行性引导细化引入短时域MBD，可在安全关键足式避障中同时获得高安全性与高计算效率。

Abstract: Safety-critical high-speed legged locomotion requires motion planners to balance safety foresight with online computational efficiency. Although short prediction horizons reduce rollout costs, they provide limited safety foresight, and strict safety constraints severely restrict the feasible trajectory space. We propose a recovery-guided model-based diffusion (MBD) framework for safety-critical legged locomotion. It incorporates a learned terminal recovery critic into short-horizon trajectory optimization. Specifically, the critic approximates the worst-case future clearance margin along rollouts of a designated recovery policy. We introduce a feasibility-guided refinement strategy that combines within-horizon constraints and terminal recoverability at each sampling stage, while retaining feasible elites to mitigate sampling-support loss. Across 50 paired randomized Unitree Go2 obstacle-avoidance trials, our approach achieves zero collisions and 98% strict-clearance success, while requiring substantially less computation than long-horizon MBD baselines. A project page is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [86] [JEPA4R: Embodied JEPA World Models with 4D Point Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19771)
*Jiarui Dong,Zhiheng Liang,Yanjun Li,Zeyu Zhang,Heejeong Nam,Haoyu Chen,Hao Tang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: JEPA4R 是一种基于单深度相机点云的 JEPA 世界模型，在潜空间中预测和规划 4D 点云，并在冻结潜表征上训练策略头，实现仿真到真机的零样本机器人操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 JEPA 世界模型在潜空间预测未来嵌入并进行规划，但 2D 图像使操作所依赖的度量几何隐式且与外观纠缠，且模型“想象”的内容不透明，常仅通过短时程潜空间规划在仿真中评估。

Method: JEPA4R 观察单深度相机点云，在点云潜空间中预测未来并规划，称为 4D 点规划；在冻结世界模型潜表征上接策略头执行动作；并在冻结世界模型上使用解码器将想象内容可视化为 4D 点云。

Result: 解码器在 200 步内平均点误差小于 1.1 cm；在 OGBench 的 Cube-Single、Cube-Double、Cube-Triple 和 Scene 上优于像素 JEPA；在 MetaWorld 和 Adroit Relocate 上分别达 98.4% 和 88.0%，排名第一，高于第二的 R3M 的 96.0% 和 65.3%；仅在数字孪生中训练即可在带夹爪和灵巧手的真实机械臂上零样本运行。

Conclusion: JEPA4R 通过 4D 点云潜空间建模显式保留度量几何并提升想象可解释性，在多种操作基准上取得领先，并展现出从数字孪生到真实机械臂的零样本迁移能力。

Abstract: Joint-embedding predictive architecture (JEPA) world models predict future embeddings instead of pixels and plan in latent space. Two challenges remain: 2D images leave the metric geometry that manipulation acts on implicit and entangled with appearance, and what these models imagine stays hidden, as they are mostly judged in simulation by short-horizon latent planning. We present JEPA4R, a JEPA world model that observes single-depth-camera point clouds, predicts and plans in their latent space, and acts through a policy head on its frozen latents; we call this 4D point planning. A decoder on the frozen world model shows what it imagines as 4D point clouds, with a mean point error within 1.1 cm over 200 steps. Under the policy-learning protocols of OGBench and CortexBench, JEPA4R leads pixel JEPA models on OGBench Cube-Single, Cube-Double, Cube-Triple and Scene, and ranks first on MetaWorld and Adroit Relocate with 98.4% and 88.0%, above the runner-up, R3M, at 96.0% and 65.3%. Trained only in a digital twin, JEPA4R and its policy heads run zero-shot on the real arm with both a gripper and a dexterous hand.

</details>


### [87] [M-CROSS: A Multisensor Simulation Dataset for SLAM across Water--Land Transitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19765)
*Yan Dong,Tianyi Wu,Junyu Yang,Shaoqiang Qiu,Zefeng Yan,Tao Zhang,Bin Han*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出M-CROSS：首个面向连续水陆过渡场景的多传感器SLAM仿真数据集与基准，并给出过渡感知评测协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水陆跨域机器人需在陆地、过渡和水下阶段连续定位，但传感器特性和可用性会突变；现有数据集与传统指标难以刻画过渡导致的性能退化与失败。

Method: 构建同步采集LiDAR、声呐、双目相机、IMU、DVL和深度测量的仿真数据集，提供6DoF位姿真值、逐传感器介质状态标注及介质相关传感器退化模型；设计TCR、PW-APE、TEE、TSP四项过渡感知指标，分别评估轨迹完成率、单域精度、过渡诱发误差和长时间部分浸没下的定位存活能力。

Result: 对6种代表性SLAM系统的实验表明，现有方法无法在全部水陆过渡中同时保持定位精度与轨迹连续性，出现过渡诱发漂移、轨迹不连续和跟踪失败。

Conclusion: M-CROSS公开可用，揭示了跨介质SLAM在过渡阶段的评测空白与方法缺陷，为水陆跨域定位鲁棒性评估和后续方法发展提供基准。

Abstract: Water--land cross-domain robots require continuous localization across land, transition and underwater phases, where sensor characteristics and availability change abruptly. Existing datasets and conventional metrics are not designed to capture transition-induced degradation and failures. We present \textbf{M-CROSS}, to the best of our knowledge, the first multisensor simulation dataset and benchmark for SLAM across continuous water--land transitions. It provides synchronized LiDAR, sonar, stereo camera, IMU, DVL, and depth measurements; 6DoF pose ground truth; sensor-wise medium-state annotations; and medium-dependent sensor degradation models. We establish a transition-aware protocol comprising the Trajectory Completion Rate (TCR), Piecewise Absolute Pose Error (PW-APE), Transition End Error (TEE), and Transition Survival Period (TSP), which measure trajectory completion, single-domain accuracy, transition-induced error, and localization survival under prolonged partial immersion, respectively. Experiments with 6 representative SLAM systems show that existing methods fail to maintain both localization accuracy and trajectory continuity across the full set of water--land transitions, exhibiting transition-induced drift, trajectory discontinuities, and tracking failures. M-CROSS is publicly available this https URL

</details>


### [88] [Cooperative Transport of Unknown Objects by Distributed Effort Regulation in Multi-agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19731)
*Tamzidul Mina,Shyam S. Kannan,Wonse Jo,Shaocheng Luo,Galen B. King,Byung-Cheol Min*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种无需物体先验知识的分布式多机器人物体运输策略，机器人沿物体边界动态选择推点，并通过基于航向误差的推力调节实现目标导向运输。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法通常依赖物体形状、尺寸、质量分布或机器人数量等先验信息，难以适用于未知物体和动态变化场景。

Method: 机器人沿物体边界运动并动态确定可行推点；提出无物体模型的航向误差驱动推力调节方法，利用个体与集体航向误差调节推力；当推点丢失或所需推力超出机器人纠正航向能力时，机器人沿边界动态重定位。

Result: 在简单/复杂、凸/非凸边界及动态质量分布物体上验证；分析机器人分布和初始位姿对运输时间与路径效率的影响；可扩展性表明运输时间取决于可用推面数量，而推面数量受物体尺寸、形状和机器人间安全距离影响；多试验中获得高效路径和稳定运输时间，并对非均匀摩擦和轮滑等外部扰动具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 该分布式策略无需物体或机器人数量先验知识，可实现高效、稳定且鲁棒的多机器人未知物体运输。

Abstract: A distributed multi-robot object transportation strategy is proposed for objects of unknown shape, size, geometry, mass and mass distribution without any prior knowledge about the object or number of robots involved in the transportation process. Robots follow the boundary of the object and dynamically determine viable pushing points. A novel object-model-free heading-error-driven pushing effort regulation method is proposed to promote goal-directed object motion; robots regulate their pushing effort using individual and collective heading errors. Robots dynamically relocate around the object boundary if a pushing point is lost or the required pushing effort exceeds its capability to correct its heading. The proposed method is validated using simple and complex shapes with convex and non-convex boundary regions and dynamically changing weight distribution of objects. Detailed analysis on the effects of robot distribution and initial object pose on the transportation performance in terms of required time and path efficiency is also presented. Scalability analysis revealed that object transportation time is dependent on the number of pushing surfaces available which in turn depends on the object size, shape, and set inter-robot safety distance. The proposed system yielded highly efficient object paths with consistent elapsed transport times over several trials robust to external disturbances such as non-uniform friction between contacting surfaces and robot wheel slip.

</details>


### [89] [AlphaTruck: A Platform for High-Speed Off-Road Autonomy on Unstructured Terrain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19730)
*Rwik Rana,Adam J. Thorpe,Jesse Quattrociocchi,William Ward,Goktug Poyrazoglu,Adam Uccello,Christian Ellis,Rahul Moorthy Mahesh,Yukang Cao,Nathan Tsoi,Garrett Warnell,Volkan Isler,Ufuk Topcu,Joydeep Biswas*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出AlphaTruck：基于1/5比例RC底盘、集成多模态感知与嵌入式CPU-GPU计算及车载自主栈的高速越野导航平台，可在仅靠车载感知与计算下以6-10 m/s自主行驶。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 越野自主导航不仅依赖算法进步，也需要适合开发与部署的稳健物理平台。现有平台多处于两个极端：全尺寸车辆能力强但昂贵、部署维护后勤负担大且超限使用成本高；RC比例车辆小而便宜，但地形通过性与载荷能力有限，难以承载传感器和计算设备。因此需要一个兼顾能力、成本与可部署性的平台。

Method: AlphaTruck将多模态传感、嵌入式CPU-GPU计算和车载自主导航栈集成到大型1/5比例RC车辆底盘上，作为研究测试台和可外场部署平台。由于在该尺度下自主栈、传感器和电池共同竞争质量、功率与散热预算，平台需平衡五项需求：复杂地形机动性、丰富感知、车载计算、承载自主栈并预留任务载荷的载荷能力，以及实际运行与维修性。

Result: AlphaTruck仅使用车载感知与计算即可在越野环境中自主导航，速度达到6-10 m/s；平台可携带任务特定载荷，面向勘测、搜救和农业等商业与国防任务。

Conclusion: AlphaTruck在能力有限的小型RC平台与昂贵的全尺寸车辆之间提供了一种可研究、可部署的1/5比例越野自主导航平台方案，并通过系统级权衡实现高速越野自主运行。

Abstract: Progress in autonomous navigation in off-road environments requires not only advances in algorithms, but also robust and capable physical platforms suited to both development and deployment. Today, off-road autonomy is typically developed and tested at one of two extremes of vehicle scale, each with different drawbacks. Full-size vehicles are capable but expensive, require large logistic infrastructure for deployment and maintenance, and expensive when pushed beyond their limits. RC-scale vehicles are small and inexpensive, but limited in the terrain they can traverse and the payload that they can carry, which includes their sensors and compute. We present the AlphaTruck platform, which leverages recent advances in compact sensing and compute to integrate multimodal sensing, embedded CPU-GPU compute, and an onboard autonomy stack for high-speed, off-road navigation, all integrated into a large-scale, 1/5th RC vehicle chassis. AlphaTruck is designed as both a research testbed and a field-deployable platform for commercial and defense missions such as survey, search and rescue, and agriculture, with the capacity to carry task-specific payloads. At 1/5 scale, the autonomy stack, sensors, and batteries all compete for the same mass, power, and cooling budget, so the platform must balance five needs: mobility over rough terrain, rich sensing, onboard computation, payload capacity to carry the autonomy stack while leaving room for task-specific equipment, and practical operation and repair. Thus, AlphaTruck navigates off-road environments autonomously at 6-10 m/s using only onboard sensing and compute.

</details>


### [90] [Sim-to-Real Room-Mediated Object-Goal Navigation with ROS 2 and Nav2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19720)
*Matthew Chua,Adam Scicluna,Gavin Paul*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文将基于房间概率向量（RPV）的ObjectNav策略Room-Mediated Co-occurrence部署到Spot与TurtleBot 4真实机器人上，通过机器人无关的ROS 2桥接研究语义先验的仿真到现实泛化，并分析真实部署中的性能差距与适配问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ObjectNav通常在仿真中评估，具备可扩展性和可重复性，但真实世界部署多停留在可行性演示，缺乏对仿真到现实迁移的系统性考察。作者希望研究RPV语义先验在物理环境中的泛化能力，并明确从仿真迁移到硬件所需的适配。

Method: 在Boston Dynamics Spot和Clearpath Robotics TurtleBot 4两个平台上，通过机器人无关的ROS 2桥接部署Room-Mediated Co-occurrence策略；该策略基于Room Probability Vectors进行推理。论文描述RPV从仿真迁移到硬件时在传感、定位和动作接口方面的适配，并在大学研究生科研设施中进行真实环境实验。

Result: 在Spot上开展66次试验，覆盖11个目标类别和两个起始位置，RPV取得原始成功率0.455；与论文报告的仿真成功率0.539相比存在8.4个百分点的差距。作者将差距归因于未检测到的玻璃墙、进入禁入区域、可用深度范围受限、目标尺寸以及房间词典与物理布局不匹配等因素。

Conclusion: RPV语义先验能够迁移到真实机器人平台，但真实部署仍面临感知、定位、动作接口和环境语义表示不匹配等挑战；真实ObjectNav评估需要超越可行性演示，系统分析仿真到现实的性能差距及其成因。

Abstract: Although object-goal navigation (ObjectNav) approaches are usually evaluated in simulation, which offers scale and repeatable environments, real-world deployments are rarely examined beyond feasibility demonstrations. This paper presents the deployment of Room-Mediated Co-occurrence, an ObjectNav policy that reasons over Room Probability Vectors (RPVs), on two robotic platforms (Boston Dynamics Spot and Clearpath Robotics TurtleBot 4) through a robot-agnostic ROS 2 bridge. We study how these semantic priors generalise to physical environments and describe the adaptations required to transfer RPV from simulation to hardware, covering sensing, localisation, and the action interface. Across 66 trials on Spot, searching for 11 object categories from two starting positions in a university postgraduate research facility, RPV achieves a raw Success Rate (SR) of 0.455. Our analysis identifies undetected glass walls, entry into off-limits areas, limited usable depth range, target size, and mismatch between the room lexicon and physical layout as plausible contributors to the 8.4 percentage-point gap between the real-world SR and the reported simulated SR of 0.539. Code is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [91] [Cache or Denoise Less? Residual Reuse in a Flow-Matching World Action Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19709)
*Abhinav Gupta,Nissan Pow*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文在 Fast-WAM 上比较跨周期缓存与减少去噪步数，发现少步数基线更快且几乎不损失成功率，缓存收益有限且对采样器/噪声设置敏感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流匹配机器人策略每个动作块需多次网络评估；跨周期缓存（如 C3ache）复用早期重规划计算，但其是否优于直接减少去噪步数尚不明确。

Method: 在 6B 参数世界动作模型 Fast-WAM 的 LIBERO-Goal 任务上，对比无缓存少步数与跨周期缓存；测量成功率、RTX A6000 上重规划延迟，并用成本模型分析缓存覆盖流路径比例及噪声重采样影响。

Result: 两个无缓存步数在 500 个初始状态上与十步成功率非劣（2 个百分点内，单一 checkpoint 和噪声种子），重规划调用从 202 ms 降至 92 ms；保留两个新鲜步的缓存不可能快于两个无缓存步，缓存两步中的第一步且每四次调用刷新最多节省 11–14%。C3ache 八个缓存步覆盖 80% 流路径（而非 44%）时成功率 63% vs 95%；每次调用重采样噪声使覆盖≥60%的缓存下降 14–40 个百分点。

Conclusion: 应优先采用少步数基线，并在实际部署的采样器和噪声设置下测试计算复用；作者发布评估框架、日志和检查清单。

Abstract: Flow-matching robot policies generate each action chunk through repeated network evaluations, one per denoising step. Cross-cycle caches such as C$^3$ache reuse computation from earlier replanning calls, but whether they beat simply taking fewer steps is unclear. We compare the two in Fast-WAM, a 6B-parameter world action model, on LIBERO-Goal. Two uncached steps keep the success of ten: on all 500 initial states they are non-inferior within two percentage points, for one checkpoint and noise seed. Under PyTorch's default compile mode, they also cut a replanning call on an RTX A6000 from 202 to 92 ms. This leaves little room for caching. A cache that keeps two fresh steps cannot be faster than two uncached steps: every call still encodes the images, runs the video expert and computes those two steps. Caching the first of two steps, refreshed every fourth call, saves at most 11--14% of a call by our cost model. Cached policies are also fragile. Their losses tracked the share of the flow path the cached steps cover: under Fast-WAM's current time shift, C$^3$ache's eight cached steps cover 80% of the path instead of 44%, and the cached policy succeeds on 63% of states against 95% uncached. Resampling the noise on every call cost caches covering 60% or more of the path 14--40 points. These results argue for fewer-step baselines and for testing reuse under the deployed sampler and noise settings. We release our evaluation harness, logs and a checklist.

</details>


### [92] [Real-Time Air Pollution Monitoring and Tracking via Adaptive Density-Driven Multi-UAV Coordination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19703)
*Julian Martinez,Ruchika Singh,Mohammad Afrazi,Kooktae Lee*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种自适应密度驱动控制（D$^2$C）框架，用于协调多无人机在动态污染羽流中实现跟踪与建图，并在仿真中显著优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准确、及时的空气污染监测与追踪对公共健康和环境管理至关重要，但污染物分布动态演化，尤其在高风速、时变风场下易漂移出固定监测区域，给多无人机协同监测带来挑战。

Method: 将D$^2$C策略扩展至基于预测污染传播和动态更新参考密度的无人机轨迹引导；采用传感器驱动的代理羽流更新机制，通过梯度吸引、风平流、样本间排斥和随机扩散传播参考样本；采用集中协调结构，各无人机共享局部测量以维护统一参考样本集，并支持系统化目标分配与轨迹规划。

Result: 在温和与强风条件下的仿真表明，所提方法在Sinkhorn距离指标上显著优于先进基线；在高速度、时变风场导致羽流漂移出固定监测域时，基线方法无法维持跟踪，而D$^2$C框架能够成功适应演化分布。

Conclusion: D$^2$C是一种高效且具有韧性的动态环境大气监测解决方案，适用于多无人机协同污染跟踪与建图。

Abstract: Accurate and timely air pollution monitoring and tracking are critical for protecting public health and supporting effective environmental management. This paper presents an adaptive Density-Driven Control (D$^2$C) framework for coordinating multiple unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs) to track and map dynamically evolving pollutant distributions. The proposed framework extends the D$^2$C strategy to guide UAV trajectories based on predictive pollutant propagation and dynamically updated reference densities. A sensor-driven surrogate plume update mechanism is employed, in which reference samples are propagated using locally sensed concentration gradients and wind estimates through gradient attraction, wind advection, inter-sample repulsion, and stochastic diffusion, enabling UAVs to maintain an accurate spatio-temporal representation of the pollution field. The framework utilizes a coordinated structure where individual UAVs share their local measurements with a central coordinator to maintain a unified set of reference samples, facilitating systematic target allocation and trajectory planning. Simulation results under both mild and strong wind conditions demonstrate that the proposed method significantly outperforms state-of-the-art baselines in terms of Sinkhorn distance. Specifically, in scenarios where the plume drifts beyond a fixed monitoring domain under high-velocity, time-varying wind fields, the tested baseline methods fail to maintain tracking, whereas the proposed D$^2$C framework successfully adapts to the evolving distribution. These findings highlight D$^2$C as an efficient and resilient solution for atmospheric monitoring in dynamic environments.

</details>


### [93] [Physics-Grounded STL Monitoring from Image Observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19688)
*Xinpei Ni,Glen Chou,Samuel Coogan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于多视角图像共享隐表示与谓词物理裕度解码的STL视觉监控方法，并用保形预测校准区间STL鲁棒性，实现对未见公式的满足/违反认证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人操作任务需满足由STL表达的时序与条件约束；从图像监控STL很困难，因为鲁棒性依赖距离、间隙等物理裕度，而这些量不能直接观测，且为每个公式单独学习视觉监控器不现实。

Method: 从多视角图像学习共享隐表示，并为各谓词解码具有物理意义的裕度；将编码器与谓词解码器联合训练，再利用STL语义把谓词裕度组合为任意有界公式的鲁棒性估计而无需重训练；最后用保形预测校准所选STL子句的解码误差，得到双侧区间并通过区间STL传播到由这些子句组成的任意公式。

Result: 联合训练将认证率从冻结编码器的58.7%提升到96.3%，谓词符号准确率几乎不变；在LIBERO仿真厨房任务中，对18个未见公式的4662次评估满足/违反预测正确率为98.9%；对最长17分钟任务，校准边界在96.9%的任务上给出满足或违反结论且无错误结论；一次校准在八个子句的全部255个合取上达到同时91.0%覆盖率，支持无需重新校准的新公式。

Conclusion: 该方法无需为每个STL公式单独训练监控器，即可从图像中解码物理裕度、组合STL鲁棒性并进行校准认证，在未见公式和长时任务上实现高准确率与可证明的满足/违反区间认证。

Abstract: Robotic manipulation missions must satisfy timed and conditional requirements that can be expressed in signal temporal logic (STL). While a practical means of monitoring these requirements is from camera images, this is challenging because STL robustness depends on physical margins, such as distances and clearances, that are not directly observed. Rather than learning a separate visual monitor for each formula, we learn a shared latent representation from multi-view images and decode physically meaningful margins for individual predicates. Co-training the encoder with predicate decoders increases the certification rate from 58.7% to 96.3% compared with a frozen encoder, despite nearly unchanged predicate sign accuracy. STL semantics then compose these margins into robustness estimates for any bounded formula over the trained predicates, without retraining. We further calibrate the decoding errors of selected STL clauses using conformal prediction, yielding two-sided intervals that certify both satisfaction and violation and propagate through interval STL to any formula composed from the clauses. In simulated kitchen missions (LIBERO), our monitor correctly predicts satisfaction or violation in 98.9% of 4662 evaluations across 18 unseen formulas. For missions lasting up to 17 min, calibrated bounds establish satisfaction or violation for 96.9% of missions without incorrect conclusions. A single calibration also provides simultaneous 91.0% coverage across all 255 conjunctions of eight clauses, allowing new formulas without recalibration.

</details>


### [94] [Early Readability of Trajectory Quality in Diffusion Driving Planners](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19684)
*Xiang Zhang,Ziyang Leng,Yuxin Bao,Zhihao Zhao,Bolei Zhou,Xugui Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究发现扩散式驾驶规划器在第一次去噪器评估后的内部状态就已包含足以区分候选轨迹优劣的信息，据此提出"首次评估后即选、只完成被选候选"的早选方法，在降低计算量的同时提升开环轨迹质量与闭环驾驶表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散式规划器能在同一场景生成多条候选轨迹，但候选质量参差不齐；若能在去噪完成前就从模型内部状态判断哪条候选更优，便可避免对全部候选做完整去噪，从而同时改善输出质量与计算效率。

Method: 在 Alpamayo、ReCogDrive-IL、HDP 三个预训练规划器上，使用线性探针从中间去噪状态预测最终轨迹质量，并在留出场景上评估候选排序与选择性能；进一步分析读出信号对内部表示与读取器（reader）的依赖，以及选择能力与整体排序精度之间的关系；最终提出早选方法：在第一次去噪器评估后即完成候选选择，仅对被选候选执行后续去噪直至完成。

Result: 三个规划器均显示：第一次去噪器评估的状态已可支持有信息量的候选选择，而继续去噪并不能稳定提升选择质量；选择性能可与有限的整体排序精度并存，且读出效果取决于表示与读取器。早选方法相较对所有候选完整去噪降低了计算量，并在三个规划器上均优于随机选择的开环轨迹质量；其中 Alpamayo 在单相机与四相机配置下平均位移误差（ADE）分别降低 17.8% 与 13.4%。闭环评估亦显示更高的平均驾驶得分与行驶进度。

Conclusion: 扩散规划器的中间去噪状态蕴含可提前读出的轨迹质量信号，基于首次去噪评估的早选策略是一种有效的计算—质量折中方案，可在减少去噪计算的同时提升轨迹质量与闭环驾驶性能。

Abstract: Diffusion-based driving planners can generate multiple trajectories for the same scene, but these candidates vary in quality. Can their internal states reveal which candidate will be better before denoising is complete? We systematically study this question in three pretrained planners: Alpamayo, ReCogDrive-IL, and HDP, using linear probes to predict final trajectory quality from intermediate states and evaluating candidate ranking and selection on held-out scenes. Across all three planners, we observe that states from the first denoiser evaluation already support informative selection, while further denoising does not consistently improve selection quality. Further analyses show that readout depends on the representation and reader, and that selection can coexist with limited overall ranking accuracy. These findings motivate an early-selection method that selects after the first denoiser evaluation and completes only the chosen candidate. This reduces computation relative to fully denoising every candidate and improves open-loop trajectory quality over random selection across all three planners. In particular, it reduces Alpamayo's average displacement error by 17.8% and 13.4% in one- and four-camera configurations, respectively. Closed-loop evaluations also show higher mean driving scores and progress.

</details>


### [95] [PSK: A Matrix-Free Single-Loop Method for K-Level Physics-Based Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19660)
*Zherong Pan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文研究仅用梯度步骤能否替代隐式物理仿真中昂贵的 Hessian/KKT 线性求解，提出惩罚式单环 K 层方法 PSK，用于接触丰富约束下的物理控制，并给出复杂度、下界及多机器人导航实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于物理的控制通常需要对隐式物理步进行微分，但接触和碰撞约束使每步成为带约束能量最小化，传统方法需 Hessian 或 KKT 线性求解。作者希望回答：仅用梯度和投影是否足以完成控制优化；同时接触丰富约束常违反约束资格条件 CQ，需要处理该理论障碍。

Method: 将无约束情况下 K 步控制视为具有强凸下层的 K 层优化，并重写为一个 min-max 问题；提出 Penalized Single-loop K-level 方法 PSK，仅用梯度和投影、并行更新所有层。对违反 CQ 的接触约束，用恒定宽度带上的光滑墙替代每个约束，在无需 CQ 的温和假设下保证下层精确解可行且满足带状 Fritz-John，并保持 O(ε^{-3}) 复杂度。作者还分析收缩带宽下缺少一致 CQ 时的复杂度下界，并为平滑的运动学和动力学多机器人分段障碍导航证明假设。

Result: PSK 达到 ε-稳定控制需 O(ε^{-3}) 次迭代，指数与 K 无关但常数依赖 K。若没有随带宽一致成立的 CQ，收缩带会丢失 K 无关指数：带宽为 ε、固定 K≥2 时，确定性零尊重方法在控制维数随 1/ε 增长的困难实例上需 Ω(ε^{-(K+3)/4}) 次调用。导航实验中，逐案例调参的 PSK 用 16 层达到所有目标；相同时间下，在 18 个案例中的 17 个取得比调参展开更低的损失，而隐式微分在 10 个案例中更低。

Conclusion: 研究表明，在带状光滑墙和无需 CQ 的假设下，仅梯度单环方法可用于接触丰富物理控制并保持多项式复杂度；但带宽与一致 CQ 会影响复杂度指数，且实验性能与展开和隐式微分各有优劣。

Abstract: Physics-based control is central to robot learning, but differentiating through implicit physics steps typically requires Hessian or Karush--Kuhn--Tucker (KKT) linear solves; we ask whether gradient steps suffice. Each simulation step minimizes an energy under many contact and collision constraints. Without constraints, we view control over $K$ steps as $K$-level optimization with strongly convex lower levels, recast as one min--max problem. Our Penalized Single-loop $K$-level method (PSK) uses only gradients and a projection, updates all levels in parallel, and reaches an $\epsilon$-stationary control in $O(\epsilon^{-3})$ iterations, with a $K$-independent exponent but a $K$-dependent constant. Contact-rich constraints, however, typically violate a constraint qualification (CQ). We replace each constraint by a smooth wall that acts on a band of constant width. Under mild assumptions that need no CQ, every exact solution of the banded lower levels is then feasible and banded Fritz--John, and PSK keeps the $O(\epsilon^{-3})$ complexity. Without a CQ that is uniform in the band width, however, a shrinking band loses the $K$-independent exponent: with width $\epsilon$ and fixed $K\geq2$, deterministic zero-respecting methods need $\Omega(\epsilon^{-(K+3)/4})$ calls on a hard instance whose control dimension grows with $1/\epsilon$. We prove our assumptions for smoothed kinematic and dynamic multi-robot navigation among segment obstacles. In navigation experiments, PSK, tuned per case, reaches every goal with sixteen levels and, at equal time, reaches a lower loss than tuned unrolling in 17 of 18 cases, while implicit differentiation is lower in 10.

</details>


### [96] [Towards Probabilistically Safe Reinforcement Learning: Theory and Deployment on Humanoid and Aerial Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19616)
*Pol Mestres,Varun Gabbita,Jason H. K. Siu,Sergio A. Esteban,Hampus Serneke,Joel W. Burdick,Aaron D. Ames*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文扩展了 RSGF-RL 框架，使其支持一般轨迹级安全约束和带偏估计器，并能与 PPO 等深度 RL 算法结合，实现具有训练过程安全界和收敛保证的安全策略学习，并在人形机器人和无人机上完成零样本 sim-to-real 部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准强化学习无法保证策略满足安全约束，限制其在安全关键场景中的应用；现有可证明安全 RL 方法通常仅支持受限约束类型，且要求目标与约束函数的无偏估计，难以直接集成 PPO 等先进算法。

Method: 作者扩展 robust safe gradient flow-based reinforcement learning（RSGF-RL）框架，支持包括以指定概率避碰的概率约束在内的一般轨迹级安全约束，并允许价值和梯度估计存在偏差；理论分析给出训练全程约束违反的高概率上界及收敛性，并将 RSGF-RL 与 PPO 风格估计结合。

Result: 理论结果表明，约束违反由估计器偏差加上随 rollout 数增加而消失的项界定，若初始策略不安全则违反量衰减至该水平；迭代点收敛到原安全 RL 问题的近似 KKT 点。实验上，RSGF-RL 结合 PPO 风格估计实现了 Unitree G1 人形机器人全身控制策略的零样本 sim-to-real 部署，以及 Crazyflie 无人机的无碰撞航点导航。

Conclusion: 该工作将可证明安全 RL 扩展到更一般的约束和带偏估计场景，使其可与 PPO 等现代算法实用结合，并在真实机器人系统中验证了安全约束下的零样本迁移能力。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) enables robots to acquire complex skills, but standard RL formulations do not guarantee that learned policies satisfy safety constraints, which limits their use in safety-critical settings. Existing safe RL methods with provable guarantees typically support only restricted classes of constraints and require unbiased estimators of the objective and constraint functions, which prevents their direct integration with state-of-the-art algorithms such as proximal policy optimization (PPO). In this paper, we extend the recently introduced robust safe gradient flow-based reinforcement learning (RSGF-RL) framework to overcome these limitations. Our formulation accommodates a general class of trajectory-level safety constraints, including chance constraints such as collision avoidance with a prescribed probability, and our analysis allows for biased value and gradient estimators. We show that, with high probability, the constraint violation throughout training is bounded by the estimator bias plus a term that vanishes as the number of rollouts grows, and decays to this level if the initial policy is unsafe. We also establish the convergence properties of the iterates to approximate KKT points of the original safe RL problem. Finally, we show that RSGF-RL implemented with PPO-style estimates enables zero-shot sim-to-real deployment of whole-body control policies on a Unitree G1 humanoid and collision-free waypoint navigation on a Crazyflie drone.

</details>


### [97] [ResWAM: Learning What Changes, Not What Stays, for World-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19559)
*Chongyu Fan,Gaowen Liu,Ramana Rao Kompella,Jason Corso,Sijia Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出视觉残差学习与ResWAM：在冻结DINOv3特征空间监督当前帧到未来帧的特征变化，以更高效地增强VLA策略的世界动作建模能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界动作模型监督完整未来观测，但动作块内观测变化小，损失被静态背景复制主导，忽略机器人/物体运动；且依赖大视频生成模型或独立世界模型，训练与推理开销大，阻碍闭环部署。

Method: 将未来监督改为冻结DINOv3空间中的视觉残差，即仅预测当前帧到未来帧的特征变化；提出ResWAM，总参数2.3B，含0.5B双分支DiT，通过条件流匹配联合训练动作分支与残差分支。

Result: 无机器人预训练下，LIBERO-Plus平均成功率81.1%，RoboTwin 2.0达93.8%，RoboDojo达9.40%成功率和13.41分数；训练算力2.7-4.0 TFLOPs、推理1.0-1.4 TFLOPs；真实机器人Open/Put/Stack平均80.0%，较π0.5提升10.0%，延迟92.7 ms。

Conclusion: 监督未来特征残差比监督完整未来观测更聚焦任务相关运动，并提升表示多样性；ResWAM在低训练/推理算力下取得领先性能，验证了残差世界动作建模对策略学习与部署效率的有效性。

Abstract: World-action models (WAMs) augment vision-language-action policies (VLAs) with future prediction so that the policy can anticipate how its actions will affect the physical world. Existing WAMs supervise complete future observations. However, observations typically change little over an action chunk, so the future loss is dominated by copying static background content rather than learning task-relevant robot and object motion, limiting policy learning. Moreover, existing WAMs rely on large video generation models or separate world models, incurring heavy training and inference compute that hinders closed-loop deployment. To improve policy performance, we propose visual residual learning, supervising only the feature change from the current frame to the future frame in a frozen DINOv3 space. Compared with complete future features, this residual learning target yields a higher feature rank with richer and more diverse representations of scene changes, and spatially concentrates on the robot arm and manipulated objects rather than the background. To further improve computational efficiency, we present ResWAM, a residual world-action model with 2.3B parameters in total, including a lightweight 0.5B dual-branch DiT that jointly trains an action branch and a residual branch via conditional flow matching. Without robot pretraining (i.e., pretraining on external robot action trajectories beyond the benchmark demonstrations), ResWAM achieves top average success rates on LIBERO-Plus (81.1%), RoboTwin 2.0 (93.8%), and RoboDojo (9.40% success, 13.41 score) at the lowest training (2.7-4.0 TFLOPs) and inference compute (1.0-1.4 TFLOPs), and leads real-robot Open, Put, and Stack (80.0% average, +10.0% over $\pi_{0.5}$) at 92.7 ms latency.

</details>


### [98] [Cooperative Gradient Estimation for Multi-Robot Source Seeking with Heteroskedastic Measurements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19535)
*Alessandro Renzaglia,Lara Briñón-Arranz*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种异构协作移动机器人团队的三维标量场源定位方法，将按传感器噪声方差参数化的球面加权质心Voronoi编队与加权最小二乘梯度估计结合，实现噪声下的近最优鲁棒梯度估计并引导机器人趋向源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人测量存在噪声且各机器人传感器精度不同，导致异方差数据；需要在编队设计与估计中显式处理异构性，以稳健估计场梯度并定位三维标量场源。

Method: 各机器人测量所在位置场强并在团队内共享信息；在球面上构建由传感器噪声方差参数化的加权质心Voronoi tessellation编队；用加权最小二乘估计编队中心处场梯度；推导梯度估计协方差的闭式表达，给出球面上任意编队可达估计方差下界，并证明所提剖分渐近满足达到该下界的条件；将估计梯度用于梯度上升策略引导团队趋近源。

Result: 仿真结果表明该方法相较替代策略更有效，并在机器人故障时能够在线重构编队，体现出内在适应性和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 通过将异构传感精度同时纳入编队设计与梯度估计，该方法在噪声测量下实现近最优的梯度估计和三维标量场源定位，并具备在线重构与故障适应能力。

Abstract: This paper proposes a novel approach for localizing the source of a three-dimensional scalar field using a team of heterogeneous cooperative mobile robots. Each robot measures the field intensity at its location and shares the information within the team. Measurement noise is explicitly considered, and heterogeneity arises from robot-dependent sensing accuracies, leading to heteroskedastic data. The proposed framework combines a weighted centroidal Voronoi tessellation-based formation strategy, defined on a spherical surface and parameterized by sensor noise variances, with a Weighted Least Squares estimator to compute the field gradient at the formation center. By explicitly accounting for sensor heterogeneity in both formation design and estimation, the method achieves robust gradient estimation under noisy measurements. In particular, we show analytically that this construction is nearly optimal. The covariance of the gradient estimate is derived in closed form, providing a lower bound on the attainable estimation variance for any formation on the sphere, and the proposed tessellation is shown to asymptotically satisfy the conditions under which this bound is attained. The estimated gradient is then exploited within a gradient-ascent strategy to guide the team toward the source location. Simulation results demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed approach compared to alternative strategies and highlight its intrinsic ability to reconfigure online in response to robot failures, thereby enhancing its adaptability.

</details>


### [99] [Depth-Aware Path Planning and Collision Avoidance for Robotic Nursing Assistants Using Real-Time 3D Vision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19534)
*Peeyush Thakur,Dharmendra Sharma,Archit Sharma,Sandeep Gupta,Anuj Nandanwar,Laxmidhar Behera*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出面向机器人护理助手的深度感知路径规划与避障框架，融合RGB-D深度传感、规则避障与NavfnROS全局规划，在低矮及透明障碍物场景中提升导航安全性与成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统LiDAR导航对透明或低矮障碍物检测不足，纯视觉方法易受噪声和遮挡影响，难以满足护理机器人在杂乱环境中的安全导航需求。

Method: 构建RGB-D深度感知、规则式避障和NavfnROS全局路径规划相结合的多层架构，实现全局规划、感知融合与实时控制；在配备Kinova Gen3机械臂的Husky A200机器人上于ROS-Gazebo环境实现并测试。

Result: 仿真杂乱环境中，相比单传感器基线，成功率最高提升40%，最小安全间隙改善35%；真实环境实验验证了该方法计算高效且具备安全感知能力。

Conclusion: 该框架可作为自主医疗护理机器人的高效、安全感知导航方案，有效应对低矮和透明障碍物等复杂场景。

Abstract: This paper presents a depth-aware path planning and collision avoidance framework for the safe navigation of robotic nursing assistants in cluttered environments with low-height obstacles. Conventional LiDAR-based navigation systems, while accurate for mapping and localization, fail to detect transparent or low-height obstacles, whereas vision-based systems are prone to noise and occlusion. The proposed framework integrates RGB-D depth sensing, rule-based obstacle avoidance, and the NavfnROS global path planner to drive the robot from an initial point to a goal point. The proposed approach is implemented on the Husky A200 robot, equipped with a Kinova Gen3 arm, to form a complete setup for a robotic nursing assistant. This system features a multi-layer architecture for global planning, perception fusion, and real-time control in the ROS-Gazebo environment. Experimental evaluations in a simulated cluttered environment demonstrate up to 40\% higher success rate and 35\% improvement in minimum clearance compared with single-sensor baselines. Furthermore, real-world experimental results validate the proposed approach as a computationally efficient and safety-aware navigation solution for autonomous healthcare robots.

</details>


### [100] [Semantics First, Pixels Later: Mission-Driven Event Streams for Drone Video over Bandwidth-Starved Links](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19463)
*Cong Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出 BPS 行为基元流：在无人机端用检测器和跟踪器抽取多帧行为事件，优先传输短语义记录而非视频像素，以极低带宽让地面语言模型回答操作员问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation:  surveillance 无人机链路带宽极低，而操作员问题通常不是像素级，而是“几个人是否汇聚、何时、何地”等语义事件；因此需要研究在 1 Mbps 到 1 kbps 链路上应传什么、按什么顺序传。

Method: BPS 在机上运行检测与跟踪，按 16–125 字节任务卡筛选多帧事件，发送平均 25 字节的短事件记录，先于位置和图像传输；用地面侧语言模型根据流内容答题，在预注册协议和留出集上，与 H.265 视频经同一模型视觉微调读取、地面端对 H.265 运行自身感知、量化轨迹流和机上字幕对比。

Result: 开发集上 84% 问题关于汇聚且多为一地，事件流以 0.13 kbps 答对 74.3%，高于“什么都不收”的 57.4%、100 kbps 视频读取的 59.6% 和源帧的 64.7%；地面运行感知的视频在 100 kbps 才追平事件流，约为其 800 倍码率，64 kbps 及以下落后；轨迹流需 96 倍字节才追平，17 倍字节时低 0.03。留出集上效果变弱：用标注轨迹产生事件时事件流在 100 kbps 比 H.265 高 0.13，但用机上感知时仅高 0.01 [-0.04,+0.06]，且不比什么都不收更好。单独经模拟链路传输时，事件记录在 1 Mbps 答对 0.739，在 0.3 kbps 仍为 0.724；逐帧轨迹流可维持到 1 kbps，地面感知视频约需 100 kbps。Jetson Orin Nano 上未改 pipeline 仅 4.5 fps，漏掉多数事件。

Conclusion: BPS 表明“语义事件优先”在极低带宽下具有显著潜力，但优势依赖事件检测质量与机上感知可靠性；留出集上机上感知未显著优于基线，且实时性不足，说明该方法目前更适合作为低带宽语义传输方向，而非已稳健部署的方案。

Abstract: A surveillance drone on a bandwidth-starved link cannot send the video its operator would need, yet the operator's questions are often not about pixels: did three people converge, when, and where? We study what should cross a link of 1 Mbps down to 1 kbps instead, and in what order. BPS, a behavior-primitive stream, sends semantics first and pixels later: it runs a detector and a tracker onboard and sends short records (25 bytes on average) of multi-frame events selected by a mission card of 16-125 bytes, ahead of positions and pictures. We measure it by what a ground-side language model answers from it, under a preregistered protocol with a held-out set, against H.265 read by the vision fine-tune of the same model, H.265 on which the ground runs our own perception, a stream of quantized tracks, and onboard captions. On the development set (84% of questions about convergence, mostly one location) the event stream answers 74.3% of questions at 0.13 kbps. A reader that receives nothing answers 57.4%, and the video reader 59.6% at 100 kbps and 64.7% on the source frames. When the ground instead runs our perception on the video, video matches the stream at 100 kbps, about 800 times the stream's rate, and falls behind at 64 kbps and below. A track stream matches it at 96 times the bytes and loses 0.03 at 17 times. On held-out data the result is weaker: with events taken from annotated tracks, an oracle, the stream beats H.265 at 100 kbps (+0.13), but with onboard perception it does not (+0.01 [-0.04, +0.06]) and is no better than receiving nothing. Sent alone through emulated links the event records answer 0.739 at 1 Mbps and still 0.724 at 0.3 kbps; an every-frame track stream holds down to 1 kbps and video with ground-side perception down to about 100 kbps. On a Jetson Orin Nano the unchanged pipeline runs at 4.5 fps and misses most events.

</details>


### [101] [Long-Horizon Causal Inertial Odometry in Multistory Buildings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19387)
*Tianzi Zhang,Yan Wang,Hao Qiao,Jian Kuang,Xiaoji Niu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出LTCA长时程惯性里程计框架，用1 Hz运动token和60 s因果缓存维持电梯/扶梯等无步态激励时段的运动状态，并引入可插拔气压计分支及在线/回退条件训练，提升多层建筑室内3D定位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电梯/扶梯加速后长时间匀速巡航使步态激励消失、比力退化为恒定重力反作用，1–2 s窗口内瞬时速度不可观测；短窗口学习式惯性里程计输出趋向训练集条件均值，垂直位移被系统性低估。

Method: LTCA将每个1 s惯性窗口压缩为1 Hz运动token，并在有界60 s因果缓存上保持起始事件建立的运动状态；气压计在线时通过局部差分高度观测为垂直通道提供直接高程约束，训练时采用成对在线/回退条件训练，使气压计正则化两种模式共享表示，推理时可禁用气压计退化为纯惯性。

Result: 在覆盖9栋建筑的MSIO上，气压计在线时RoNIN/TLIO实例的ATE-3D在Seen/Unseen分别为2.83/4.18 m和2.44/5.12 m；推理时禁用气压计后同一权重纯惯性结果为3.24/4.28 m和2.64/5.23 m，仍优于所有原生短窗口基线；并在公开RoNIN和TLIO基准上验证。

Conclusion: 长时程因果建模与可插拔气压计条件训练可缓解短窗口惯性里程计在匀速长时段和垂直方向的退化，并在有/无气压计时均保持较强3D定位性能。

Abstract: In indoor 3D localization inside multistory buildings, an elevator or an escalator accelerates for a few seconds and then cruises at constant velocity for tens of seconds. Gait excitation disappears, the specific force degenerates into a constant gravity reaction, and instantaneous velocity becomes unobservable within a 1-2 s window. The output of short-window learned inertial odometry consequently degrades toward the conditional mean of the training set, and vertical displacement is systematically underestimated. We propose Local-Token Causal Attention (LTCA), a long-horizon architecture that compresses each 1 s inertial window into a 1 Hz motion token and sustains, over a bounded 60 s causal cache, the motion state established at the onset event. We further introduce a pluggable barometer branch that, when online, supplies a direct elevation constraint to the vertical channel through local differential height observations, and we train it with paired online/fallback conditional training so that the barometer also regularizes the representation shared by the two modes during training. On MSIO, our benchmark of nine buildings, the RoNIN and TLIO instances reach 3D trajectory errors (ATE-3D) of 2.83/4.18 m and 2.44/5.12 m on the Seen and Unseen splits, respectively, with the barometer online. With the barometer disabled at inference, the same weights fall back to pure inertial inference and reach 3.24/4.28 m and 2.64/5.23 m, still below every native short-window baseline. We further validate the framework on the public RoNIN and TLIO benchmarks.

</details>


### [102] [MARS: Dynamic Routing of Reinforcement-Learned Atomic Skills for Multi-Stage Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19324)
*Zequan Zeng,Junqin Yang,Kai Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出MARS模块化原子技能路由系统，将冻结的通用VLA策略与多个经强化学习训练的原子技能策略结合，并通过融合时序上下文与技能语义的动态路由器实现多阶段机器人操作中的策略协调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型在多任务机器人操作中表现良好，但在抓取、对齐、插入等关键阶段的控制精度和执行稳定性不足；需要兼顾通用操作能力与任务特定精细控制，并在多阶段任务中有效协调通用策略与专用策略。

Method: MARS保留冻结的通用VLA策略，集成多个独立训练的原子技能策略；以共享RL Token状态表示和VLA参考动作优化专用控制策略。高层策略选择采用动态路由器：利用Mamba循环编码和历史特征窗口捕捉操作状态演化，将候选策略的技能描述编码为语义查询，并通过注意力机制建立技能语义与时序状态特征的对应关系。

Result: 在四个LIBERO任务套件的固定轨迹评估中，MARS平均Macro-F1达90.47%，较MLP路由器高7.08个百分点；在仿真Tube Transport任务中，全任务成功率由20.04%提升至85.67%。

Conclusion: 阶段特定的强化学习与具备时序状态感知的动态策略路由能够提升多策略协调能力和多阶段机器人操作的整体执行性能。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have demonstrated strong capabilities in multi-task robotic manipulation. However, they still face limitations in control precision and execution stability during critical manipulation stages, such as object grasping, alignment and insertion. To combine general-purpose manipulation capabilities with task-specific fine-grained control, while enabling effective coordination between generalist and specialized policies throughout multi-stage manipulation tasks, this paper proposes MARS (Modular Atomic-skill Routing System), a dynamic routing framework for reinforcement learning-based atomic skills. MARS integrates a frozen generalist VLA policy with multiple independently trained atomic skill policies. Using a shared RL Token state representation and the reference action of VLA, specialized control policies are optimized through reinforcement learning for different cr`itical manipulation stages. For high-level policy selection, we develop a dynamic router that integrates temporal context and skill semantics. The router employs Mamba-based recurrent encoding and a historical feature window to capture state evolution during manipulation. Skill descriptions of candidate policies are encoded into semantic queries, and attention mechanisms are used to establish correspondences between skill semantics and temporal state features. Experimental results show that MARS achieves an average Macro-F1 score of 90.47% across four LIBERO task suites under fixed-trajectory evaluation, outperforming an MLP-based router by 7.08 percentage points. In the simulated Tube Transport task, the full-task success rate increases from 20.04% to 85.67%. These results demonstrate that stage-specific reinforcement learning and temporal state-aware dynamic policy routing can improve multi-policy coordination and overall task execution performance in multi-stage robotic manipulation.

</details>


### [103] [Residual Reinforcement Learning for Visual Servo Control with Zero-Shot Sim-to-Real Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19318)
*Mahdi Nobar,Alisa Rupenyan,John Lygeros,Jürg Keller*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 在传送带上用Franka Emika Panda机械臂跟踪运动物体：在数字孪生不精确、测量误差与噪声存在时，将逆雅可比PI控制器与Soft Actor-Critic学习的残差策略结合，并用延迟补偿卡尔曼滤波融合乱序相机测量，实现低于模型误差性能界的跟踪精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决机械臂在传送带动态跟踪中受不精确数字孪生、测量误差和噪声影响的问题，并量化不确定运动学模型失配下基于模型控制器的最小可达误差。

Method: 提出环形逆雅可比PI控制器加残差策略的架构，残差策略在数字孪生中通过Soft Actor-Critic学习；采用延迟补偿卡尔曼滤波融合乱序相机测量，提供延迟感知的运动目标位置估计；通过频域灵敏度分析推导一类基于模型控制器的性能界并进行对比。

Result: 仿真与硬件实验表明残差策略使跟踪误差低于性能界；在27.9–41.8 mm/s传送带速度范围内，平均跟踪误差由基线逆雅可比PI的1.1–1.4 mm降至0.3–0.7 mm。

Conclusion: 在有限保真度仿真器中学习残差修正并结合延迟补偿，可在建模与测量不确定性下显著提升基于模型的控制性能，同时保持可靠的sim-to-real迁移。

Abstract: We address tracking a moving object on a conveyor with a Franka Emika Panda robot arm, despite an imprecise digital twin, measurement errors, and noise. Our novel approach augments a loop-shaped inverse-Jacobian PI controller with a residual policy learned via Soft Actor-Critic in the digital twin. A delay-compensated Kalman filter fuses out-of-sequence camera measurements to provide latency-aware moving target position estimates. We compare the achieved performance to a performance bound for a class of model-based controllers derived from a frequency-domain sensitivity analysis, quantifying the minimum achievable error imposed by uncertain kinematic model mismatch. Simulation and hardware experiments indicate that our residual policy drives tracking error below the bound. Across conveyor speeds (27.9-41.8 mm/s), our method reduces mean tracking errors from 1.1-1.4 mm (baseline inverse-Jacobian PI) to 0.3-0.7 mm. These results show that learning residual corrections in a limited-fidelity simulator, combined with delay compensation, can substantially improve model-based control under modeling and measurement uncertainties while maintaining reliable sim-to-real transfer.

</details>


### [104] [VLN-Flash: Parallel Action Decoding with Adaptive Execution for Vision-and-Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19287)
*Ruizhe Xie,Honghua Chen,Binglin Wang,Tong Guo,Xu Tang,Puti Yan,Shaoxiong Cai*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: VLN-Flash 是一种免训练解码器，通过单次前向并行评估完整动作前缀树，并依据分数间隔自适应决定执行步数，从而加速视觉语言导航策略的动作解码并提升成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归动作生成需要等待一串依赖预测，导致机器人动作间等待时间长；而小的离散动作词表使并行评估短时域动作计划成为可能。

Method: 在单次前向中评估完整动作前缀树，利用分支特定注意力和匹配的 token 位置，在精确算术下保持原策略对每个前缀的条件动作分布；根据每层逐步贪心前缀与其最强替代方案的分数间隔决定执行多少动作，然后执行所选深度下联合分数最高的计划再重新观测。

Result: 在匹配输入下，VLN-Flash 在 Uni-NaVid 和 StreamVLN 上较原生自回归实现近 3 倍动作解码加速；在 R2R-CE 和 RxR-CE 上提升成功率和 SPL，成功率增益最高达 2.7 个百分点；在 Unitree GO2 上提升导航成功率，并在扣除网络传输时间后将动作间平均等待缩短约 160 ms。

Conclusion: VLN-Flash 通过并行前缀树解码和基于分数间隔的自适应执行，在不重新训练策略的情况下实现了更快、更有效的视觉语言导航动作解码。

Abstract: When a vision-and-language navigation policy generates an action sequence autoregressively, the robot waits for a chain of dependent predictions. A small discrete action vocabulary makes it possible to score all short-horizon plans in parallel. We introduce VLN-Flash, a training-free decoder that evaluates the complete action-prefix tree in a single forward pass. Branch-specific attention and matched token positions preserve the original policy's conditional action distribution for each prefix in exact arithmetic. We use the score margin between the stepwise-greedy prefix and its strongest alternative at each depth to decide how many actions to execute. The robot then executes the plan with the highest joint score at the chosen depth before observing again. On matched inputs, VLN-Flash achieves a nearly $3\times$ speedup in action decoding over native autoregression on Uni-NaVid and StreamVLN. Compared with the native autoregressive decoders, it improves success rate and success weighted by path length (SPL) on R2R-CE and RxR-CE, with success gains up to 2.7 percentage points. On a Unitree GO2, VLN-Flash improves navigation success and cuts the mean wait between actions by about 160 ms after subtracting network transmission time.

</details>


### [105] [HospitalBench: Diagnosing Where and Why Embodied Agents Fail on Long Hospital Task Chains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19274)
*Gaytri Jena,Kapil Wanaskar,Aman Chadha,Vasu Sharma,Vinija Jain,Amitava Das*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 HospitalBench：在 NVIDIA Isaac Sim 中诊断长时程具身任务的医院家务基准，通过连续因果子任务链暴露端任务成功率无法反映的失败模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程具身任务常在中途失败，但仅看最终任务成功率无法定位问题；现有基准多将子任务并排放置，缺少前一步状态决定后一步动作的因果依赖。

Method: 包含 10 项医院家务，每项先有 2 个不计分热身步骤，随后在单次连续仿真中执行 5 个计分子任务组成的因果链；视觉语言智能体通过技能级工具基于相机图像行动；脚本机器人仅调用模型可用接口，用于验证任务可解性；评估链条断点、失败调用后的恢复行为及自身位置估计漂移。

Result: 仅报告 15/60 计划运行的首批结果（3 个高投入模型，T1、T10 各两个初始状态及 T3 一个初始状态各一次），并在 3/10 项家务上给出脚本机器人证明；15 次运行中 2 次通过全部 5 个子任务；第 1 至 5 个子任务后仍在运行的数量分别为 15、8、4、2、2；189 次失败调用中，168 次后续调用不同，111 次后续调用未失败。

Conclusion: 结果为初步结果而非完整基准；作者将发布场景、任务链、脚本机器人、评估工具和轨迹。

Abstract: Long-horizon embodied tasks fail in ways that end-task success cannot see. HospitalBench is a diagnostic benchmark of ten hospital chores in NVIDIA Isaac Sim, each two unscored warm-up steps followed by a causal chain of five scored sub-tasks in one continuous simulation: the state one step leaves (an object pose, a choice, a fact seen once) determines what the next step must do, unlike benchmarks that place sub-tasks side by side. Vision-language agents act through skill-level tools from camera images. A scripted robot that makes only the calls a model gets is the solvability check of a chore. From each trajectory the benchmark reads where a chain breaks, what the agent did after a failed call, and how far its own position estimate drifts from the true one. This version reports first results only: 15 of 60 planned runs (three models at high effort, one run each on chores T1 and T10 from both start states and T3 from one), with scripted-robot proofs on three of the ten chores. Of the 15 runs, 2 passed all five sub-tasks. Runs still going after sub-tasks 1 to 5 were 15, 8, 4, 2, 2. Of 189 failed calls, 168 were followed by a different call and 111 by a call that did not fail. These are first results and not a full benchmark. We will release the scenes, task chains, scripted robot, evaluation harness and trajectories.

</details>


### [106] [Physics-Informed Causal Reasoning for Nonstationary Robot Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19251)
*Jungjin Lee,Se Jin Kim,Sung Kwon On,Insoon Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出物理信息因果推理（PICR），将基于近期交互推断的潜在上下文与固定的物理信息网络结合，仅更新上下文即可让机器人无需重训练或手工模型适应变化的物理条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推理动作后果需要内化系统动力学，但相同快照下的系统可能因隐藏物理条件而对同一动作响应不同；上下文模型未说明上下文如何改变运动方程，物理信息神经网络（PINN）虽嵌入方程却只描述单一系统且无法识别系统变化。

Method: PICR桥接二者：用从近期交互重新推断的上下文调节一个固定物理信息网络，由该网络确定机器人惯量和势能；只更新上下文，不重训练网络，也不依赖手工指定的机器人模型，并支持替代力矩下的反事实查询。

Result: 在NVIDIA Isaac Sim的Franka Panda机械臂上，PICR在变化及未见负载下预测运动，未见负载误差较基线降低1.6-3.8倍；分析表明仅由交互推断的上下文编码负载物理属性、跟踪其变化并控制机械臂响应，且上下文诱导的重力和惯量项在各拉格朗日分量上与仿真器一致。

Conclusion: PICR表明交互推断的上下文具有物理有效性，可编码并跟踪隐藏物理条件，使机器人在不重训练的情况下实现自适应动力学预测与反事实推理。

Abstract: Reasoning about what an action will cause requires internalizing how a system behaves. As conditions seldom stay fixed, this must often happen within a few interactions. Yet systems that look identical in a snapshot can respond differently to the same action, depending on physical conditions the snapshot hides. Context-based models capture this separation by summarizing recent transitions into a latent context, but do not specify how that context should change the equations of motion. Physics-informed neural networks build those equations into the model, but describe only a single system and cannot recognize when it has changed. We present Physics-Informed Causal Reasoning (PICR), which bridges the two. Only a context, re-inferred from recent interactions, is updated; it conditions a fixed physics-informed network that determines the robot's inertia and potential energy, so the robot adapts to new physical conditions from its own experience without retraining or a hand-specified robot model. On a Franka Panda arm in NVIDIA Isaac Sim, PICR predicts motion under changing and unseen payloads, reducing error on the latter by 1.6-3.8x over baselines, and extends to counterfactual queries under alternative torques. Our analysis shows that the context, inferred solely from interaction, encodes payload physical properties, tracks their changes, and governs the arm's response. The gravity and inertia terms induced by the context further demonstrate its physical validity, each agreeing with the simulator at the level of individual Lagrangian components.

</details>


### [107] [Continuous Lower-Limb Joint-Angle Estimation from sEMG and IMU: The Conditional Value of Cross-Activity and Cross-Subject Data under Update-Matched Controls](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19238)
*Jiaying Xu,Guangyuan Chen,Buyun Gao,Zhen Dong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该文在更新量匹配的对照下分解用户自身其他活动数据与他人同活动数据对下肢关节角度估计的增益，揭示数据价值具有层级性、状态依赖性和方向性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 意图驱动外骨骼控制需要对新用户提供连续、因果的关节运动学估计，但新用户训练数据来源选择常被简化为数据更多或更少的比较，混淆了源数据信息量与优化更新量，并可能以平均指标掩盖负迁移。

Method: 基于公开数据集10名受试者、6种移动活动的高密度sEMG与IMU，估计髋、膝、踝角度；冻结紧凑的残差因果TCN-GRU解码器，采用更新量匹配的回放对照，将每个新增数据源与用户自身数据上相同数量的更新进行等价比较。

Result: 用户自身其他活动将R²由0.722提升至0.788，其中+0.048为回放之外的额外信息；下楼梯是可靠的有害供体，坐-站-走是可靠的有益供体。仅有目标活动时，他人同活动数据增益G_T=+0.053；拥有自身其他活动数据后减半为G_W=+0.028；充分校准后，供体价值在一到两条目标记录后迅速消失。两个来源的逐目标增益相关，留一受试者门控仅在低校准条件下恢复大部分跨受试者价值。

Conclusion: 数据价值是分层、状态依赖且方向性的；关键瓶颈是在无测试标签时判断何时进行迁移，而非单纯增加数据量。

Abstract: Intent-driven exoskeleton control needs a continuous, causal estimate of the wearer's joint kinematics and an answer to a second question: whose data should train the estimator for a new user?
