Borrowing the user's recordings of other activities, or other users' recordings of the same activity, is usually evaluated as "more data" versus "less data," which confounds the information a source carries with the optimizer updates it adds and hides negative transfer behind averages. We separate these effects for hip, knee, and ankle angle estimation from high-density sEMG and an IMU across six locomotion activities on all ten participants of a public dataset. A compact residual causal TCN-GRU decoder is frozen, and an update-matched replay control prices every added source against the same number of updates on the user's own data. A user's own other activities raise $R^2$ from 0.722 to 0.788, of which +0.048 is information beyond replay; stair descent is a reliably harmful donor and sit-stand-walk a reliably beneficial one. Other users' same-activity data help while the user has only the target activity ($G_\mathrm{T} = +0.053$) and half as much once own other-activity data exist ($G_\mathrm{W} = +0.028$); at full calibration, donor value collapses after one to two target records. Per-target gains from the two sources correlate, and leave-one-participant-out gates recover most of the cross-subject value only at low calibration. Data value is hierarchical, state-dependent, and directional; the bottleneck is deciding, without test labels, when to transfer.

</details>


### [108] [Self-Calibrating Vision-Agent-Action: Deploying Pick-and-Place to New Robot Cells without Demonstrations or Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19198)
*Jae Hun Hwang,Yechan Seo,Hong-In Won*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: VAA 是一种无需机器人示范或微调的仿真抓放智能体，通过预训练视觉语言/语言模型与自校准几何测量，在夹爪、相机几何和基座坐标系变化下显著优于八个 LIBERO 微调的 VLA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉-语言-动作模型（VLA）对机器人硬件、相机几何、外观和基座坐标系变化敏感，通常依赖示范或微调；论文旨在实现无需机器人示范和微调的零样本适应。

Method: VAA 使用预训练视觉语言模型和语言模型解释指令，通过自身运动与指尖测量命令映射和相机几何，估计镜头畸变，并基于测量到的物体深度规划抓取；采用受控实验改变机器人硬件、相机几何、外观和基座坐标系，并与八个公开 LIBERO 微调 VLA 比较，辅以配对消融验证自校准、镜头估计和深度管线。

Result: 在含夹爪或基座变化的 18 个单元中，冻结 VAA 成功 230/360（64%），精化 VAA 成功 268/360（74%），最强已发布 VLA 仅 76/360（21%）；配对消融支持自校准组合、复合变化下镜头估计和测量深度管线；在携带物体状态的两步 LIBERO-Goal 链中，VAA 在变化单元完成 54/60，而四个评估 VLA 完成 0。

Conclusion: 结果表明，基于预训练模型和自校准几何测量的 VAA 能在机器人本体与传感器配置变化下保持较强抓放能力，尤其在夹爪/基座变化和需要状态传递的多步任务中显著优于微调 VLA。

Abstract: In simulation, Vision-Agent-Action (VAA) performs pick-and-place in robot cells with gripper or base-frame changes without robot demonstrations or fine-tuning. It uses pre-trained vision-language and language models for interpretation, measures the command map and camera geometry from its own motion and fingertips, estimates lens distortion, and plans grasps from measured object depth. A controlled study of eight public LIBERO-fine-tuned vision-language-action models (VLAs) varies robot hardware, camera geometry, appearance, and base frame. Gripper and frame changes dominate their losses in the tested fractional design. Across 18 cells with these changes, frozen VAA achieves 230/360 successes (64%), the refined VAA 268/360 (74%), and the strongest released VLA on this subset 76/360 (21%). Paired ablations support the self-calibration bundle, lens estimation under compounded changes, and the measured-depth pipeline. On a two-step LIBERO-Goal chain with object state carried between instructions, VAA completes 54/60 chains in changed cells, while four evaluated VLAs complete none.

</details>


### [109] [Ego2Sim2Humanoid: Agentic Real-to-Sim for Learning Loco-Manipulation VLAs without Robot Demonstrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19196)
*Chen Liu,Nikolaus Correll*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种将第一视角眼镜录制的人类演示转换为经物理验证的机器人训练轨迹的数据生成流程，并用其微调GR00T N1.7，在Unitree G1上实现自主移动操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统视觉运动策略的模仿学习依赖遥操作机器人演示，成本高且需要操作员实时控制物理机器人和穿戴式追踪硬件；第一视角眼镜虽可直接采集人类演示，但仅追踪头部和视野内手部，且因体型、臂展和手部形态差异，直接复制人类动作难以产生可行接触。

Method: 该方法将第一视角眼镜记录转换为机器人训练轨迹：首先通过智能体式real-to-sim阶段，从RGB-D和头部流重建碰撞与纹理视觉资产，必要时补全未见表面，并预测被操作物体上的目标接触带；在运动适配中，抓取规划器将接触带转化为可行的机器人腕部位姿与手指闭合，双层站姿优化规划保持手部目标可达的无碰撞站立姿态，全身运动生成器合成下肢移动与视野外手臂运动；随后两阶段运动修正以运动学方式融合可见性切换，并在物理仿真中利用智能体设计的语义奖励训练强化学习跟踪策略，使物体离开相机视野时仍能完成操作阶段；最后沿优化轨迹通过机器人机载相机渲染重建场景，得到同步的机器人视角图像、本体感受和已验证动作标签。

Result: 仅使用该转换数据集微调GR00T N1.7，不使用真实机器人演示，模型即可在Unitree G1上自主执行移动操作任务。

Conclusion: 该工作降低了机器人模仿学习对昂贵遥操作演示的依赖，展示了将无机器人、无手持夹爪的第一视角人类演示转化为物理可行机器人训练数据的可行路径，并验证了转换数据用于训练自主移动操作策略的有效性。

Abstract: Imitation learning for visuomotor policies typically relies on teleoperated robot demonstrations, which are costly because every episode requires a trained operator controlling a physical robot in real time through teleoperation interfaces and wearable body-tracking hardware. Egocentric glasses capture human demonstrations without a robot or handheld grippers, yet they track only the head and in-view human hands, and directly copying human motion to a robot with different stature, arm reach and hand morphology fails to produce feasible contacts. \method{} converts egocentric glasses recordings into physically verified robot training trajectories. An agentic real-to-sim stage reconstructs collision and textured visual assets from the recorded RGB-D and head streams, completes unseen surfaces when needed, and predicts target contact bands on the manipulated objects. During motion adaptation, a grasp planner turns each contact band into a feasible robot wrist pose and finger closure, bilevel stance optimization plans collision-free standing postures that keep hand targets reachable, and a whole-body motion generator synthesizes lower-body locomotion and out-of-view arm motion. Two-stage motion correction then blends visibility transitions kinematically and trains a reinforcement-learning tracking policy in physics simulation under agent-designed semantic rewards, completing manipulation phases even when the object leaves the camera view. Rendering the reconstructed scene through the robot's onboard camera along the optimized rollout yields synchronized robot-view images, proprioception and verified action labels. Fine-tuned exclusively on this converted dataset, without real-robot demonstrations, GR00T N1.7 performed loco-manipulation tasks autonomously on a Unitree G1. Project page: this https URL

</details>


### [110] [Distilling Safety Filters for Filter-free Multi-agent RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19190)
*Srikar Babu Gadipudi,Somayeh Sojoudi,Claire J. Tomlin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出将集中式CBF安全过滤器蒸馏进多机器人RL策略的方法，使策略在无过滤器、仅用局部观测部署时仍保持安全，并给出约束违反与碰撞概率的理论界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多机器人共享空间RL训练依赖试错，真实机器人难以承受碰撞；CBF安全过滤器虽可纠正不安全动作，但团队中约束数随智能体数量二次增长，且过滤器会掩盖不安全动作代价并排除近碰撞状态，影响策略学习。

Method: 学习仅来自已执行安全动作的数据，采用implicit Q-learning；在合成的近碰撞状态上将CBF过滤器蒸馏进策略。理论证明蒸馏损失可界定策略约束违反程度及安全裕度越界幅度，避免误差累积；当蒸馏状态覆盖部署状态时，还可逐步界定碰撞概率。

Result: 无过滤器且仅用局部观测时，多智能体1,800个测试回合无碰撞；3智能体训练的策略以恒定单智能体计算量零样本迁移到20智能体团队，并在最多8智能体时保持无碰撞；在Crazyflie四旋翼上验证最多5架同时飞行。

Conclusion: 将安全过滤器蒸馏进策略可实现可扩展、去中心化部署的安全多机器人RL，兼具理论安全界与真实机器人实验验证。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) can teach robot teams to navigate shared spaces, but trial and error causes collisions that real robots cannot afford. Control barrier function (CBF) safety filters prevent these collisions by correcting unsafe actions, yet in a team the filter becomes a centralized optimization whose number of constraints grows quadratically with the team size. We propose an approach that builds the filter's safety into the RL policy and examine how such a system behaves during filter-free deployment. However, the filter that keeps training safe also hides what the policy must learn: it masks the cost of unsafe actions, which actor-critic methods then overvalue, and it keeps near-collision states out of the training data. Our approach therefore learns only from executed safe actions with implicit Q-learning and distills the filter into the policy at synthetic near-collision states. We prove that filter distillation loss bounds the policy's constraint violation and hence how far agents can cross their safety margin, so errors never accumulate. When distillation states cover deployed states, the loss also bounds the collision probability at every step. Without a filter and acting only on local observations, our policies have no collisions in 1,800 test episodes with multiple agents. A policy trained with three agents transfers zero-shot to teams of twenty at constant per-agent computation and stays collision-free with up to eight agents. We test our policies on Crazyflie quadrotors with up to five flying at the same time.

</details>


### [111] [ImpedanceMimic: Learned Impedance as Action Space for Force-Accurate Motion Imitation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19153)
*Matthias Jammot,Pembe Gizem Özdil*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出ImpedanceMimic：一种仅需运动输入即可推理的强化学习策略，通过从运动与力测量中学习逐关节刚度、阻尼和位置设定点，解决固定增益PD控制在运动模仿中运动跟踪与力跟踪的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从运动反推力是病态问题，不同控制器可产生相同运动但不同接触力；现有基于物理的运动模仿依赖固定增益PD控制，而接触与摆动相对关节刚度和阻尼需求冲突，导致运动与力跟踪难以兼顾。

Method: ImpedanceMimic用强化学习策略根据运动和力测量调制每关节刚度、阻尼与位置设定点，但推理阶段只需运动；将增益表示为惯性缩放先验的倍数，使单一策略无需显式人体测量输入即可控制受试者特异性生物力学模型。

Result: 在129名参与者和六项活动中，ImpedanceMimic在运动学与力保真度上优于匹配的固定增益PD（含直接扭矩、肌肉驱动及现有模仿控制器等多种集成方案），接近专用运动到力回归器；并可零样本迁移到未见人类和人形机器人。

Conclusion: 学习到的阻抗构成跨身体共享的低维运动状态表示，可泛化到未参与训练的活动：刚度随负载增大，阻尼提前于卸载进行调节。

Abstract: Recovering the forces behind a motion is ill-posed: different controllers can reproduce the same movement while generating different contact forces. Physics-based motion imitation resolves this ambiguity through simulation, but relies on fixed-gain proportional-derivative (PD) control. We show that fixed gains impose a trade-off between motion and force tracking, as contact and swing place conflicting demands on joint stiffness and damping. We introduce ImpedanceMimic, a reinforcement-learning policy that modulates per-joint stiffness, damping, and position setpoints from motion and force measurements, but requires only motion at inference. Across 129 participants and six activities, ImpedanceMimic outperforms matched fixed-gain PD under every integration scheme, direct-torque, muscle-actuated and existing imitation controllers in kinematic and force fidelity, approaching a dedicated motion-to-force regressor. By expressing gains as multiples of inertia-scaled priors, a single policy controls subject-specific biomechanical models without explicit anthropometric input and transfers zero-shot to unseen humans and a humanoid robot. The learned impedance forms a low-dimensional representation of motor state, shared across bodies, that generalizes to withheld activities: stiffness scales with load, and damping anticipates unloading.

</details>


### [112] [SuperGlance: Driving at a Glance with a Vision-Language Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19138)
*Jieyuan Pei,Xin Yan,Mingwei Xu,Haiyi Zhang,Siyu Zou,Zhangyi Hu,Zhuoxuan Li,Yuguang Yang,Rongjunchen Zhang,Cheng Chi,Hui Xiong,Yan Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SuperGlance利用解码器式VLM在生成首token前的隐状态，通过线性头对固定候选轨迹打分，将VLA转为单次前向的编码器式驾驶策略，在保持视觉问答能力的同时显著加速规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA逐步解码规划耗时超过1秒，难以满足System 1式快速反应；而将快速System 1置于VLM之外又会丢失视觉语言模型的场景理解与问答能力。作者希望利用VLM写首token前已形成的场景隐状态，实现既快又保留VLM能力的驾驶策略。

Method: SuperGlance读取VLM首token前的隐状态，用线性头对5,273条固定候选轨迹打分。候选集固定使奖励可离线计算，从而支持奖励加权监督目标和最大化精确期望奖励的强化学习。训练前及策略训练中引入多选问答训练，并要求同一问题在两种选项顺序下均答对以抵抗捷径；同时用KL项约束各阶段模型靠近起始模型，防止漂移。

Result: SuperGlance的视觉多选题表现与基座VLM解码相当；规划仅需84 ms GPU时间，比Qwen-Drive-1.0快6倍，并在NAVSIM、PAI-AV和WOD-E2E上至少不差。AlpaSim闭环中，两规划器在最高300 ms延迟下每有责事故里程保持，超过后下降；将GPU时间计为延迟时，SuperGlance相对Qwen-Drive-1.0的领先从2.8倍增至3.9倍。延迟1.2 s时，其表现低于Qwen-Drive-1.0在其自身518 ms下的表现。

Conclusion: 结果表明，VLM首token前的隐状态足以支撑驾驶与场景问答，单次前向可实现高性能System 1策略，并为VLA提供兼顾效率与视觉语言能力的可行路径。

Abstract: Autonomous driving is turning to vision-language-action models (VLAs). They answer and plan by decoding, one step at a time. Each step is another forward pass, so a plan can take over a second of GPU time. Embodied AI often pairs such a slow, reasoning System 2 with a fast System 1 that acts. Human drivers plan at a glance, with System 1. Faster designs usually put System 1 outside the vision-language model (VLM), in a planner without its abilities. Yet before a VLM writes its first token, it has already read the scene into one hidden state. We show that, once trained, this hidden state is enough to drive and to answer questions about what the model sees. SuperGlance reads it to turn a decoder-only VLM into an encoder-style driving policy. Its linear head scores a fixed set of 5,273 candidate trajectories. With the set fixed, rewards are computed offline, so supervised targets can be reward-weighted and reinforcement learning maximizes the exact expected reward. Before and during policy training, multiple-choice training keeps the VLM's visual abilities in one pass, so the policy still answers questions about driving scenes. Training questions are built against shortcuts. Each must be answered right in two option orders. Against drift, a KL term keeps each stage near its starting model. One pass offers VLAs a more plausible path to System 1. SuperGlance answers visual multiple-choice questions as well as its base VLM does by decoding. It plans in 84 ms of GPU time, six times faster than Qwen-Drive-1.0, and at least as well on NAVSIM, PAI-AV and WOD-E2E. In AlpaSim closed loop, km per at-fault incident holds for both planners up to a 300-ms delay and drops beyond. With GPU time charged as delay, SuperGlance's lead over Qwen-Drive-1.0 grows from 2.8 to 3.9 times. Delayed by 1.2 s, our model falls below Qwen-Drive-1.0 at its own 518 ms.

</details>


### [113] [Wireless Edge-Assisted Adaptive Latent Reasoning for Vision-Language-Action Models in Physical AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19131)
*Fatih Temiz,Melike Erol-Kantarci*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出无线边缘辅助的自适应潜在推理框架WEALR，通过联合选择执行位置与潜在推理收敛阈值，在真实无线信道下平衡VLA任务成功率、推理时延与能耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型将视觉与语言指令映射为机器人动作，但实时控制受限于车载算力；潜在推理可提升隐藏表示而无需显式CoT令牌，却带来任务成功、时延和能耗之间的权衡，且需考虑真实通信条件。

Method: 构建无线边缘辅助的自适应潜在推理框架，将执行位置和潜在推理收敛阈值联合决策建模为约束马尔可夫决策过程，采用拉格朗日约束深度强化学习方法TRPO-Lag进行求解，并结合LIBERO操作任务、实测VLA推理开销和射线追踪无线信道评估。

Result: 实验表明，TRPO-Lag相比基准CDRL方法最高实现23%更高的可行性加权任务成功率，并能在变化无线条件与任务需求下平衡任务性能与资源消耗。

Conclusion: 边缘辅助的自适应潜在推理与约束强化学习联合优化可有效支持VLA模型在真实无线场景下的高效协同推理。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models enable embodied artificial intelligence (AI) by translating visual observations and language instructions into robotic actions. Emerging use cases in physical AI are expected to drive the need for efficient inference and communication, as the computational demands of the VLA models pose challenges for real-time control with limited onboard resources. Test-time scaling through latent reasoning allows these models to refine hidden representations without generating explicit chain-of-thought (CoT) tokens, but introduces a trade-off between task success, inference latency, and energy consumption. In this paper, we address the efficient collaborative inference under a realistic communication scenario. We propose a wireless edge-assisted framework for adaptive latent reasoning (WEALR) that augments onboard computation with edge computing. A decision-making agent jointly selects the execution location and latent-reasoning convergence threshold. We formulate this joint adaptation as a constrained Markov decision process (CMDP). A Lagrangian-constrained deep reinforcement learning (CDRL) approach, namely trust region policy optimization Lagrangian (TRPO-Lag), learns to balance task performance and resource consumption under varying wireless conditions and task requirements. We evaluate the framework using LIBERO manipulation tasks, profiled VLA inference costs, and ray-traced wireless channels. Experimental results demonstrate that TRPO-Lag achieves up to 23% higher feasibility-weighted task success rate compared to benchmark CDRL methods.

</details>


### [114] [Leaning Over to Reach Far-Away Objects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19100)
*Allen Devaraj,William Xie,Nikolaus Correll*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该工作用基于采样的全身模型预测控制，让Unitree H1-2人形机器人借助环境支撑完成伸展、支撑、抓取与恢复，从而把可达工作空间向外扩展约50 cm。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人常规全身控制通常避碰或仅被动适应接触，但放置于远处桌面上的物体需要机器人主动利用接触支撑来稳定身体并扩展可达范围。

Method: 采用基于采样的全身MPC统一处理倾斜、支撑、伸展和恢复，仅依赖局部感知与基准标记；控制器在笔记本上以34 Hz实时运行，并实现从仿真到真机的迁移。

Result: 在6个目标、3种桌面高度共54次试验中，到达目标成功率为89%，完整“伸展-恢复”循环成功率为81%；相比无支撑站立工作空间，可达距离增加约50 cm，负载姿态可维持超过4分钟直至位姿估计漂移。分析表明，采样动作的时间持续性和探索尺度显著影响成功，CEM在规划频率与模型失配下具有最宽的工作区域。

Conclusion: 该研究建立了在线、接触丰富的人形机器人操作的真实世界基线，并说明小规模实时采样预算足以支撑此类全身支撑伸展任务。

Abstract: We study supported reaching to extend a humanoid robot's workspace by bracing against the environment for stabilization using a straightforward yet capable whole-body model predictive control (MPC) approach, exclusively with local sensing and fiducial markers. While typical solutions for whole body humanoid control aim at avoiding or accommodating contact, contact is explicitly required to reach objects placed on surfaces far out of normal reach. We employ sampling-based MPC for whole-body leaning, bracing, reaching, and recovery on a Unitree H1-2 humanoid. The controller runs in real-time at 34 Hz on a laptop, transfers sim-to-real, extends reach by about 50 cm beyond the unbraced standing workspace, and sustains the loaded posture for over four minutes, before pose estimation drifts. The controller reaches the commanded target in 89% of trials and completes the full reach-and-recovery cycle in 81%, across 54 trials spanning six targets and three table heights. We further characterize why such a small real-time sampling budget is sufficient, finding that temporal persistence of sampled actions and exploration scale strongly govern success, while CEM provides the widest operating region over planning rate and model mismatch. These results establish a real-world baseline for online, contact-rich humanoid manipulation. Code, videos, and full hardware details are available at this https URL

</details>


### [115] [RoboCoEvo: Expanding Robot Capabilities through Agent-Policy Co-Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19081)
*Ran Qi,Yixin Xiao,Dawei Wang,Zhiyuan Li,Jinbang Huang,Xuan Zhao,Yintao Ma,Yangzheng Wu,Tongtong Cao,Yingxue Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 RoboCoEvo，一种以失败引导的协同演化框架，将 MLLM 智能体的可执行知识精炼与 VLA 等机器人基础模型的参数学习耦合起来，实现智能体与模型相互促进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 MLLM 智能体与机器人基础模型的集成系统改进大多是解耦的，智能体或模型单方面进步未必提升整体系统能力，限制了组合系统的潜力；因此需要二者协同演化。

Method: RoboCoEvo 采用 code-as-policy 形式，让智能体生成并精炼可复用的失败检测与恢复程序，产生有针对性的纠正经验；再通过后训练将这些纠正行为固化到机器人基础模型的端到端策略中；更新后策略的失败分布又用于指导智能体程序的下一轮修改。

Result: 在 BEHAVIOR-1K、RoboCasa 和 RoboTwin 上的实验表明，随着协同演化轮次增加，自主 VLA 任务性能持续提升。

Conclusion: 将智能体可执行知识精炼与机器人基础模型参数学习闭环耦合的失败引导协同演化，可缓解组件解耦问题，并持续增强自主机器人任务执行能力。

Abstract: Integrating MLLM-based agents with robotic foundation models has attracted increasing attention as a means of enhancing robotic capabilities. However, the improvement of such integrated systems remains largely decoupled across their agent and model components, with advances in one component not necessarily benefiting the other, substantially limiting the capabilities of the combined system. As both MLLMs and robotic foundation models become increasingly capable, fully realizing their combined potential calls for co-evolution, an iterative process in which improvements in agents and robotic foundation models reinforce one another. We propose RoboCoEvo, a failure-guided co-evolution framework that couples the refinement of the agent's explicit and reusable executable knowledge with parameter learning in robotic foundation models, such as vision-language-action (VLA) models. Through a code-as-policy formulation, the agent develops and refines executable failure-detection and recovery programs, generating targeted corrective experience. Post-training consolidates the resulting corrective behaviors into the robotic model's end-to-end policy, improving autonomous task execution. Changes in the updated policy's failure distribution, in turn, guide subsequent revisions to the agent's programs. Experiments on BEHAVIOR-1K, RoboCasa, and RoboTwin demonstrate sustained improvements in autonomous VLA task performance across successive co-evolution rounds.

</details>


### [116] [Certify, Then Select: Viability-Aware Runtime Steering of Frozen Diffusion Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19047)
*Angelo Moroncelli,Loris Roveda*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出CAST：在测试时从冻结的扩散策略中采样少量动作块，先用障碍条件认证安全性，再由学习到的价值集成模型对已认证动作块排序并执行最优者；并扩展到CAST-V/CAST-VL以保持策略的安全控制权威、减少一步认证看不到的死胡同。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散策略可从示教中学习机器人技能，但部署时会遇到示教中未包含的约束，且为每个约束重新训练不现实。现有安全过滤器只修改动作而忽略任务，较大修改可能使策略进入无法继续的状态；仅认证当前动作块也不足以保证后续仍有安全动作。

Method: CAST从冻结策略采样若干动作块，对每个动作块用障碍条件进行认证，并执行经学习价值集成模型排序最高的已认证动作块。理论证明：无论critic如何，已认证步骤安全；只要存在已认证样本，执行动作块与可行条件下策略的差距在log N nats内，而安全过滤器投影可能奇异；精确闭环恒等式将排序增益分解为可用性、分布范围和critic目标。CAST-V通过策略条件化的可行性保持安全控制权威，可用带有限样本保证的模型估计或从控制器自身日志学习（CAST-VL）。

Result: 在robomimic操作任务、平面四旋翼、MuJoCo人形和独轮车上，使用冻结策略时，CAST在几乎全部设置中把联合任务与约束成功率提高到输出过滤器之上；当过滤器修改较大时，先认证再排序尤为关键；可行性机制消除了大部分一步认证无法发现的死胡同。

Conclusion: CAST为冻结扩散策略提供了一种可认证的测试时动作选择框架，通过先认证后排序并结合闭环可行性保持，在满足部署约束的同时提升任务成功率和安全性。

Abstract: Diffusion policies, which generate short action sequences by denoising random noise, are a leading way to learn robot skills from demonstrations. Once deployed, they meet constraints the demonstrations never contained, and retraining for each is impractical. A safety filter edits the policy's action to satisfy them, but the edit ignores the task, and a large edit can leave the policy where it cannot continue. We propose CAST (Certified Action Selection at Test time): draw a few action chunks from the frozen policy, certify each with a barrier condition, and execute the certified chunk that a learned value ensemble ranks highest. We prove that a certified step is safe whatever the critic does; that, whenever a certified sample exists, the executed chunk stays within log N nats of the policy conditioned on feasibility, while a safety-filter projection can be singular to it; and that an exact closed-loop identity splits the gain of ranking into availability, spread and the critic's target. Certifying the next chunk is not enough: a generative controller can have safe actions available now and none later. We characterise the safe control authority of a frozen policy under finite sampling, and CAST-V preserves it through a policy-conditioned viability, estimated with a model under a finite-sample guarantee or learned from the controller's own logs (CAST-VL). On robomimic manipulation, a planar quadrotor, a MuJoCo humanoid and a unicycle, with frozen policies, CAST raises joint task-and-constraint success above the output filter in nearly every setting; certifying before ranking is decisive where a filter's edit is large, and viability removes most dead ends that one-step certification cannot see. Project page: this https URL .

</details>


### [117] [Refinement vs. Replanning: Inference Compute Allocation in Robot Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19034)
*Alba Maria Tellez Fernandez*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该文研究冻结的分块机器人策略在不更新权重时，如何联合分配部署期推理算力：是在每次策略调用内增加去噪细化，还是提高重规划频率；并在 SmolVLA+LIBERO 上给出受控案例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分块策略的部署期算力可花在“每次调用内细化动作块”或“更频繁调用以基于新观测重规划”上，两者耦合；需要同时评估生成努力与反馈频率，并以算术量和时延等不同成本口径衡量，而不能只比较名义计算率。

Method: 提出不更新模型权重的评估流程：同时报告成功率和名义计算率/完整尝试成本；在 discovery 任务上选择比较，在 held-out 任务上做预设比较，事后比较仅总结观察模式。案例使用一个冻结 SmolVLA checkpoint、同步 LIBERO 仿真，以去噪步数 d 作为细化控制，固定块长 K=50，并比较不同 h 设置。

Result: d=10 时 82.1% 的计数算术与去噪无关，而固定输入主机时间的仿射拟合将 17.7% 归于截距；h=2 时 d=2 成功 139/160，d=5 为 122/160（+10.6 个百分点，任务条件 95% 区间 +4.4 至 +16.9），且每控制步算术少 5.9%。d=2 下 h=5 与 h=2 为 138/160 对 139/160，每次尝试算术低 60.7%，但未建立等价性。

Conclusion: 部署自适应必须联合评估生成努力和反馈频率，并以多种成本口径报告；该案例说明不同算力分配可显著改变成功率与成本，但不定义通用最优设置。

Abstract: A frozen chunked robot policy can spend deployment-time inference compute in two coupled ways: more work within each policy call to refine a predicted action chunk, or more frequent policy calls to replan from fresh observations. We study this allocation without updating model weights. Our evaluation procedure reports success with both nominal compute rate and complete attempt cost, selects comparisons on discovery tasks, and tests prespecified comparisons on held-out tasks. Comparisons added after inspecting the held-out results only summarize observed patterns. We demonstrate the procedure with one frozen SmolVLA checkpoint in synchronous LIBERO simulation, using denoising count as the refinement control and fixing chunk length at K=50. Arithmetic and latency characterize cost differently: at d=10, 82.1% of counted arithmetic is independent of denoising, whereas a descriptive affine fit to fixed-input host time assigns 17.7% to its intercept. At h=2, d=2 succeeds on 139/160 attempts versus 122/160 for d=5 (+10.6 percentage points; task-conditional 95% interval: +4.4 to +16.9), while using 5.9% less arithmetic per control step. Executing h=5 rather than h=2 at d=2 yields 138/160 versus 139/160 successes and 60.7% lower arithmetic per attempt; equivalence is not established. The case study shows why deployment adaptation must evaluate generation effort and feedback frequency jointly; it does not define a universal setting.

</details>


### [118] [Robot Demonstration Videos Should Include Transparency Disclosures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18982)
*Nozomi Nakajima*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文通过六国大规模预注册实验发现公众难以辨别机器人演示视频的视觉真实性与行为真实性，并提出披露干预与披露标准以校准公众预期。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人与具身AI受物理和资金限制，公众主要依赖精选演示视频了解进展，但视频常省略关键信息，可能扭曲公众对机器人能力的认知。

Method: 开展N=9,341、六国、预注册的大规模实验，检验公众对视觉真实性（真实拍摄 vs CGI）和行为真实性（自主 vs 遥操作）的辨别能力，并基于行为科学设计披露干预。

Result: 公众难以辨别演示视频的视觉与行为真实性；信息省略导致高估机器人完成任务和导航真实环境的能力；在视频中加入清晰披露可显著提升辨别力和对机器人能力的理解。

Conclusion: 应采纳机器人演示视频披露标准，以准确校准公众预期并促进机器人研究的透明度。

Abstract: This paper argues that the robotics and AI community should adopt disclosure standards for demonstration videos. As physical and financial constraints limit direct public access to embodied AI, public dissemination of progress in robotics is mainly through curated demonstration videos rather than direct experience with the technology. In a large-scale (N=9,341), pre-registered experiment across six countries, we find that the general public struggles to discern both the visual authenticity (e.g., distinguishing real footage from CGI) and behavioral authenticity (e.g., distinguishing autonomy from teleoperation) of robot demonstration videos. The omission of these details distorts public perception, leading people to overestimate the ability of robots to perform tasks and navigate real world environments. Using evidence from behavioral science, we design a disclosure intervention and demonstrate that the inclusion of clear disclosures in robot demonstration videos significantly improves discernment of visual and behavioral authenticity, as well as understanding robots' capabilities. We propose the adoption of disclosure standards for robot demonstration videos to accurately calibrate public expectations and promote transparency in robotics research.

</details>


### [119] [TMA: Sim-to-Real Forceful Humanoid Manipulation via Tactile-Driven Motor Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18976)
*Yiting Chen,Kelin Yu,Sarthak Kaingade,Bryan Trinh,Kaiyu Hang,Roberto Martin-martin,Nima Fazeli,X. Alice Wu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出TMA（Tactile-Driven Motor Adaptation）与全身系统，实现零样本sim-to-real的 forceful humanoid manipulation，以圆形门把手开启为基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有类人loco-manipulation多关注几何主导交互，而 forceful multi-finger manipulation 需应对真实接触与交互动力学不确定性，如稳定摩擦抓握、无滑移扭矩生成、力控推压与全身稳定。

Method: TMA围绕触觉可观测交互组织策略学习，以锥体几何作为多指接触表示归纳偏置，并基于触觉历史在线适应运动行为；将上肢操作策略与预训练通用locomotion控制器联合，实现灵巧交互与全身稳定协同，全部在仿真中训练。

Result: 零样本迁移至配备BrainCo Revo2多指手的Unitree G1人形机器人，在四种商用圆形门把手上显著优于标准RMA和反应式触觉策略基线。

Conclusion: 触觉驱动在线运动适应结合全身控制可有效支撑 forceful multi-finger humanoid manipulation 的零样本sim-to-real迁移，圆形门把手开启是具挑战性的基准任务。

Abstract: Recent humanoid loco-manipulation in articulated environments has largely focused on geometry-dominant interactions, where success is primarily determined by geometric alignment and motion execution. In contrast, forceful multi-finger manipulation requires the robot to adapt to real-world uncertainties in contact and interaction dynamics. We present Tactile-Driven Motor Adaptation (TMA) and an integrated whole-body system for zero-shot sim-to-real forceful humanoid manipulation, and study round-doorknob opening as a challenging benchmark requiring stable multi-finger frictional grasping, sufficient torque generation without slip, force-regulated pushing, and whole-body stability. TMA structures policy learning around interactions observable through tactile feedback, uses cone geometry as an inductive bias for multi-finger contact representation, and adapts motor behavior online from tactile history. An upper-body manipulation policy is trained alongside a pretrained general locomotion controller to coordinate dexterous interaction and whole-body stabilization. Trained entirely in simulation, TMA transfers zero-shot to a Unitree G1 humanoid with BrainCo Revo2 multi-fingered hands and substantially outperforms standard Rapid Motor Adaptation (RMA) and reactive tactile policy baselines across four commercial round doorknobs in the real world.

</details>


### [120] [Optimal Operating-Point Selection for Single-Linearization Trajectory Planning of Nonlinear Robot Manipulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18935)
*Bhanuka Dayawansa,Rohan Munasinghe*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种离线性能驱动的单点线性化位置选择方法，将线性化工作点作为优化变量，通过网格搜索与有界细化选取使非线性轨迹目标最小的候选点，从而在保持固定仿射规划模型的同时提升轨迹规划性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单点线性化计算高效，但其规划性能依赖所选线性化点；传统初始点、中点或终点线性化未必最优，因此需要系统选择工作点以改善非线性机械臂轨迹规划效果。

Method: 从共同的三次种子轨迹中采样候选工作点，对每个候选点建立仿射模型并求解相同的约束有限时域二次规划（QP）；将所得控制序列作用于原始非线性动力学，以非线性轨迹目标为评价指标，通过网格搜索和有界细化选择最优工作点。

Result: 在三自由度UR5机械臂的四个点到点运动上，最优归一化工作点位于0.325至0.935之间，体现轨迹依赖的选择；与表现最好的传统初始、中点或终点线性化相比，所提方法在四个任务中将非线性轨迹目标分别降低17-18%、21-22%、37-38%和7-8%。在代表性任务上加入相同PID反馈校正后的非线性执行仍保持性能优势。

Conclusion: 系统化的单点线性化工作点选择可显著改善单线性化轨迹规划性能，同时保留固定的仿射规划模型。

Abstract: Single-point linearization provides a computationally efficient basis for optimization-based trajectory planning of nonlinear robot manipulators. However, planning performance depends on the selected linearization point. This paper presents an offline performance-driven approach that treats the location of a single linearization as an optimization variable. Candidate operating points are sampled from a common cubic seed trajectory, and each corresponding affine model is used to solve an identical constrained finite-horizon quadratic program (QP). The resulting control sequences are executed on the original nonlinear dynamics, and the operating point minimizing the nonlinear trajectory objective is selected through grid search and bounded refinement. Evaluation on four point-to-point motions of a three-degree-of-freedom UR5 manipulator yields optimal normalized operating-point locations between 0.325 and 0.935, demonstrating trajectory-dependent selection. Compared with the best-performing conventional initial, midpoint, or terminal linearization, the proposed approach reduces the nonlinear trajectory objective by 17-18%, 21-22%, 37-38%, and 7-8% across the four tasks, respectively. Additional nonlinear execution with identical PID feedback correction confirms that the performance advantage persists for a representative task. These results demonstrate that systematic operating-point selection can substantially improve single-linearization trajectory planning while preserving a fixed affine planning model.

</details>


### [121] [Correcting Persistent Failures in Point-Cloud Grasping Policies using Human Demonstrations in Virtual Reality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18919)
*Achyuthan Unni Krishnan,Karthik Rengarajan,Bharath Narasimhan,Chris von Csefalvay*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种人在回路的失败样本挖掘与VR示范方法，用少量人类抓取点示范补强点云强化学习抓取策略在持续失败案例上的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 家庭、仓库和农场中的机器人需抓取未见物体，通常没有3D模型或类别标签；仅依赖深度点云的抓取策略可直接利用观测几何，但Contact-GraspNet等离线生成器按学习到的合理性排序，而非按实际能否抬起物体排序。强化学习可直接优化抓取成功，但仍会持续失败于特定抓取情形。

Method: 构建人在回路失败闭环：自动挖掘RL策略持续失败的抓取情形，发送到VR应用，由人用手完成抓取，并作为两个抓取点示范返回训练。人类只提供“抓哪里”，机器人自行选择接近方式。

Result: 仿真中，RL将未见物体抓取成功率从Contact-GraspNet的55%提升到83%。加入58个人类示范后，RL持续失败的困难案例成功率从24%提升到45%，接近翻倍；并在未示范的相似案例上恢复RL单独训练所损失的性能，同时不降低未见物体上的成功率。

Conclusion: 针对RL持续失败模式的人类抓取点示范能有效补足纯点云强化学习抓取策略的短板，在困难案例和相似案例泛化上带来提升，且不牺牲总体未见物体性能。

Abstract: Robots in homes, warehouses and farms must pick up objects they have never seen, usually without a 3D model or a category label. A grasping policy that works directly on the point cloud from a depth sensor needs only the geometry the robot observes, so it applies to any new object. Offline grasp generators such as Contact-GraspNet work this way, but they rank grasps by learned plausibility rather than by whether the grasp actually lifts the object. Reinforcement learning (RL) can optimize for that directly and make a point-cloud grasping policy much better on average, yet it leaves specific grasps persistently unsolved. We present a human-in-the-loop failure loop that targets those grasps: situations an RL policy keeps failing are mined automatically, sent to a virtual-reality application where a person picks the object up with their own hands, and returned to training as two grasp points. The person contributes only where to grasp; the robot chooses its own approach. In simulation, RL raises success on unseen objects from 55% with Contact-GraspNet to 83%. Adding 58 human demonstrations nearly doubles success on the hard cases RL kept failing, from 24% to 45%, and recovers performance that RL alone loses on similar cases that were never demonstrated, without reducing success on unseen objects.

</details>


### [122] [Passage-Focused Soft Coordination for Hierarchical Multi-Robot Navigation in Bottleneck Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18877)
*Haoze Dong,Stepan Dergachev,Meng Guo,Konstantin Yakovlev,Zhongkui Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种以通道（passage）为核心的多机器人导航框架，将软全局协调与灵活局部执行相结合，在长时域协作与执行偏差鲁棒性之间取得平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 刚性全局调度对执行偏差敏感，而纯局部方法缺乏长时域协调能力，因此需要一种兼具全局协作与局部灵活性的导航方法。

Method: 构建由凸集构成的通道诱导图，支持带优先级的规划与两级软协调；通道感知路径选择考虑通道使用与通行方向，路径受限的连续时空规划惩罚时间冲突；生成的参考轨迹引导模型预测路径积分控制器在线避碰，并通过自适应时间与几何跟踪应对执行偏差，选择性重规划在无需刚性轨迹预留的情况下维持协调。

Result: 在4种环境、5–30个机器人的1200个实例中取得接近完美的成功率，并在makespan和flowtime上优于所评估基线；8机器人硬件实验全部完成且无碰撞。

Conclusion: 结果表明，以通道为中心的协调能够有效兼顾长时域合作与灵活执行，提升多机器人导航的成功率与效率。

Abstract: In multi-robot navigation, rigid global schedules are sensitive to execution deviations, while local methods lack long-horizon coordination. We propose a passage-focused framework combining soft global coordination with flexible local execution. A passage-induced graph of convex sets supports priority-ordered planning with two levels of soft coordination. Passage-aware route selection accounts for passage usage and traversal directions, while route-restricted continuous space-time planning penalizes temporal conflicts. The resulting references guide a model predictive path integral controller that avoids collisions online. Adaptive temporal and geometric tracking accommodates execution deviations, while selective replanning maintains coordination without rigid trajectory reservations. Across 1,200 instances in 4 different environments with 5-30 robots, the framework achieves a near-perfect success rate and outperforms evaluated baselines in makespan and flowtime. Hardware experiments with 8 robots demonstrate successful completion of all trials without collisions. These results highlight the effectiveness of passage-focused coordination in balancing long-horizon cooperation with flexible execution.

</details>


### [123] [Technical Report of Auriga's 1:10 Scale Autonomous Vehicle Robot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18855)
*Marya Sabouri Dodaran,Ali Taheri Garakani,Mandana Shabanzadeh Nasrollah-Abadi,Yousef Ashrafi,Mohammad Mahdi Azimi,Erfan Nekouei Ghahfarokhi,Amirhossein Kheiri Holighi,Seyyed Sobhan Hosseini Hajibakandeh,Matin Jamshidi,Amirhossein Gholizadeh Behbahani,Mohammad Hossein Moaiyeri*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该报告介绍 Shahid Beheshti 大学 Robotics and Intelligent Automation Laboratory 设计的 1:10 比例自动驾驶车辆，采用自定义机械底盘、ESP32 分布式嵌入式系统和 Jetson Orin NX，实现感知、决策与轨迹控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建一个模块化、可靠的研究平台，用于结构化城市类场景下的自动驾驶研究与未来扩展。

Method: 平台集成自定义机械底盘、基于 ESP32 的分布式嵌入式层和 Jetson Orin NX 高层计算模块；嵌入式层负责传感器接口、实时数据采集和执行器控制，Jetson 通过深度学习进行车道线、标志和交通灯检测，并完成决策与轨迹控制；系统通过结构化 UART 链路通信，软件栈采用 ROS、模块化固件设计以及 Stanley、PID 和 Pure Pursuit 控制算法。

Result: 该系统在类城市结构化驾驶场景中实现了稳定的自主导航，并表现出良好的鲁棒性与可靠性。

Conclusion: 该 1:10 自动驾驶车辆可作为模块化研究平台，支撑后续自动驾驶研究与应用拓展。

Abstract: This report presents the design and implementation of Auriga Robotics' 1:10 scale autonomous vehicle, developed at Shahid Beheshti University's Robotics and Intelligent Automation Laboratory. The platform integrates a custom mechanical chassis, a distributed embedded system based on ESP32 microcontrollers, and a Jetson Orin NX module for high-level perception and control. The embedded layer manages sensor interfacing, real-time data acquisition, and actuator control, while the Jetson handles environment perception through deep-learning--based lane, sign, and traffic light detection, as well as decision-making and trajectory control. System communication is achieved via structured UART links, ensuring synchronized operation between perception and control subsystems. The vehicle's software stack incorporates ROS, modular firmware design, and multiple control algorithms---Stanley, PID, and Pure Pursuit---to achieve stable autonomous navigation in structured driving scenarios. The overall system demonstrates robust performance and reliability in urban-like environments, serving as a modular research platform for future autonomous driving studies.

</details>


<div id='cs.CR'></div>

# cs.CR [[Back]](#toc)

### [124] [STA: A Split Temporal Adversary Method for Evaluating Robustness of Multimodal RL-based Driving Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20765)
*Shenghao Xiong,Xiaolin Chang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出分割时序对抗攻击STA，在预算约束与策略感知下联合优化攻击时机和模态选择，以评估多模态强化学习自动驾驶策略的时序鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态强化学习自动驾驶依赖跨模态时序对齐，微小错位或延迟即可破坏融合表征并导致决策失败；现有诱导跨模态时序不一致的对抗攻击在通用性、真实性和自适应性上仍有限。

Method: 将多模态时序攻击建模为统一决策过程，联合优化攻击时机与模态选择；包含统一多模态时序扰动机制，通过历史状态延迟观测而无需模态特定噪声，以及分割多模态对抗决策机制，利用 victim policy 的干净动作识别敏感决策模式并自适应优化；同时给出分布偏移理论分析和模态选择原则。

Result: 在CARLA中，STA相对PPO将最强基线碰撞率提高一倍以上，且攻击尝试减少17.8%；并可泛化到基于VLM的驾驶策略。

Conclusion: 时序一致性是多模态自动驾驶策略的关键脆弱点，时序鲁棒性对安全自动驾驶至关重要。

Abstract: Multimodal reinforcement learning (MRL) offers a promising paradigm for autonomous driving by jointly leveraging complementary sensor observations to learn effective decision-making policies. However, its effectiveness critically depends on temporal alignment across modalities, making temporal inconsistency a fundamental vulnerability. Even subtle misalignment or latency can corrupt fused representations and ultimately lead to decision failures. Existing studies have explored adversarial attacks to induce cross-modal temporal inconsistencies, thereby enabling systematic evaluation of the temporal robustness of MRL policies. However, existing methods remain limited in generality, realism, and adaptivity. To address these limitations, we propose the Split Temporal Adversary (STA), which formulates multimodal temporal attacks as a unified decision process that jointly optimizes attack timing and modality selection under realistic policy-aware and budget-constrained settings. STA includes two mechanisms: a) a unified multimodal temporal perturbation mechanism that delays observations via historical states, removing the need for modality-specific noise; b) a split multimodal adversarial decision mechanism that jointly optimizes attack timing and modality selection through adaptive optimization while leveraging the victim policy's clean actions to identify sensitive decision patterns. We provide a theoretical analysis of the induced distribution shift and a modality selection principle. Experiments in the CARLA simulator show that STA more than doubles the collision rate of the strongest baseline against PPO while using 17.8\% fewer attack attempts, and that it generalizes to a VLM-based driving policy. These results reveal temporal consistency as a critical vulnerability, highlighting temporal robustness as essential for safe autonomous driving.

</details>


<div id='eess.SY'></div>

# eess.SY [[Back]](#toc)

### [125] [Data-Driven Predictive Control for Autonomous Vehicle Racing under Varying Friction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21378)
*Julius Beerwerth,Julius Tischbein,Bassam Alrifaee*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文研究在自动驾驶赛车中，将不同路面摩擦条件下记录的数据池化后用于数据驱动预测控制（DPC），能否在无需显式摩擦估计的情况下保持预测与控制精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DPC依赖与当前工况匹配的输入-输出轨迹数据；但赛车中轮胎-路面摩擦随赛道分段和轮胎组变化，若初始轨迹与记录数据摩擦不匹配，预测精度会下降。显式保证摩擦匹配需要记录时已知摩擦并在线估计当前摩擦，因此作者希望评估数据池化能否作为一种隐式适应手段。

Method: 作者比较使用当前摩擦下记录数据（匹配）、不同摩擦下记录数据（不匹配）以及两种摩擦混合数据（池化）的DPC预测精度，并在仿真和Cyber-Physical Mobility Lab（CPM Lab）小尺度车辆实验中评估多步预测均方根误差及闭环赛道参考线跟踪误差，同时与非线性模型预测控制（NMPC）和匹配数据DPC对比。

Result: 池化数据将多步预测RMSE保持在匹配数据的1.5倍以内，而不匹配数据使其增大2.2至3.4倍。闭环跟踪中，仿真里池化数据DPC与NMPC相当（13.7 cm对13.3 cm）；CPM Lab中池化数据DPC接近匹配数据DPC，低摩擦轮胎相差0.1 cm（1.6 cm对1.5 cm），高摩擦轮胎相差0.2 cm（0.9 cm对1.1 cm）。

Conclusion: 使用跨摩擦条件池化数据的DPC能够对摩擦变化提供部分适应能力，在不采用专门适应方法的情况下保持较好的预测精度和闭环跟踪性能。

Abstract: Data-driven predictive control (DPC) predicts future outputs by combining recorded input-output trajectory data such that the combined trajectory approximately matches a measured initial trajectory and tracks a provided reference. This paper investigates to what extent and under which conditions DPC using data pooled across operating conditions retains the prediction accuracy of DPC using data from the current operating condition. In autonomous vehicle racing, the tire-road friction varies between racetrack sections and tire sets. If the initial trajectory and the recorded data originate from different frictions, prediction accuracy of DPC degrades. Ensuring the frictions match requires knowing the friction during recording and estimating the current friction explicitly. Instead, we pool data recorded at different frictions. We compare the prediction accuracy of DPC using data recorded under the current friction (matched), a different friction (mismatched) and data pooled from both frictions. We evaluate in simulation and small-scale vehicle experiments in the Cyber-Physical Mobility Lab (CPM Lab). Our results show that pooled data keep the multi-step root-mean-square prediction error within 1.5 times that of matched data, whereas mismatched data increase it by a factor of 2.2 to 3.4. To assess how this prediction accuracy carries over to control, we evaluate the tracking error from the raceline in closed loop. In simulation, DPC with pooled data tracks comparably to nonlinear model predictive control (13.7 cm versus 13.3 cm) and close to DPC with matched data in the CPM Lab, differing by 0.1 cm on the low-friction tires (1.6 cm versus 1.5 cm) and by 0.2 cm on the high-friction tires (0.9 cm versus 1.1 cm). DPC thus provides partial adaptation to varying friction, without a dedicated adaptation method.

</details>


### [126] [Course-Referenced Lateral Control of Trail-Following Convoys: String Stability and Adaptive Feedforward under Load Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20320)
*Nitesh Kumar,Sixu Li,Swaroop Darbha*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 该文研究无车道线（如雪覆盖道路）场景下车辆队列沿前车GNSS轨迹循迹时的横向误差传播，提出以轨迹方向为航向参考的弧长参数化方法，分析误差有界条件，并给出消除曲线上稳态偏移的自适应律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 车队中后车以前车GNSS轨迹为参考，前车跟踪误差会进入后车参考，导致横向误差沿队列累积；同时载荷与轮胎刚度变化会造成曲率前馈失配和曲线稳态偏移。

Method: 将循迹写成以弧长为自变量的航向参考表述，使每辆跟随车以拟合轨迹方向作为航向参考，从而把误差传播简化为单一传递函数；推导联合使用领航车与前车轨迹时误差对任意队列长度有界的条件，并分析仅跟随前车时的误差累积；将载荷和轮胎刚度变化归结为曲率前馈中的单一系数，提出在曲线上估计该系数的自适应律以消除偏移。

Result: 证明联合领航车与前车轨迹可在任意队列长度下保持误差有界，而仅跟随前车时即使无放大也会在道路末端转向或横移时使误差无界累积；载荷与轮胎刚度失配经单一系数造成曲线稳态偏移，自适应律可估计该系数并消除偏移；十一车队列仿真验证了分析。

Conclusion: 采用领航车与前车轨迹融合的航向参考可保证长队列循迹误差有界，且通过单系数自适应补偿可抑制参数变化引起的曲线稳态偏移。

Abstract: Vehicles in a convoy can steer along the GNSS trails of the vehicles ahead when lane markings are unavailable, as on snow covered roads. Since each vehicle's tracking error enters the reference of the next, lateral errors can grow along the convoy. This paper presents a course referenced formulation of trail following in arc length, in which each follower takes the direction of its fitted trail as its heading reference. Error propagation then reduces to a single transfer function. We derive a condition under which combining the trails of the leader and the predecessor keeps the errors bounded for any convoy length, and show that following the predecessor alone lets the errors accumulate without bound, even without amplification, on roads that end turned or shifted sideways. Passenger and cargo loads and tire stiffness vary in practice, and we show that they enter the curvature feedforward through a single coefficient, whose mismatch leaves steady offsets on curves. We propose an adaptive law that estimates this coefficient on curves and removes the offsets. Simulations of an eleven vehicle convoy confirm the analysis.

</details>


### [127] [A Geometric Observer for Visual-Inertial Odometry using Optical-Flow Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20098)
*Tarek Bouazza,Soulaimane Berkane,Tarek Hamel*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 提出一种由单目视觉-惯性里程计中逐特征光流约束驱动的几何观测器：对每个跟踪点，用其方位向量及旋转补偿后的导数构造与深度无关的齐次速度约束，并在重力增广的 SE_3(3) 群上以 Riccati 增益引入新息，无需估计深度或重建运动方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统单目视觉惯性里程计通常需要估计特征深度或显式重建运动方向，增加了状态维数与耦合复杂度；本文希望直接利用光流约束构造几何观测器，并厘清速度—重力误差的可观测性与收敛条件。

Method: 对每个跟踪点，利用 bearing 及其旋转补偿的导数得到关于机体坐标系速度的深度无关齐次约束；将这些约束通过 Riccati 增益纳入增广群 SE_3(3)（含重力状态）上的观测器新息；引入辅助重力状态使六维机体速度—重力误差动力学与姿态校正解耦；证明该误差的一致可观测性等价于由惯性系 bearing 及其导数构成的矩阵的持续激励；在此基础上给出速度与重力误差的全局一致指数收敛，并结合基于重力的姿态校正得到速度、重力与约化姿态误差的几乎全局渐近稳定及局部一致指数稳定。

Result: 仿真验证了持续激励条件、初始误差恢复能力以及对测量扰动的敏感性；在持续激励条件下，机体速度与重力误差全局一致指数收敛，加入基于重力的姿态校正后组合误差几乎全局渐近稳定并在局部一致指数稳定。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，仅凭逐点光流约束即可在无需深度估计与运动方向重建的情况下驱动几何观测器；速度—重力误差的一致可观测性可由惯性系 bearing 与其导数的持续激励刻画，为单目视觉惯性里程计提供具有明确收敛保证的观测器设计框架。

Abstract: This paper presents a geometric observer for monocular visual-inertial odometry driven by individual optical-flow constraints. For each tracked point, the bearing and its rotationally compensated derivative give a depth-free homogeneous constraint on the body-frame velocity. These constraints enter the innovation of an observer on the gravity-augmented group $\mathbb{SE}_3(3)$ through a Riccati gain, without estimating depths or reconstructing the direction of motion. The auxiliary gravity state makes the six-dimensional body-frame velocity-gravity error dynamics independent of the attitude correction, and their uniform observability is shown to be equivalent to persistent excitation of a matrix formed from the inertial bearings and their derivatives. Under this condition, the body-frame velocity and gravity errors converge globally and uniformly exponentially, and a gravity-based attitude correction renders the combined velocity, gravity, and reduced-attitude error almost globally asymptotically stable and locally uniformly exponentially stable. Simulations illustrate the excitation condition, recovery from initial errors, and sensitivity to perturbed measurements.

</details>


### [128] [Representing Differential Inclusions as Monads: A Framework for Compositional Controller Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19469)
*Ahmad Ahmad,Kevin LEahy*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 该文用轨迹单子与Kleisli范畴将安全认证控制器的顺序组合归约为单一代数条件：所有控制器属于对单子乘法封闭的公共子单子即可保证组合安全。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有混合与非光滑机器人系统中CBF控制器的顺序组合多按分段处理，缺乏统一代数判据，也难以解释不同屏障组合方式的安全差异。

Method: 为Filippov正则微分包含构造轨迹单子，将控制器视为Kleisli态射、顺序执行视为轨迹拼接；把CBF前向不变性认证对应为子单子，并用公共子单子及其乘法封闭性刻画组合安全性；对逐相位执行推导失效率的闭式Bernoulli模型，在3000个随机实例上验证，并与单QP同时约束所有屏障的基线比较；在四模式分段仿射系统和异构地形上的独轮车降阶模型上实例化。

Result: 逐相位CBF组合的闭式Bernoulli失效模型在3000个随机实例上得到验证；随着屏障数量增加，单QP基线在约束冲突下性能退化；平滑多屏障交集支持安全组合，而逐相位强制屏障不保证安全。

Conclusion: 该框架将CBF组合实践重述为范畴/单子条件，给出统一的代数安全组合判据，并揭示组合方式与失效风险，适用于混合和非光滑机器人系统。

Abstract: Sequential composition of safety-certified controllers for hybrid and non-smooth robotic systems is well studied piecewise. Yet no existing framework reduces this question to a single algebraic condition. We construct a \emph{trajectory monad} for Filippov-regular differential inclusions. Its Kleisli category treats controllers as morphisms and sequential execution as trajectory concatenation. Control Barrier Function (CBF) certification, a guarantee of forward invariance, corresponds to a sub-monad of this category. Joint safety of composed controllers then follows from one algebraic condition. All controllers must land in a common sub-monad, which is closed under the monad multiplication. The sub-monad condition recasts recasts existing CBF composition practice categorically. It explains why a smooth intersection of multiple barriers supports safe composition, while enforcing barriers phase by phase does not. For the phase-by-phase case, we derive a closed-form Bernoulli model of the failure rate. We validate it over a randomized sweep of 3{,}000 instances. The same sweep shows that the standard baseline degrades under constraint conflict as the barrier count grows. This baseline enforces all barriers simultaneously as constraints in a single quadratic program (QP). We instantiate the framework on a four-mode piecewise-affine system and on a unicycle reduced-order model over heterogeneous terrain.

</details>


### [129] [Distributed Shaping of Robot Swarms Through Spatial Moments of Arbitrary Order](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19272)
*Jin Chen,Hector Garcia de Marina*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 该论文通过控制群体空间矩，将无序集群塑造成由图像采样的目标构型，并保证高阶形状修正不破坏低阶矩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统几何编队控制依赖指定智能体间的距离、方位或相对位置，需给个体分配目标位置；本文希望以整体统计量描述并控制群体形状，不指定任一智能体的具体位置。

Method: 将目标图像采样为参考构型，用质心和协方差设定位置与主展布，在协方差白化坐标中用高阶矩编码剩余形状。核心是在群体经验内积下做正交化：把负梯度速度投影到低次多项式采样空间的补空间，使高阶修正精确保持所有低阶矩。由此得到具有下三角误差动力学的分层控制器，并给出联合正则化Gauss–Newton替代方案，同时保持质心和协方差动力学；还给出智能体数量界、正则性条件及仅依赖相对位置和邻居通信的分布式实现。

Result: 分层控制器对任意正增益局部指数收敛，且可逐阶增加而不破坏已实现矩；联合方法也能逼近完整规格。稀疏图分布式仿真中，350个智能体从无序集群通过至十阶矩形成飞机图案：分层法达到完整规格，联合法以更小速度逼近。

Conclusion: 空间矩控制为无个体指派的全群形状生成提供了可扩展框架，其正交化分层机制保证矩约束的逐级精确保持，并可通过分布式实现获得局部指数收敛。

Abstract: This paper shapes a swarm into a target pattern, such as a figure taken from an image, by controlling its spatial moments. The image is sampled into a reference configuration whose centroid and covariance set the position and principal spreads of the formation, while its higher-order moments, expressed in covariance-normalized (whitened) coordinates, encode the remaining shape. In contrast with geometric formation control, which prescribes distances, bearings, or relative positions between specific agents, this specification concerns the swarm as a whole and assigns no agent to a particular place. The key mechanism is orthogonalization in the swarm's own empirical inner product: projecting negative-gradient velocities onto the complement of low-degree polynomials sampled at the agents makes every higher-order correction preserve all lower-order moments exactly. The resulting hierarchical controller has lower triangular error dynamics and converges locally exponentially for any positive gains, so orders can be added without undoing those already achieved. A joint alternative regulates the higher moments together through a regularized Gauss--Newton step while preserving the centroid and covariance dynamics. We give bounds on the number of agents and sufficient conditions for regularity. Both controllers admit a distributed implementation that uses only relative positions and neighbor communication and retains local exponential convergence when estimation is sufficiently fast. In a distributed simulation on a sparse graph, 350 agents form an airplane from an unstructured swarm through moments up to order ten; the hierarchy reaches the full specification, while the joint law approaches it with smaller velocities.

</details>


### [130] [Reliable Conditioned Hamilton-Jacobi Reachability via Subsolution Learning on Exact Local Windows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19088)
*Seunghwan Jang,Wei Xiao*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 提出一种条件化Hamilton-Jacobi可达性模型：通过限制障碍物裕度使值函数具有可证明局部性，并将值界嵌入输出结构，结合反例挖掘训练，使近似误差落在保守侧，从而在跨场景快速迁移的同时实现低碰撞安全滤波。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HJ可达性需在障碍物变化时重新求解，部署机器人难以承受；条件化学习单个值函数虽免去重解，但一个网络需覆盖整族安全问题，近似误差无符号偏好，罕见障碍配置会产生假安全状态。

Method: 限制障碍物裕度使值函数可证明局部，模型仅读取体坐标系窗口，在小场景训练并可迁移到任意大小场景，每场景准备仅需毫秒级；输出结构内置值界，并在有限维窗口配置空间上做反例挖掘，在HJ不等式约束下最大化声称安全体积，使学习安全集从保守侧逼近真实安全集。

Result: 在所有测试障碍设置中，该方法是唯一同时达到与逐场景DeepReach训练相当恢复效果和近零 rollout 碰撞的方法；扩展至10D四旋翼后，碰撞率仍显著低于基线。

Conclusion: 该方法为条件化可达性安全滤波提供了可扩展且保守的解决方案，支持跨场景快速迁移并显著降低碰撞风险。

Abstract: Hamilton-Jacobi (HJ) reachability computes the maximal safe set and a safety filter for a given obstacle configuration, but it must be re-solved whenever the obstacles change, which a deployed robot cannot afford. Learning a single value function conditioned on the obstacle set removes the re-solve, yet it sharpens the central weakness of learned reachability: one network must now cover a whole family of safety problems, and its approximation error carries no preferred sign, so rare obstacle configurations silently produce false-safe states. To address these challenges, we present a conditioned reachability model designed so that the remaining error falls on the conservative side. Specifically, our method caps the obstacle margin, which makes the value function provably local, in the sense that obstacles farther than a known threshold cannot affect it, so the model reads only a body-frame window, trains on small scenes, and transfers to scenes of any size with millisecond per-scene preparation. Moreover, the output structure builds the value bound into the model. By training with counterexample mining over the finite-dimensional window configuration space, we maximize the claimed safe volume subject to the HJ inequality, so the learned set grows toward the true safe set from the conservative side. Across all tested obstacle settings, ours is the only method that combines recovery comparable to per-scene DeepReach training with near-zero rollout collisions. We also extend the method to a 10D quadrotor, again with markedly lower collision rates than the baselines.

</details>


<div id='cs.SD'></div>

# cs.SD [[Back]](#toc)

### [131] [Dependable Voice Command for Multi-UAV Swarms Using Small Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18924)
*Afshin Ameri,Luiz Giacomossi,Håkan Forsberg,Ivan Tomasic,Baran Cürüklü*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 提出一种基于小语言模型（SLM）的多无人机集群可靠语音指令框架，通过ASR与SLM语义处理将操作员语音转化为结构化任务定义，并用PCFG合成不同口音与信噪比语音进行端到端鲁棒性评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多无人机监督控制对人类操作员造成较高视觉与认知负荷；语音交互虽自然且可降低负荷，但受环境噪声、语音变异和识别错误影响，可靠性不足。

Method: 构建ASR模块与基于SLM的语义处理层，将口语指令转换为下游多无人机规划与执行所需的结构化任务定义；开发端到端评估流水线，利用概率上下文无关文法（PCFG）生成合成监督指令，转换为不同操作员口音和环境信噪比（SNR）的音频，经ASR处理后由SLM进行上下文解释并在需要时进行音素/转写纠错。

Result: 评估隔离了语音指令流水线在声学退化和语音变异下的韧性，表明在挑战性操作条件下能够可靠生成多无人机监督任务。

Conclusion: 该框架为多无人机集群监督提供了一种可依赖的语音指令交互方案，可提升复杂条件下语音任务生成的可靠性。

Abstract: Supervisory control of multiple autonomous unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs) imposes high visual and cognitive demands on human operators, particularly when communicating mission objectives and system constraints. Voice command provides a natural interaction modality that can reduce operator workload; however, its reliability is affected by environmental noise, speech variability, and recognition errors. This paper presents a dependable voice command framework for multi-UAV swarm supervision using Small Language Models (SLMs). The proposed architecture integrates an Automatic Speech Recognition (ASR) module with an SLM-based semantic processing layer to transform spoken operator commands into structured task definitions for downstream multi-UAV planning and execution. To evaluate robustness, an end-to-end assessment pipeline is developed using a Probabilistic Context-Free Grammar (PCFG) to generate synthetic supervisory commands, which are converted into audio samples with varying operator accents and environmental signal-to-noise ratios (SNR). The resulting speech signals are processed by the ASR module, and the SLM performs contextual interpretation and phonetic correction of transcriptions when required. The evaluation isolates the resilience of the voice command pipeline under acoustic degradation and speech variation, demonstrating reliable task generation for multi-UAV supervision in challenging operational conditions.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [132] [D'BEAST: Logged Bad Experiences as Explanation for Autonomous Driving Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20019)
*Ahmad Alfan Alfian Irfan,Nur Ahmad Khatim,Mansur Arief*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究表格型强化学习智能体的决策能否由其“坏经验”（与安全或任务事件相关的训练转移）解释，并通过重放训练日志、消融坏经验及在CARLA闭环驾驶中验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习智能体虽从错误中学习，但其决策难以追溯到具体经验；作者希望检验坏经验是否携带对决策关键的信息。

Method: 从相同初始Q表重放固定训练日志，设置三种条件：FULL保留所有更新，SKIP省略坏经验更新，NEUTRALIZE保留坏经验更新但去除事件惩罚；在六个经典MDP和CARLA闭环驾驶中评估。

Result: 在完整驾驶任务中，FULL从40个测试起点中的33个到达目标，而SKIP和NEUTRALIZE均未到达；在SKIP或NEUTRALIZE失败前的47个局部决策中，移除或中和一小组坏经验（中位数为1）使智能体在46个案例中选择失败策略的动作。

Conclusion: 对该表格型智能体而言，其决策可以由坏经验解释，表明坏经验对决策具有可追溯且关键的影响。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning agents learn from mistakes, yet their decisions can be difficult to trace back to those experiences. We investigate whether a tabular agent's decisions can be explained by its bad experiences, defined as the training transitions associated with safety or task events. A fixed training log is replayed from the same initial Q-table under three conditions. FULL retains all updates, SKIP omits bad-experience updates, and NEUTRALIZE retains those updates without their event penalties. We illustrate the method on six classic Markov decision processes and evaluate it in closed-loop driving in CARLA. On the full driving task, FULL reached the goal from 33 of 40 test starts, whereas SKIP and NEUTRALIZE reached it from none, indicating that the bad experiences carry decision-relevant information. At the local level, for 47 decisions shortly before a failure of SKIP or NEUTRALIZE, removing or neutralizing a small group of bad experiences, with a median size of one, caused the trained agent to select the failing policy's action in 46 cases. For this agent, the results indicate that its decisions can be explained by its bad experiences.

</details>


### [133] [Spectral Steering: Local Inverse Geometry for Frozen Diffusion Generators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19572)
*Angelo Moroncelli,Alessandro Leanza,Loris Roveda*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文研究冻结扩散生成器在执行“改变部分属性、保持其余属性”的结构化引导请求时何时能忠实实现，并证明局部逆修正可改善引导方向；提出谱引导（Spectral steering）并在多模态任务中验证其机制与下游收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散引导常假设条件化足以实现结构化编辑，但请求可能同时要求改变与保持多个输出属性；作者关注引导何时忠实实现此类请求，以及局部逆修正能否改善方向，并指出仅靠条件化不足，关键在于请求与目标特定剩余采样器雅可比的交互。

Method: 提出 Spectral steering：结合请求特定的余弦增益预测器、阻尼逆修正和固定时间重线性化以支持有限编辑；用标量零假设和匹配运动对照检验预测机制，并在图像、音频、机器人、控制与规划（含复杂角色运动生成）中实验。

Result: 在回顾性识别的局部区域内 8,923 个实例上，预测增益与实现增益的 Spearman 相关为 0.79，而条件数为 -0.11；50 个成对修复案例中，四次修正以相同总导数遍历数将未观测像素 PSNR 较 Adam 提升 3.25 dB。

Conclusion: 分析表明请求相关几何决定方向性增益，对照实验支持该机制；这些增益可在下游任务中转化为性能提升，并界定了其有效范围。

Abstract: A frozen diffusion generator may need to change several output properties while preserving others. We study when guidance faithfully realizes such a structured request and whether local inverse correction improves its direction. Our account links this benefit to the interaction between the request and the objective-specific remaining-sampler Jacobian: conditioning alone is insufficient. Spectral steering combines a request-specific cosine-gain predictor with damped inverse correction and fixed-time relinearization for finite edits. Scalar nulls and matched-movement controls test the predicted mechanism. Across 8,923 instances in a retrospectively identified local regime, predicted and realized gains correlate at Spearman 0.79, versus -0.11 for condition number. Experiments span images, audio, robotics, and control and planning, including complex character motion generation. On 50 paired inpainting cases, four corrections improve unobserved-pixel PSNR over Adam by 3.25 dB at equal total derivative traversals. Together, the analysis and controls connect request-dependent geometry to directional gains and establish where these gains translate into downstream performance.

</details>


### [134] [Successive Convexification in Learned World Models for Safe Offline Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19466)
*Samuel Younger*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文将基于离线数据学习的世界模型中的安全规划表述为潜空间连续凸规划（SCP），在规划时域每一步施加学习到的离散时间控制屏障函数约束，并通过平滑网络与采样计划初始化提升可行性与可验证性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 采样型规划器（如MPPI、CEM）通常仅通过评分或拒绝采样动作序列来处理安全约束，缺乏对计划可行性、最优性及安全约束满足的可验证保证；同时，世界模型离线规划需要一种能显式处理安全约束且尽量少交互的规划方法。

Method: 在学得世界模型的潜空间中求解安全规划问题，将其建模为SCP，并在每个时域步引入学习到的离散时间控制屏障函数作为约束；为满足SCP对平滑动力学的要求，网络采用平滑MaxMin激活和谱归一化层，并以Lipschitz界与平滑宽度给出线性化误差界；由于SCP是局部方法，使用采样计划作为初始化。

Result: 在两个Safety-Gymnasium离线数据导航任务中，该方法在主比较中于相同墙钟时间下比使用128倍样本的MPPI和CEM违规更少（但不显著），在更长时域上获得更高回报，并显著优于无采样初始化的SCP；在Lipschitz受限模型上SCP收敛更频繁；与采样方法不同，它能在每一步报告计划是否为模型规划问题的可行一阶最优KKT点，或附近未找到可行计划。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，将学习世界模型中的安全规划转化为带控制屏障函数约束的SCP，可在离线数据条件下提供比采样更可验证的安全规划，并兼顾回报与收敛性；其代价是依赖平滑动力学、局部优化及采样初始化，作者已公开实现。

Abstract: World models learned from offline data allow an agent to plan without further interaction with the environment, but the sampling-based planners used with them handle safety constraints only by scoring or rejecting sampled action sequences. We instead pose safe planning in the latent space of a learned world model as a successive convex program (SCP), in which a learned discrete-time control barrier function is imposed as a constraint at every step of the horizon. SCP requires smooth dynamics, so we build the networks from smoothed MaxMin activations and spectrally normalised layers, and bound the resulting linearisation error in terms of the Lipschitz bound and the smoothing width. As SCP is a local method, we start it from a sampled plan. On two Safety-Gymnasium navigation tasks with offline data, this planner has fewer violations than MPPI and CEM drawing 128 times as many samples at equal wall-clock time in our main comparison, though not significantly, and earns greater return at longer horizons, and is much safer than SCP without a sampled start. SCP converged more often on Lipschitz-bounded models. Unlike sampling, it also reports at each step whether its plan is a feasible, first-order optimal KKT point of the planning problem in the model, or that no feasible plan was found nearby. Our implementation is publicly available at this https URL .

</details>
